1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain, and evict them
     9→  again when objective backlog returns.
    10→
    11→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, QueueSyncResult
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    22→
    23→from .context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# ID helpers
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    32→
    33→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    34→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    35→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    36→    """
    37→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    38→        state,
    39→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→
    43→def current_under_target_ids(
    44→    state: StateModel,
    45→    *,
    46→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    47→) -> set[str]:
    48→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    49→
    50→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    51→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    52→    """
    53→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    54→        state,
    55→        target_strict=target_strict,
    56→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→# Helpers
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    65→    order: list[str],
    66→    *,
    67→    keep_ids: set[str],
    68→    pruned: list[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    71→    to_remove = [
    72→        fid for fid in order
    73→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    74→        and fid not in keep_ids
    75→    ]
    76→    for fid in to_remove:
    77→        order.remove(fid)
    78→        pruned.append(fid)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    82→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
    83→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    85→        return set()
    86→    return {
    87→        str(fid)
    88→        for fid in skipped
    89→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    90→    }
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    *,
    96→    skipped_ids: set[str],
    97→    pruned: list[str],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return
   102→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   103→    for fid in to_remove:
   104→        order.remove(fid)
   105→        pruned.append(fid)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   109→    order: list[str],
   110→    *,
   111→    inject_ids: set[str],
   112→    injected: list[str],
   113→) -> None:
   114→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   115→
   116→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   117→    """
   118→    existing = set(order)
   119→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   120→        if sid not in existing:
   121→            order.append(sid)
   122→            injected.append(sid)
   123→
   124→
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→
   129→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    state: StateModel,
   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   134→
   135→    1. **Prune** — always remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order``
   136→       that are no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs —
   137→       that is ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).  Pruning runs even
   138→       mid-cycle to clean up items that were incorrectly injected by older
   139→       code that lacked the mid-cycle guard.
   140→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   141→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —
   142→       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.
   143→    """
   144→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   145→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   146→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   147→
   148→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   149→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   150→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   151→    )
   152→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   153→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   154→
   155→    if not mid_cycle:
   156→        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a
   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   161→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   162→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   163→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   164→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   165→
   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   167→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   168→
   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   170→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   171→    # Mid-cycle: prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty) since they
   172→    # shouldn't be in the queue mid-cycle at all.
   173→    if mid_cycle:
   174→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=set(), pruned=result.pruned)
   175→    else:
   176→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   179→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.
   180→    if not mid_cycle:
   181→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   182→            order,
   183→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   184→            injected=result.injected,
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   195→    plan: PlanModel,
   196→    state: StateModel,
   197→    *,
   198→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   199→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   200→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   204→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   205→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   206→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   207→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   208→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   209→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   210→       b. A cycle just completed.
   211→       Never reorders existing items.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   215→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   216→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   217→    )
   218→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   219→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   220→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   221→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   222→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   223→
   224→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   225→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   226→
   227→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   228→
   229→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   230→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   231→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   232→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   233→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   234→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   235→    if mid_cycle:
   236→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   237→    else:
   238→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   239→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   240→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   241→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   242→
   243→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   244→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   245→
   246→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   247→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "current_under_target_ids",
   254→    "current_unscored_ids",
   255→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→

     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod

    41→def _subjective_state_sets(
    42→    state: StateModel,
    43→    *,
    44→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    45→    target_strict: float,
    46→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:
    47→    """Return (stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids) for subjective cluster logic."""

    49→        unscored_ids = policy.unscored_ids
    50→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids
    51→        under_target_ids = policy.under_target_ids

    58→    return stale_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_ids

    60→
    61→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""
    63→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    64→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    65→        return set()
    66→    return {
    67→        str(fid)
    68→        for fid in skipped
    69→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    70→    }
    71→
    72→
    73→def _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan: dict) -> None:
    74→    plan.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes
   114→
   115→
   116→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    issues: dict,
   120→    clusters: dict,
   121→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   122→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   123→    now: str,
   124→    *,
   125→    target_strict: float,
   126→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   127→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   128→) -> int:
   129→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   130→    changes = 0
   131→    skipped_subjective_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   132→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   133→    if skipped_subjective_ids and isinstance(order, list):
   134→        overlap = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_subjective_ids]
   135→        for fid in overlap:
   136→            order.remove(fid)
   137→            changes += 1
   138→
   139→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   140→        fid
   141→        for fid in order
   142→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   143→    )
   144→
   145→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   146→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   150→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   151→    )
   152→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   153→        fid
   154→        for fid in all_subjective_ids
   155→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   159→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   160→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   161→        description = (
   162→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   163→        )
   164→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   165→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   166→            plan,
   167→            clusters,
   168→            existing_by_key,
   169→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   170→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   171→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   172→            description=description,
   173→            action=action,
   174→            now=now,
   175→        )
   176→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   177→
   178→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   179→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   180→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   181→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   182→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   183→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   184→            plan,
   185→            clusters,

   187→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   188→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   189→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   190→            description=description,
   191→            action=action,
   192→            now=now,
   193→        )
   194→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   195→
   196→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(
   197→        fid for fid in under_target_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   198→    )
   199→    stale_candidate_ids = sorted(
   200→        fid
   201→        for fid in stale_state_ids
   202→        if fid not in unscored_state_ids and fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   203→    )
   204→    deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(
   205→        set(stale_candidate_ids) | set(under_target_queue_ids)
   206→    )
   207→
   208→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   209→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   210→    ut_prune = [

   212→        for fid in prev_ut_ids
   213→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   214→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   215→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   216→        and fid in order
   217→    ]
   218→    for fid in ut_prune:
   219→        order.remove(fid)
   220→        changes += 1
   221→
   222→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   223→
   224→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   225→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   226→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   227→        description = (
   228→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   229→            f"dimensions under target score"
   230→        )
   231→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   232→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   233→            plan,
   234→            clusters,
   235→            existing_by_key,
   236→            [REDACTED],
   237→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   238→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   239→            description=description,
   240→            action=action,
   241→            now=now,
   242→            optional=True,
   243→        )
   244→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   245→
   246→        existing_order = set(order)
   247→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   248→            if fid not in existing_order:
   249→                order.append(fid)
   250→
   251→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   252→        if deferred_subjective_ids:
   253→            defer_state = update_defer_state(
   254→                plan.get(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   255→                state=state,
   256→                deferred_ids=set(deferred_subjective_ids),
   257→                options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   258→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   259→                    now=now,
   260→                ),
   261→            )
   262→            escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   263→                defer_state,
   264→                state=state,
   265→                options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   266→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   267→                    now=now,
   268→                ),
   269→            )
   270→            if escalated:
   271→                defer_state[_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = list(deferred_subjective_ids)
   272→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   273→                changes += _promote_subjective_ids(order, deferred_subjective_ids)
   274→            else:
   275→                defer_state.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   276→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   277→                objective_evict = [
   278→                    fid
   279→                    for fid in order
   280→                    if fid in under_target_ids or fid in stale_state_ids
   281→                ]
   282→                for fid in objective_evict:
   283→                    order.remove(fid)
   284→                    changes += 1
   285→        else:
   286→            _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   287→    else:
   288→        _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   289→
   290→    return changes
   291→
   292→
   293→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]

1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def current_stale_ids(
    25→    state: StateModel,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    28→) -> set[str]:
    29→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    30→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    31→    if not dim_scores:
    32→        return set()
    33→
    34→    stale: set[str] = set()
    35→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    36→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    37→            continue
    38→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    39→        if dim_key:
    40→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    41→    return stale
    42→
    43→
    44→def current_unscored_ids(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    *,
    47→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    48→) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    50→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    51→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    52→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    53→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    54→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    55→                continue
    56→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    57→                continue
    58→            if dim_key:
    59→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    60→        return unscored
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    unscored = set()
    67→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    68→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    69→            continue
    70→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    71→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    72→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    73→            continue
    74→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    75→            continue
    76→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    77→        if dim_key:
    78→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    79→    return unscored
    80→
    81→
    82→def current_under_target_ids(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    86→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    87→) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    89→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    90→    if not dim_scores:
    91→        return set()
    92→
    93→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    94→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    95→
    96→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    97→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    98→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    99→            continue
   100→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   101→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   102→            continue
   103→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   104→        if not dim_key:
   105→            continue
   106→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   107→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
   108→            under_target.add(item_id)
   109→    return under_target
   110→
   111→
   112→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   113→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   114→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   115→    if not review_ids:
   116→        return ""
   117→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   118→
   119→
   120→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   124→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   125→
   126→
   127→def is_triage_stale(
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   132→
   133→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   134→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   135→    forces triage stages to the front.
   136→    """
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   139→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   144→    "current_stale_ids",
   145→    "current_under_target_ids",
   146→    "current_unscored_ids",
   147→    "is_triage_stale",
   148→    "open_review_ids",
   149→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   150→]
   151→

1→"""Shared defer-tracking helpers for triage and subjective rerun fairness."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
     9→
    10→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3
    11→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class DeferUpdateOptions:
    16→    """Options for durable defer-state updates."""
    17→
    18→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    19→    now: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DeferEscalationOptions:
    24→    """Options for defer-state escalation checks."""
    25→
    26→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    27→    now: str | None = None
    28→    min_scans: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS
    29→    min_days: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS
    30→
    31→
    32→def _coerce_non_negative_int(value: object, *, default: int = 0) -> int:
    33→    try:
    34→        parsed = int(value)
    35→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    36→        return default
    37→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else default
    38→
    39→
    40→def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:
    41→    return _coerce_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count"), default=0)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _normalize_timestamp(raw: object) -> str | None:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
    46→        return None
    47→    value = raw.strip()
    48→    return value or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _parse_timestamp(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    52→    normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    53→    if not normalized:
    54→        return None
    55→    try:
    56→        return datetime.fromisoformat(normalized.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    57→    except ValueError:
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→def _current_timestamp(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    now: str | None = None,
    65→) -> str:
    66→    for raw in (state.get("last_scan"), now):
    67→        normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    68→        if normalized:
    69→            return normalized
    70→    return utc_now()
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalized_ids(ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    74→    return sorted({str(issue_id).strip() for issue_id in ids if str(issue_id).strip()})
    75→
    76→
    77→def _deferred_ids(
    78→    defer_state: dict[str, object],
    79→    *,
    80→    deferred_ids_field: str,
    81→) -> list[str]:
    82→    raw = defer_state.get(deferred_ids_field)
    83→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted(
    86→        {
    87→            str(issue_id).strip()
    88→            for issue_id in raw
    89→            if str(issue_id).strip()
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def update_defer_state(
    95→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    deferred_ids: set[str],
    99→    options: DeferUpdateOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> dict[str, object]:
   101→    """Return updated durable defer metadata for the current deferred ID set."""
   102→    resolved_options = options or DeferUpdateOptions()
   103→    previous = defer_state if isinstance(defer_state, dict) else {}
   104→    current_ids = _normalized_ids(deferred_ids)
   105→    previous_ids = _deferred_ids(
   106→        previous,
   107→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   108→    )
   109→    scan_count = _scan_count(state)
   110→    timestamp = _current_timestamp(state, now=resolved_options.now)
   111→
   112→    updated = dict(previous)
   113→    updated[resolved_options.deferred_ids_field] = current_ids
   114→    updated["last_deferred_scan"] = scan_count
   115→    updated["last_deferred_at"] = timestamp
   116→
   117→    same_ids = bool(current_ids) and current_ids == previous_ids
   118→    if same_ids:
   119→        defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("defer_count"), default=1)
   120→        prior_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("last_deferred_scan"), default=-1)
   121→        if prior_scan != scan_count:
   122→            defer_count += 1
   123→        branch_updates = {
   124→            "defer_count": max(1, defer_count),
   125→            "first_deferred_scan": _coerce_non_negative_int(
   126→            previous.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   127→            default=scan_count,
   128→            ),
   129→            "first_deferred_at": str(previous.get("first_deferred_at") or timestamp),
   130→        }
   131→    else:
   132→        branch_updates = {
   133→            "defer_count": 1 if current_ids else 0,
   134→            "first_deferred_scan": scan_count,
   135→            "first_deferred_at": timestamp,
   136→        }
   137→    updated.update(branch_updates)
   138→    return updated
   139→
   140→
   141→def should_escalate_defer_state(
   142→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
   143→    *,
   144→    state: StateModel,
   145→    options: DeferEscalationOptions | None = None,
   146→) -> bool:
   147→    """Return True when defer metadata crosses configured escalation bounds."""
   148→    if not isinstance(defer_state, dict):
   149→        return False
   150→    resolved_options = options or DeferEscalationOptions()
   151→
   152→    deferred_ids = _deferred_ids(
   153→        defer_state,
   154→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   155→    )
   156→    if not deferred_ids:
   157→        return False
   158→
   159→    min_scans = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_scans))
   160→    min_days = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_days))
   161→
   162→    defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(defer_state.get("defer_count"), default=0)
   163→    if defer_count >= min_scans:
   164→        return True
   165→
   166→    first_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(
   167→        defer_state.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   168→        default=-1,
   169→    )
   170→    current_scan = _scan_count(state)
   171→    if first_scan >= 0 and current_scan - first_scan + 1 >= min_scans:
   172→        return True
   173→
   174→    first_deferred_at = _parse_timestamp(defer_state.get("first_deferred_at"))
   175→    current_at = _parse_timestamp(state.get("last_scan")) or _parse_timestamp(
   176→        resolved_options.now
   177→    )
   178→    if first_deferred_at is None or current_at is None:
   179→        return False
   180→    return (current_at - first_deferred_at).total_seconds() >= min_days * 86400
   181→
   182→
   183→__all__ = [
   184→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS",
   185→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS",
   186→    "DeferEscalationOptions",
   187→    "DeferUpdateOptions",
   188→    "should_escalate_defer_state",
   189→    "update_defer_state",
   190→]
   191→
```

1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues).
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available.
    32→    Falls back to scanning *state_or_issues* directly — accepts either a
    33→    full ``StateModel`` (looks up ``issues``) or a raw issues dict.
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→
```

     7→

     9→

    43→    WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,

    46→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")

    49→@dataclass
    50→class QueueSyncResult:
    51→    """Unified result for all queue sync operations."""
    52→
    53→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    54→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    55→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    56→    deferred: bool = False
    57→
    58→    @property
    59→    def changes(self) -> int:
    60→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned) + len(self.resurfaced)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    64→    """Extract the triage stages dict from meta or a raw stages dict."""
    65→    if not isinstance(meta_or_stages, dict):
    66→        return {}
    67→    if "triage_stages" in meta_or_stages:
    68→        raw = meta_or_stages.get("triage_stages")
    69→        return raw if isinstance(raw, dict) else {}
    70→    candidate_names = {str(name) for name in meta_or_stages.keys()}
    71→    if candidate_names and candidate_names.issubset(_TRIAGE_STAGE_NAMES):
    72→        return meta_or_stages
    73→    return {}
    74→
    75→
    76→def confirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return triage stage names with an explicit ``confirmed_at`` marker."""
    78→    return {
    79→        str(name)
    80→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    81→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload.get("confirmed_at")
    82→    }
    83→
    84→
    85→def recorded_unconfirmed_triage_stage_names(meta_or_stages: dict[str, Any] | None) -> set[str]:
    86→    """Return recorded triage stage names that still need confirmation."""
    87→    return {
    88→        str(name)
    89→        for name, payload in _resolve_triage_stages(meta_or_stages).items()
    90→        if isinstance(payload, dict) and payload and not payload.get("confirmed_at")
    91→    }
    92→
    93→
    94→def normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix(queue_order: list[str]) -> None:
    95→    """Keep workflow items ahead of triage, then preserve the rest as-is."""
    96→    seen: set[str] = set()
    97→    normalized: list[str] = []
    98→
    99→    for issue_id in WORKFLOW_PRIORITY_ORDER:
   100→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   101→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   102→            seen.add(issue_id)
   103→
   104→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   105→        if issue_id.startswith(WORKFLOW_PREFIX) and issue_id not in seen:
   106→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   107→            seen.add(issue_id)
   108→
   109→    for issue_id in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
   110→        if issue_id in queue_order and issue_id not in seen:
   111→            normalized.append(issue_id)
   112→            seen.add(issue_id)
   113→
   114→    for issue_id in queue_order:
   115→        if issue_id in seen:
   116→            continue
   117→        normalized.append(issue_id)
   118→        seen.add(issue_id)
   119→
   120→    queue_order[:] = normalized
   121→
   122→
   123→__all__ = [

   126→    "normalize_queue_workflow_and_triage_prefix",

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups issues into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import AUTO_PREFIX
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster_sync import (
     8→    prune_stale_clusters as _prune_stale_clusters,
     9→    sync_issue_clusters as _sync_issue_clusters,
    10→    sync_subjective_clusters as _sync_subjective_clusters,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Repair
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
    21→    """Cross-check cluster membership between cluster.issue_ids and overrides.
    22→
    23→    Repairs three kinds of drift:
    24→    1. Override points to a cluster that does not exist → clear override.
    25→    2. Override points to cluster X, but issue is not in X's issue_ids → add it.
    26→    3. Issue is in cluster.issue_ids but override points elsewhere (or has no
    27→       cluster ref) → update override to match cluster.issue_ids.
    28→
    29→    cluster.issue_ids is treated as authoritative when it disagrees with
    30→    the override, because the sync helpers always write issue_ids first.
    31→    """
    32→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    33→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    34→    repaired = 0
    35→
    36→    # Direction 1: override → cluster that doesn't exist
    37→    for override in overrides.values():
    38→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
    39→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
    40→            override["cluster"] = None
    41→            override["updated_at"] = now
    42→            repaired += 1
    43→
    44→    # Build authoritative map: issue_id → cluster_name from cluster.issue_ids.
    45→    # When an issue appears in multiple clusters, prefer manual (non-auto)
    46→    # clusters, matching the priority in collapse_clusters.
    47→    canonical: dict[str, str] = {}
    48→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    49→        for issue_id in cluster.get("issue_ids", []):
    50→            if issue_id not in canonical or not cluster.get("auto"):
    51→                canonical[issue_id] = name
    52→
    53→    # Direction 2+3: ensure overrides agree with cluster.issue_ids
    54→    for issue_id, cluster_name in canonical.items():
    55→        override = overrides.get(issue_id)
    56→        if override is None:
    57→            overrides[issue_id] = {
    58→                "issue_id": issue_id,
    59→                "cluster": cluster_name,
    60→                "created_at": now,
    61→                "updated_at": now,
    62→            }
    63→            repaired += 1
    64→        elif override.get("cluster") != cluster_name:
    65→            override["cluster"] = cluster_name
    66→            override["updated_at"] = now
    67→            repaired += 1
    68→
    69→    # Direction 2 (reverse): override says cluster X, but issue not in X.issue_ids.
    70→    # If the issue isn't in *any* cluster's issue_ids, clear the override ref.
    71→    for issue_id, override in overrides.items():
    72→        ref = override.get("cluster")
    73→        if not ref or ref not in clusters:
    74→            continue
    75→        if issue_id in canonical:
    76→            continue  # already handled above
    77→        # Override points to a real cluster, but issue is not in any
    78→        # cluster's issue_ids → stale ref, clear it.
    79→        override["cluster"] = None
    80→        override["updated_at"] = now
    81→        repaired += 1
    82→
    83→    return repaired
    84→
    85→
    86→def auto_cluster_issues(
    87→    plan: PlanModel,
    88→    state: StateModel,
    89→    *,
    90→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    91→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
    92→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
    93→) -> int:
    94→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open issues.
    95→
    96→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
    97→    """
    98→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    99→
   100→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   101→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   102→
   103→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   104→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   105→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   106→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   107→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   108→            if ck:
   109→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   110→
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   113→    changes = 0
   114→
   115→    changes += _sync_issue_clusters(
   116→        plan, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   117→    )
   118→    changes += _sync_subjective_clusters(
   119→        plan, state, issues, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   120→        target_strict=target_strict,
   121→        policy=policy,
   122→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   123→    )
   124→    changes += _prune_stale_clusters(
   125→        plan, issues, clusters, active_auto_keys, now,
   126→    )
   127→    changes += _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   128→
   129→    plan["updated"] = now
   130→    return changes
   131→
   132→
   133→__all__ = [
   134→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   135→    "auto_cluster_issues",
   136→]
   137→
```

1→"""Lifecycle and reset mutations for plan operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    empty_plan,
    10→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    13→
    14→
    15→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
    16→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
    17→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    18→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
    19→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
    20→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
    21→
    22→
    23→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    24→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
    25→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    26→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
    27→
    28→
    29→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
    30→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp.
    31→
    32→    Sets ``plan_start_scores`` to a sentinel so the next scan seeds real
    33→    scores instead of incorrectly treating the reset as a completed cycle.
    34→    """
    35→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
    36→    plan.clear()
    37→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
    38→        plan[k] = v
    39→    plan["created"] = created
    40→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {"reset": True}
    41→
    42→
    43→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, issue_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    44→    """Remove issue IDs from the plan entirely.
    45→
    46→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
    47→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
    48→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
    49→    """
    50→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    51→    found = 0
    52→
    53→    purge_set = set(issue_ids)
    54→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, purge_set)
    55→
    56→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    57→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    58→    for fid in issue_ids:
    59→        was_present = False
    60→        if fid in order:
    61→            order.remove(fid)
    62→            was_present = True
    63→        if fid in skipped:
    64→            skipped.pop(fid)
    65→            was_present = True
    66→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    67→            ids = cluster.get("issue_ids", [])
    68→            if fid in ids:
    69→                ids.remove(fid)
    70→                was_present = True
    71→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
    72→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
    73→            override["cluster"] = None
    74→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
    75→        if was_present:
    76→            found += 1
    77→
    78→    return found
    79→
    80→
    81→__all__ = ["clear_focus", "purge_ids", "reset_plan", "set_focus"]
    82→
```

1→"""Tests for stale-dimension queue ordering and cycle-completion injections."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.dimensions import (
     6→    sync_stale_dimensions,
     7→    sync_unscored_dimensions,
     8→)
     9→from desloppify.tests.plan.test_stale_dimensions import (
    10→    _plan_with_queue,
    11→    _state_with_stale_dimensions,
    12→    _state_with_unscored_dimensions,
    13→)
    14→
    15→
    16→def test_unscored_appends_after_existing():
    17→    """Unscored dims append to back, never reorder existing items."""
    18→    plan = _plan_with_queue("issue_a", "issue_b")
    19→    plan["promoted_ids"] = ["issue_a"]
    20→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence")
    21→
    22→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
    23→    assert len(result.injected) == 1
    24→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "issue_a"
    25→    assert plan["queue_order"][1] == "issue_b"
    26→    assert plan["queue_order"][2] == "subjective::design_coherence"
    27→
    28→
    29→def test_unscored_multiple_append_to_back():
    30→    """Multiple unscored dims all append to back."""
    31→    plan = _plan_with_queue("issue_a", "issue_b", "issue_c")
    32→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
    33→
    34→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
    35→    assert len(result.injected) == 2
    36→    # Existing items keep their positions
    37→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "issue_a"
    38→    assert plan["queue_order"][1] == "issue_b"
    39→    assert plan["queue_order"][2] == "issue_c"
    40→    # Unscored dims appended at back
    41→    assert all(
    42→        fid.startswith("subjective::")
    43→        for fid in plan["queue_order"][3:5]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→# Post-cycle injection: cycle_just_completed overrides objective gate
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    50→
    51→def test_cycle_completed_injects_stale_despite_objective_backlog():
    52→    """After a completed cycle, stale dims inject even with new objective issues."""
    53→    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_issue::file.py::abc123")
    54→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
    55→    state["issues"]["some_issue::file.py::abc123"] = {
    56→        "id": "some_issue::file.py::abc123",
    57→        "status": "open",
    58→        "detector": "smells",
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    # Without cycle_just_completed: no injection (existing behavior)
    62→    result_normal = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
    63→    assert result_normal.injected == []
    64→
    65→    # With cycle_just_completed: inject at back (never reorder existing queue)
    66→    plan2 = _plan_with_queue("some_issue::file.py::abc123")
    67→    result_cycle = sync_stale_dimensions(plan2, state, cycle_just_completed=True)
    68→    assert len(result_cycle.injected) == 2
    69→    # Existing item keeps position; stale dims appended at back
    70→    assert plan2["queue_order"][0] == "some_issue::file.py::abc123"
    71→    assert plan2["queue_order"][1].startswith("subjective::")
    72→    assert plan2["queue_order"][2].startswith("subjective::")
    73→
    74→
    75→def test_cycle_completed_appends_to_back():
    76→    """Post-cycle stale injection appends to back, preserving existing order."""
    77→    plan = _plan_with_queue("issue_a", "issue_b")
    78→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence")
    79→    state["issues"]["issue_a"] = {

   145→    """After plan reset, stale dims should NOT be front-loaded.

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
     8→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     9→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
    10→)
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→    "delegation density": "abstraction_fitness",
    17→    "definition directness": "abstraction_fitness",
    18→    "type discipline": "abstraction_fitness",
    19→}
    20→
    21→
    22→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    23→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    24→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    25→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    26→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    27→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    28→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    29→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    30→            return key
    31→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    32→
    33→
    34→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    35→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    36→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    37→        components = (
    38→            data.get("detectors", {})
    39→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    40→            .get("components", [])
    41→        )
    42→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    43→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    44→        else:
    45→            keys = [
    46→                "high_level_elegance",
    47→                "mid_level_elegance",
    48→                "low_level_elegance",
    49→            ]
    50→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    51→        components = (
    52→            data.get("detectors", {})
    53→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    54→            .get("components", [])
    55→        )
    56→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    57→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    58→        else:
    59→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    60→    else:
    61→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    62→
    63→    seen: set[str] = set()
    64→    ordered: list[str] = []
    65→    for key in keys:
    66→        if not key or key in seen:
    67→            continue
    68→        ordered.append(key)
    69→        seen.add(key)
    70→    return ordered
    71→
    72→
    73→def all_subjective_entries(
    74→    state: dict,
    75→    *,
    76→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    77→) -> list[dict]:
    78→    """Return ALL subjective dimension entries, bypassing the display pipeline.
    79→
    80→    Unlike ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` this reads ``dimension_scores``
    81→    directly — no elegance collapsing, no row-count limit, no placeholder
    82→    hiding.  Use this for *enumeration* (work-queue construction, stale/
    83→    under-target detection) where every individual dimension must be visible.
    84→    """
    85→    if dim_scores is None:
    86→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    87→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
    88→        return []
    89→
    90→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    91→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    92→
    93→    entries: list[dict] = []
    94→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
    95→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    96→            continue
    97→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    98→        is_subjective = (
    99→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
   100→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_display_names
   101→        )
   102→        if not is_subjective:
   103→            continue
   104→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   105→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   106→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
   107→        placeholder = bool(
   108→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
   109→            or (
   110→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
   111→                and data.get("failing", 0) == 0
   112→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   113→            )
   114→        )
   115→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   116→        stale = bool(data.get("stale")) or bool(
   117→            dim_key
   118→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   119→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   120→        )
   121→        entries.append(
   122→            {
   123→                "name": name,
   124→                "score": score,
   125→                "strict": strict,
   126→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   127→                "failing": int(data.get("failing", 0) or 0),
   128→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   129→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   130→                "stale": stale,
   131→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   132→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   133→            }
   134→        )
   135→    return entries
   136→
   137→
   138→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
   139→    state: dict,
   140→    *,
   141→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   142→) -> list[dict]:
   143→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata.
   144→
   145→    WARNING: This function routes through the scorecard *display* pipeline
   146→    (elegance collapsing, 20-row cap, placeholder hiding).  For enumeration
   147→    of all individual dimensions (work-queue, stale/under-target detection),
   148→    use ``all_subjective_entries`` instead.
   149→    """
   150→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)

   152→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   153→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   154→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   155→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   156→        return False
   157→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   158→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   159→        return False
   160→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   161→    if scores.strict is None:
   162→        return False
   163→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   164→        "strict": scores.strict,
   165→        "overall": scores.overall,
   166→        "objective": scores.objective,
   167→        "verified": scores.verified,
   168→    }
   169→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   170→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   171→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   172→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   173→    return True
   174→
   175→
   176→def _has_objective_cycle(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    plan: dict[str, object],
   179→) -> bool | None:
   180→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   183→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   184→        )
   185→
   186→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   187→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   188→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   189→        return None
   190→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   191→
   192→
   193→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   194→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   195→) -> bool:
   196→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   197→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   198→        return False
   199→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   200→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   201→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   202→        return False
   203→
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   206→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   207→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   208→            score_display_mode,
   209→        )
   210→
   211→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   212→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   214→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   215→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   216→        return False
   217→    if not queue_empty:
   218→        return False
   219→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   220→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   221→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   222→    # in the next cycle.
   223→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   224→    return True
   225→
   226→
   227→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   228→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   229→    plan: dict[str, object],
   230→) -> bool:
   231→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   232→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   233→        return False
   234→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   235→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   236→        return False
   237→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   238→        plan,
   239→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   240→    )

Perfect! Now I have a complete understanding. Let me create a comprehensive summary document.

## Summary: Subjective Queue Policy in Desloppify

Based on thorough analysis of the codebase, here's how the subjective queue policy works:

### **1. Categories of Subjective Work (3 categories)**

Subjective dimensions are classified into three mutually-exclusive categories based on their scoring history:

#### **A. Unscored (Placeholder)**
- **Definition**: Dimensions that have never been reviewed and scored
- **Detection** (in `stale.py:current_unscored_ids()`):
  - Checks `subjective_assessments[dim_key]["placeholder"]` flag first
  - Falls back to `dimension_scores[*]["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["placeholder"]`
  - Also unscored if: `score == 0 AND failing == 0 AND checks == 0` (all zeros)
- **Queue behavior**: Unconditionally added to plan queue at cycle boundaries
- **ID format**: `subjective::${slugified_dimension_key}`

#### **B. Stale (Needs Re-review)**
- **Definition**: Dimensions previously scored but their assessment is outdated
- **Detection** (in `scorecard_projection.py:all_subjective_entries()` and `stale.py:current_stale_ids()`):
  - Either `dimension_scores[*]["stale"] == true` (explicit flag)
  - OR `subjective_assessments[dim_key]["needs_review_refresh"] == true` (assessment marked stale)
- **Queue behavior**: Conditional - injected only when no objective backlog exists OR cycle just completed

#### **C. Under-Target (Not Yet Achieved Target Score)**
- **Definition**: Dimensions with current, non-stale assessments but below target score
- **Criteria** (in `stale.py:current_under_target_ids()`):
  - NOT `placeholder`
  - NOT `stale`
  - `strict_score < target_strict` (default 80.0)
- **Queue behavior**: Same as stale - conditional on objective backlog

**Note**: These are computed independently but exclude each other:
```python
# Under-target explicitly excludes stale and unscored (line 107-108 stale.py)
if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
    under_target.add(item_id)
```

---

### **2. Defer Logic (Treats Under-Target and Stale Identically)**

The defer logic in `auto_cluster_sync.py` treats `stale` and `under_target` the same way:

#### **When Objective Backlog Exists**
- Both stale and under-target IDs are **deferred** (removed from visible queue)
- Defer metadata is tracked in `subjective_defer_meta` to escalate if they stay deferred too long
- **Escalation rules** (in `defer_policy.py`):
  - Escalate after 3 consecutive scans with same deferred IDs
  - OR escalate after 7 days of being deferred
  - When escalated: deferred IDs are forced visible via `_promote_subjective_ids()` mechanism

#### **When No Objective Backlog**
- Both stale and under-target IDs are **injected** into the queue immediately
- The `should_inject` gate (line 244 in `dimensions.py`):
  ```python
  should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
  ```

**Code evidence**:
- Lines 220-221 in `auto_cluster_sync.py`: `injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids`
- Lines 280-281 in `auto_cluster_sync.py`: same eviction logic for both categories

---

### **3. Cycle Phase Determination (3 phases: start, mid, end)**

Cycle phase is determined by **whether a score snapshot exists** in `plan_start_scores`:

#### **Start Phase (Cycle Just Completed)**
- **Detector**: `cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")`
- Computed in `plan_reconcile.py` line 255
- When `plan_start_scores` is empty or missing, it means the previous cycle completed and queue is empty
- Trigger: automatic injection of stale/under-target even with objective backlog

#### **Mid-Cycle Phase**
- **Detector**: `is_mid_cycle(plan)` from `context.py` line 52-60
- Returns True when `plan["plan_start_scores"]` contains real score values
- Returns False when it's empty OR contains sentinel `{"reset": True}`
- The reset sentinel is set by `reset_plan()` (line 40 in `operations/lifecycle.py`)
- Effect: Subjective dimensions are pruned from queue mid-cycle

#### **End Phase (Normal Operation with Backlog)**
- **Detector**: `plan_start_scores` has real values AND objective backlog exists
- This is the "normal" operating state where subjective work defers
- Mid-cycle subjective dimensions are kept only if no objective backlog

**Lifecycle Flow**:
```
Empty Queue → plan_start_scores = {} → cycle_just_completed = True
    ↓
User starts work → _seed_plan_start_scores() → plan_start_scores = {strict: X, ...}
    ↓
Mid-cycle work (is_mid_cycle = True)
    ↓
User clears queue → _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty() → plan_start_scores = {} → back to start
```

---

### **4. Subjective Defer Metadata (`subjective_defer_meta`)**

Stored in `plan["subjective_defer_meta"]` when objective backlog exists. Contains:

**Structure**:
```python
{
    "deferred_review_ids": list[str],      # IDs being deferred
    "last_deferred_scan": int,              # scan_count when last updated
    "last_deferred_at": str,                # ISO timestamp of last defer
    "defer_count": int,                     # consecutive scans with same IDs
    "first_deferred_scan": int,             # scan_count when deferral started
    "first_deferred_at": str,               # ISO timestamp of first defer
    "force_visible_ids": list[str] | None,  # Set when escalation triggered
}
```

**Lifecycle** (in `defer_policy.py`):
1. `update_defer_state()` (line 94): Records current deferred IDs and increments `defer_count` if IDs unchanged
2. `should_escalate_defer_state()` (line 141): Checks escalation criteria
3. If escalated: `force_visible_ids` is set and those IDs are promoted ahead of objective backlog (via `_promote_subjective_ids()`)

**Cleared** when:
- No deferred IDs remain (line 286 in `auto_cluster_sync.py`: `_clear_subjective_defer_meta()`)
- Cycle just completed (defer constraint no longer applies)

---

### **5. Sync Functions Summary**

| Function | Location | Behavior |
|----------|----------|----------|
| `sync_unscored_dimensions()` | `sync/dimensions.py` line 129 | **Unconditional**: Always injects unscored at cycle boundaries; pruned mid-cycle |
| `sync_stale_dimensions()` | `sync/dimensions.py` line 194 | **Conditional**: Inject when `not objective_backlog \|\| cycle_just_completed`; evict when backlog returns |
| `sync_subjective_clusters()` | `auto_cluster_sync.py` line 116 | Creates auto-clusters for grouped subjective review; handles defer escalation |

---

### **6. Key Distinctions**

1. **Stale vs Under-Target Treatment**: Functionally identical in defer logic, but detected differently:
   - Stale = assessment expired (explicit `needs_review_refresh` flag)
   - Under-Target = assessment current but score low (continuous measurement)

2. **Unscored Priority**: Always surfaces at cycle start; never deferred regardless of backlog

3. **Never Reorders**: All subjective injections append to **back** of queue; never promote existing items

4. **Cycle Sentinel**: The `{"reset": True}` sentinel in `plan_start_scores` is crucial - it prevents false "cycle completed" detection after plan resets

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain, and evict them
     9→  again when objective backlog returns.
    10→
    11→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, QueueSyncResult
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
