```
    30→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    32→
    33→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    34→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    35→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    36→    """
    37→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    38→        state,
    39→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→
    43→def current_under_target_ids(
    44→    state: StateModel,
    45→    *,
    46→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    47→) -> set[str]:
    48→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    49→
    50→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    51→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    52→    """
    53→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    54→        state,
    55→        target_strict=target_strict,
    56→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→# Helpers
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    65→    order: list[str],
    66→    *,
    67→    keep_ids: set[str],
    68→    pruned: list[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    71→    to_remove = [
    72→        fid for fid in order
    73→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    74→        and fid not in keep_ids
    75→    ]
    76→    for fid in to_remove:
    77→        order.remove(fid)
    78→        pruned.append(fid)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    82→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
    83→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    85→        return set()
    86→    return {
    87→        str(fid)
    88→        for fid in skipped
    89→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    90→    }
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    *,
    96→    skipped_ids: set[str],
    97→    pruned: list[str],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return
   102→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   103→    for fid in to_remove:
   104→        order.remove(fid)
   105→        pruned.append(fid)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   109→    order: list[str],
   110→    *,
   111→    inject_ids: set[str],
   112→    injected: list[str],
   113→) -> None:
   114→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   115→
   116→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   117→    """
   118→    existing = set(order)
   119→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   120→        if sid not in existing:
   121→            order.append(sid)
   122→            injected.append(sid)
   123→
   124→
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→
   129→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    state: StateModel,
   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   134→
   135→    1. **Prune** — always remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order``
   136→       that are no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs —
   137→       that is ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).  Pruning runs even
   138→       mid-cycle to clean up items that were incorrectly injected by older
   139→       code that lacked the mid-cycle guard.
   140→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   141→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —
   142→       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.
   143→    """
   144→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   145→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   146→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   147→
   148→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   149→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   150→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   151→    )
   152→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   153→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   154→
   155→    if not mid_cycle:
   156→        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a
   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   161→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   162→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   163→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   164→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   165→
   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   167→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   168→
   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   170→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   171→    # Mid-cycle: prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty) since they
   172→    # shouldn't be in the queue mid-cycle at all.
   173→    if mid_cycle:
   174→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=set(), pruned=result.pruned)
   175→    else:
   176→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   179→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.
   180→    if not mid_cycle:
   181→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   182→            order,
   183→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   184→            injected=result.injected,
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   195→    plan: PlanModel,
   196→    state: StateModel,
   197→    *,
   198→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   199→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   200→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   204→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   205→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   206→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   207→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   208→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   209→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   210→       b. A cycle just completed.
   211→       Never reorders existing items.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   215→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   216→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   217→    )
   218→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   219→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   220→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   221→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   222→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   223→
   224→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   225→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   226→
   227→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   228→
   229→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   230→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   231→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   232→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   233→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   234→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   235→    if mid_cycle:
   236→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   237→    else:
   238→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   239→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   240→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   241→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   242→
   243→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   244→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   245→
   246→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   247→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "current_under_target_ids",
   254→    "current_unscored_ids",
   255→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→
```

```
    41→def _subjective_state_sets(
    42→    state: StateModel,
    43→    *,
    44→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    45→    target_strict: float,
    46→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:

    61→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""
    63→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    64→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    65→        return set()
    66→    return {
    67→        str(fid)
    68→        for fid in skipped
    69→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    70→    }
    71→

    74→    plan.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes
   114→

   116→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    issues: dict,
   120→    clusters: dict,
   121→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],

   126→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   127→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   128→) -> int:
   129→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   130→    changes = 0
   131→    skipped_subjective_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   132→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   133→    if skipped_subjective_ids and isinstance(order, list):
   134→        overlap = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_subjective_ids]
   135→        for fid in overlap:
   136→            order.remove(fid)
   137→            changes += 1
   138→

   146→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   150→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   151→    )
   152→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   153→        fid
   154→        for fid in all_subjective_ids
   155→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   159→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   160→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   161→        description = (
   162→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   163→        )
   164→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   165→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   166→            plan,
   167→            clusters,
   168→            existing_by_key,
   169→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,

   172→            description=description,

   175→        )
   176→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   177→
   178→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   179→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   180→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   181→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   182→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   183→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(

   193→        )
   194→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)

   196→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(
   197→        fid for fid in under_target_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   198→    )
   199→    stale_candidate_ids = sorted(
   200→        fid
   201→        for fid in stale_state_ids
   202→        if fid not in unscored_state_ids and fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   203→    )
   204→    deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(
   205→        set(stale_candidate_ids) | set(under_target_queue_ids)
   206→    )
   207→
   208→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   209→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   210→    ut_prune = [
   211→        fid
   212→        for fid in prev_ut_ids
   213→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   214→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   215→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   216→        and fid in order
   217→    ]
   218→    for fid in ut_prune:
   219→        order.remove(fid)
   220→        changes += 1
   221→
   222→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   223→
   224→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   225→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   226→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   227→        description = (
   228→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   229→            f"dimensions under target score"
   230→        )
   231→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   232→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(
   233→            plan,
   234→            clusters,
   235→            existing_by_key,
   236→            [REDACTED],
   237→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   238→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   239→            description=description,
   240→            action=action,
   241→            now=now,
   242→            optional=True,
   243→        )
   244→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   245→
   246→        existing_order = set(order)
   247→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   248→            if fid not in existing_order:
   249→                order.append(fid)
   250→
   251→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:
   252→        if deferred_subjective_ids:
   253→            defer_state = update_defer_state(
   254→                plan.get(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY),
   255→                state=state,
   256→                deferred_ids=set(deferred_subjective_ids),
   257→                options=DeferUpdateOptions(
   258→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   259→                    now=now,
   260→                ),
   261→            )
   262→            escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(
   263→                defer_state,
   264→                state=state,
   265→                options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   266→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   267→                    now=now,
   268→                ),
   269→            )
   270→            if escalated:
   271→                defer_state[_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = list(deferred_subjective_ids)
   272→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   273→                changes += _promote_subjective_ids(order, deferred_subjective_ids)
   274→            else:
   275→                defer_state.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   276→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   277→                objective_evict = [
   278→                    fid
   279→                    for fid in order
   280→                    if fid in under_target_ids or fid in stale_state_ids
   281→                ]
   282→                for fid in objective_evict:
   283→                    order.remove(fid)
   284→                    changes += 1
   285→        else:
   286→            _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   287→    else:
   288→        _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan)
   289→
   290→    return changes
   291→
   292→
   293→__all__ = ["prune_stale_clusters", "sync_issue_clusters", "sync_subjective_clusters"]
   294→
```
```

1→"""Shared defer-tracking helpers for triage and subjective rerun fairness."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
     9→
    10→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3
    11→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class DeferUpdateOptions:
    16→    """Options for durable defer-state updates."""
    17→
    18→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    19→    now: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DeferEscalationOptions:
    24→    """Options for defer-state escalation checks."""
    25→
    26→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    27→    now: str | None = None
    28→    min_scans: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS
    29→    min_days: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS
    30→
    31→
    32→def _coerce_non_negative_int(value: object, *, default: int = 0) -> int:
    33→    try:
    34→        parsed = int(value)
    35→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    36→        return default
    37→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else default
    38→
    39→
    40→def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:
    41→    return _coerce_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count"), default=0)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _normalize_timestamp(raw: object) -> str | None:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
    46→        return None
    47→    value = raw.strip()
    48→    return value or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _parse_timestamp(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    52→    normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    53→    if not normalized:
    54→        return None
    55→    try:
    56→        return datetime.fromisoformat(normalized.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    57→    except ValueError:
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→def _current_timestamp(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    now: str | None = None,
    65→) -> str:
    66→    for raw in (state.get("last_scan"), now):
    67→        normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    68→        if normalized:
    69→            return normalized
    70→    return utc_now()
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalized_ids(ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    74→    return sorted({str(issue_id).strip() for issue_id in ids if str(issue_id).strip()})
    75→
    76→
    77→def _deferred_ids(
    78→    defer_state: dict[str, object],
    79→    *,
    80→    deferred_ids_field: str,
    81→) -> list[str]:
    82→    raw = defer_state.get(deferred_ids_field)
    83→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted(
    86→        {
    87→            str(issue_id).strip()
    88→            for issue_id in raw
    89→            if str(issue_id).strip()
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def update_defer_state(
    95→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    deferred_ids: set[str],
    99→    options: DeferUpdateOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> dict[str, object]:
   101→    """Return updated durable defer metadata for the current deferred ID set."""
   102→    resolved_options = options or DeferUpdateOptions()
   103→    previous = defer_state if isinstance(defer_state, dict) else {}
   104→    current_ids = _normalized_ids(deferred_ids)
   105→    previous_ids = _deferred_ids(
   106→        previous,
   107→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   108→    )
   109→    scan_count = _scan_count(state)
   110→    timestamp = _current_timestamp(state, now=resolved_options.now)
   111→
   112→    updated = dict(previous)
   113→    updated[resolved_options.deferred_ids_field] = current_ids
   114→    updated["last_deferred_scan"] = scan_count
   115→    updated["last_deferred_at"] = timestamp
   116→
   117→    same_ids = bool(current_ids) and current_ids == previous_ids
   118→    if same_ids:
   119→        defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("defer_count"), default=1)
   120→        prior_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("last_deferred_scan"), default=-1)
   121→        if prior_scan != scan_count:
   122→            defer_count += 1
   123→        branch_updates = {
   124→            "defer_count": max(1, defer_count),
   125→            "first_deferred_scan": _coerce_non_negative_int(
   126→            previous.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   127→            default=scan_count,
   128→            ),
   129→            "first_deferred_at": str(previous.get("first_deferred_at") or timestamp),
   130→        }
   131→    else:
   132→        branch_updates = {
   133→            "defer_count": 1 if current_ids else 0,
   134→            "first_deferred_scan": scan_count,
   135→            "first_deferred_at": timestamp,
   136→        }
   137→    updated.update(branch_updates)
   138→    return updated
   139→
   140→
   141→def should_escalate_defer_state(
   142→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
   143→    *,
   144→    state: StateModel,
   145→    options: DeferEscalationOptions | None = None,
   146→) -> bool:
   147→    """Return True when defer metadata crosses configured escalation bounds."""
   148→    if not isinstance(defer_state, dict):
   149→        return False
   150→    resolved_options = options or DeferEscalationOptions()
   151→
   152→    deferred_ids = _deferred_ids(
   153→        defer_state,
   154→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   155→    )
   156→    if not deferred_ids:
   157→        return False
   158→
   159→    min_scans = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_scans))
   160→    min_days = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_days))
   161→
   162→    defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(defer_state.get("defer_count"), default=0)
   163→    if defer_count >= min_scans:
   164→        return True
   165→
   166→    first_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(
   167→        defer_state.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   168→        default=-1,
   169→    )
   170→    current_scan = _scan_count(state)
   171→    if first_scan >= 0 and current_scan - first_scan + 1 >= min_scans:
   172→        return True
   173→
   174→    first_deferred_at = _parse_timestamp(defer_state.get("first_deferred_at"))
   175→    current_at = _parse_timestamp(state.get("last_scan")) or _parse_timestamp(
   176→        resolved_options.now
   177→    )
   178→    if first_deferred_at is None or current_at is None:
   179→        return False
   180→    return (current_at - first_deferred_at).total_seconds() >= min_days * 86400
   181→
   182→
   183→__all__ = [
   184→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS",
   185→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS",
   186→    "DeferEscalationOptions",
   187→    "DeferUpdateOptions",
   188→    "should_escalate_defer_state",
   189→    "update_defer_state",
   190→]
   191→
```

```
1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def current_stale_ids(
    25→    state: StateModel,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    28→) -> set[str]:
    29→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    30→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    31→    if not dim_scores:
    32→        return set()
    33→
    34→    stale: set[str] = set()
    35→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    36→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    37→            continue
    38→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    39→        if dim_key:
    40→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    41→    return stale
    42→
    43→
    44→def current_unscored_ids(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    *,
    47→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    48→) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    50→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    51→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    52→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    53→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    54→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    55→                continue
    56→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    57→                continue
    58→            if dim_key:
    59→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    60→        return unscored
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    unscored = set()
    67→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    68→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    69→            continue
    70→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    71→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    72→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    73→            continue
    74→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    75→            continue
    76→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    77→        if dim_key:
    78→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    79→    return unscored
    80→
    81→
    82→def current_under_target_ids(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    86→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    87→) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    89→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    90→    if not dim_scores:
    91→        return set()
    92→
    93→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    94→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    95→
    96→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    97→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    98→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    99→            continue
   100→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   101→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   102→            continue
   103→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   104→        if not dim_key:
   105→            continue
   106→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   107→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
   108→            under_target.add(item_id)
   109→    return under_target
   110→
   111→
   112→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   113→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   114→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   115→    if not review_ids:
   116→        return ""
   117→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   118→
   119→
   120→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   124→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   125→
   126→
   127→def is_triage_stale(
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   132→
   133→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   134→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   135→    forces triage stages to the front.
   136→    """
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   139→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   144→    "current_stale_ids",
   145→    "current_under_target_ids",
   146→    "current_unscored_ids",
   147→    "is_triage_stale",
   148→    "open_review_ids",
   149→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   150→]
   151→
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues).
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available.
    32→    Falls back to scanning *state_or_issues* directly — accepts either a
    33→    full ``StateModel`` (looks up ``issues``) or a raw issues dict.
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→
```

```
1→"""status command: score dashboard with per-tier progress."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import json
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_issue_inventory
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import open_scope_breakdown
    11→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import compute_health_breakdown
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    13→    scorecard_dimensions_payload,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.state_scoring import score_snapshot, suppression_metrics
    16→
    17→from .flow import render_terminal_status
    18→
    19→
    20→def cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    21→    """Show score dashboard."""
    22→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    23→    state = runtime.state
    24→    config = runtime.config
    25→
    26→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
    27→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    28→    scorecard_dims = scorecard_dimensions_payload(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    29→    subjective_measures = [row for row in scorecard_dims if row.get("subjective")]
    30→    suppression = suppression_metrics(state)
    31→
    32→    if getattr(args, "json", False):
    33→        print(
    34→            json.dumps(
    35→                _status_json_payload(
    36→                    state,
    37→                    stats,
    38→                    dim_scores,
    39→                    scorecard_dims,
    40→                    subjective_measures,
    41→                    suppression,
    42→                ),
    43→                indent=2,
    44→            )
    45→        )
    46→        return
    47→
    48→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
    49→        return
    50→
    51→    render_terminal_status(
    52→        args,
    53→        state=state,
    54→        config=config,
    55→        stats=stats,
    56→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    57→        scorecard_dims=scorecard_dims,
    58→        subjective_measures=subjective_measures,
    59→        suppression=suppression,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→
    63→def _status_json_payload(
    64→    state: dict,
    65→    stats: dict,
    66→    dim_scores: dict,
    67→    scorecard_dims: list[dict],
    68→    subjective_measures: list[dict],
    69→    suppression: dict,
    70→) -> dict:
    71→    scores = score_snapshot(state)
    72→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    73→    open_scope = (
    74→        open_scope_breakdown(issues, state.get("scan_path"))
    75→        if isinstance(issues, dict)
    76→        else None
    77→    )
    78→    return {
    79→        "overall_score": scores.overall,
    80→        "objective_score": scores.objective,
    81→        "strict_score": scores.strict,
    82→        "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    83→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    84→        "score_breakdown": compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores) if dim_scores else None,
    85→        "scorecard_dimensions": scorecard_dims,
    86→        "subjective_measures": subjective_measures,
    87→        "potentials": state.get("potentials"),
    88→        "codebase_metrics": state.get("codebase_metrics"),
    89→        "stats": stats,
    90→        "open_scope": open_scope,
    91→        "suppression": suppression,
    92→        "scan_count": state.get("scan_count", 0),
    93→        "last_scan": state.get("last_scan"),
    94→        "scan_metadata": state.get("scan_metadata", {}),
    95→    }
    96→
    97→__all__ = ["cmd_status"]
    98→
```

```
    51→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    53→
    54→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    55→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    56→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    57→    state = runtime.state
    58→
    59→    if not require_issue_inventory(state):
    60→        return
    61→
    62→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    63→    if config_warning:
    64→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    65→
    66→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    67→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    68→
    69→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    70→    if output:
    71→        try:
    72→            from pathlib import Path
    73→            output_path = Path(output)
    74→            if output_path.exists():
    75→                print(colorize(
    76→                    f"  {output} already exists — refusing to overwrite.",
    77→                    "red",
    78→                ))
    79→                print(colorize(
    80→                    f"  Delete it first (`desloppify plan reset` or `rm {output}`), "
    81→                    f"or choose a different filename.",
    82→                    "dim",
    83→                ))
    84→                return
    85→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    86→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    87→            print_agent_plan(
    88→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    89→                next_command=next_command,
    90→            )
    91→        except OSError as e:
    92→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    93→    else:
    94→        print(plan_md)
    95→        print()
    96→        print_agent_plan(
    97→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    98→            next_command=next_command,
    99→        )
   100→
   101→
   102→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   103→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
   104→    cmd_plan_output(args)
   105→
   106→
   107→def _plan_queue_line(state: dict, plan: dict) -> str:
   108→    """Build the queue headline with a resilient fallback."""
   109→    try:
   110→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   111→        return format_queue_headline(breakdown)
   112→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   113→        logger.debug("Plan show fell back to raw queue counts.", exc_info=exc)
   114→        ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   115→        return f"Queue: {ordered} items prioritized"
   116→
   117→
   118→def _skip_kind_counts(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]:
   119→    """Return total skipped plus temporary/permanent/false-positive counts."""
   120→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   121→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
   122→    kind_counts = {
   123→        kind: sum(1 for entry in skipped.values() if entry.get("kind") == kind)
   124→        for kind in USER_SKIP_KINDS
   125→    }
   126→    return (
   127→        total_skipped,
   128→        kind_counts["temporary"],
   129→        kind_counts["permanent"],
   130→        kind_counts["false_positive"],
   131→    )
   132→
   133→
   134→def _print_cluster_summary(plan: dict, *, active: str | None) -> None:
   135→    """Print cluster counts and focused cluster details."""
   136→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   137→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
   138→    if not clusters:
   139→        return
   140→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   141→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   142→        member_count = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   143→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   144→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   145→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
   146→
   147→
   148→def _print_commit_tracking(plan: dict) -> None:
   149→    """Print commit-tracking summary when enabled and populated."""
   150→    cfg = load_config()
   151→    if not cfg.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
   152→        return
   153→    ct = commit_tracking_summary(plan)
   154→    if ct["total"] <= 0:
   155→        return
   156→    pr_num = cfg.get("commit_pr", 0)
   157→    pr_str = f"  PR: #{pr_num}" if pr_num else ""
   158→    print(
   159→        f"  Commit tracking:  {ct['uncommitted']} uncommitted, "
   160→        f"{ct['committed']} committed ({ct['total']} issues){pr_str}"
   161→    )
   162→
   163→
   164→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   165→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
   166→    plan = load_plan()
   167→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   168→
   169→    queue_line = _plan_queue_line(runtime.state, plan)
   170→    total_skipped, temp_count, perm_count, fp_count = _skip_kind_counts(plan)
   171→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   172→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
   173→
   174→    described, noted = annotation_counts(plan)
   175→
   176→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
   177→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
   178→    print(f"  {queue_line}")
   179→    if total_skipped:
   180→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
   181→    else:
   182→        print("  Skipped:          0")
   183→    _print_cluster_summary(plan, active=active)
   184→    if described or noted:
   185→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
   186→    if active:
   187→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
   188→    if superseded:
   189→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
   190→
   191→    _print_commit_tracking(plan)
   192→
   193→
   194→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   195→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
   196→    plan = load_plan()
   197→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   198→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
   199→    reset_plan(plan)
   200→    append_log_entry(
   201→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
   202→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
   203→    )
   204→    save_plan(plan)
   205→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
   206→
   207→
   208→_PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS = {
   209→    "show": _cmd_plan_show,
   210→    "queue": cmd_plan_queue,
   211→    "reset": _cmd_plan_reset,
   212→    "reorder": cmd_plan_reorder,
   213→    "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   214→    "resolve": cmd_plan_resolve,
   215→    "note": cmd_plan_note,
   216→    "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   217→    "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   218→    "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   219→    "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   220→    "cluster": cmd_cluster_dispatch,
   221→    "triage": cmd_plan_triage,
   222→    "scan-gate": cmd_plan_scan_gate,
   223→    "commit-log": cmd_commit_log_dispatch,
   224→    "policy": cmd_policy_dispatch,
   225→    "repair-state": cmd_plan_repair_state,
   226→}
   227→
   228→
   229→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   230→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
   231→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
   232→    if plan_action is None:
   233→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   234→        return
   235→
   236→    handler = _PLAN_ACTION_HANDLERS.get(plan_action)
   237→    if handler is None:
   238→        print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   239→        return
   240→    handler(args)
   241→
   242→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   243→
```

```
1→"""Dimension analysis and debt computation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._scoring.detection import merge_potentials
     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     7→    DIMENSIONS,
     8→    TIER_WEIGHTS,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import compute_score_impact
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import path_scoped_issues
    12→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import STRUCTURAL_MERGE
    13→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    14→
    15→
    16→def _analyze_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict], state: StateModel) -> dict:
    17→    """Compute per-dimension structured analysis."""
    18→    if not dim_scores:
    19→        return {}
    20→
    21→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
    22→    return {
    23→        "lowest_dimensions": _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores, potentials),
    24→        "biggest_gap_dimensions": _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores, state)[:3],
    25→        "stagnant_dimensions": _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores, history),
    26→    }
    27→
    28→
    29→def _lowest_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, potentials: dict) -> list[dict]:
    30→    """Build summary entries for the lowest strict-scoring dimensions."""
    31→    sorted_dims = sorted(
    32→        (
    33→            (name, ds)
    34→            for name, ds in dim_scores.items()
    35→            if ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) < 100
    36→        ),
    37→        key=lambda item: item[1].get("strict", item[1]["score"]),
    38→    )
    39→
    40→    lowest = []
    41→    for name, ds in sorted_dims[:3]:
    42→        strict = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    43→        issues = ds.get("failing", 0)
    44→        impact = _dominant_detector_impact(
    45→            dim_scores=dim_scores,
    46→            detectors=ds.get("detectors", {}),
    47→            potentials=potentials,
    48→        )
    49→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in ds.get("detectors", {})
    50→        entry = {
    51→            "name": name,
    52→            "strict": round(strict, 1),
    53→            "failing": issues,
    54→            "impact": round(impact, 1),
    55→        }
    56→        if is_subjective:
    57→            entry["subjective"] = True
    58→            entry["impact_description"] = "re-review to improve"
    59→        lowest.append(entry)
    60→    return lowest
    61→
    62→
    63→def _biggest_gap_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    64→    """Build summary entries for dimensions with the biggest strict gap."""
    65→    biggest_gap = []
    66→    scoped = path_scoped_issues(
    67→        state.get("issues", {}), state.get("scan_path")
    68→    )
    69→    for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
    70→        lenient = ds["score"]
    71→        strict = ds.get("strict", lenient)
    72→        gap = lenient - strict
    73→        if gap > 1.0:
    74→            wontfix_count = sum(
    75→                1
    76→                for f in scoped.values()
    77→                if f["status"] == "wontfix"
    78→                and _issue_in_dimension(f, name, dim_scores)
    79→            )
    80→            biggest_gap.append(
    81→                {
    82→                    "name": name,
    83→                    "lenient": round(lenient, 1),
    84→                    "strict": round(strict, 1),
    85→                    "gap": round(gap, 1),
    86→                    "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
    87→                }
    88→            )
    89→    biggest_gap.sort(key=lambda x: -x["gap"])
    90→    return biggest_gap
    91→
    92→
    93→def _stagnant_dimensions(dim_scores: dict, history: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    94→    """Build summary entries for dimensions that have not moved recently."""
    95→    if len(history) < 3:
    96→        return []
    97→
    98→    stagnant = []
    99→    for name in dim_scores:
   100→        scores = _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name, history)
   101→        if len(scores) < 3 or max(scores) - min(scores) > 0.5:
   102→            continue
   103→        stagnant.append(
   104→            {
   105→                "name": name,
   106→                "strict": round(dim_scores[name].get("strict", dim_scores[name]["score"]), 1),
   107→                "stuck_scans": len(scores),
   108→            }
   109→        )
   110→    return stagnant
   111→
   112→
   113→def _recent_dimension_strict_scores(name: str, history: list[dict]) -> list[float]:
   114→    """Collect recent strict score samples for one dimension."""
   115→    scores: list[float] = []
   116→    for history_entry in history[-5:]:
   117→        dim_entry = (history_entry.get("dimension_scores") or {}).get(name)
   118→        if not dim_entry:
   119→            continue
   120→        strict_value = dim_entry.get("strict", dim_entry.get("score"))
   121→        if strict_value is not None:
   122→            scores.append(float(strict_value))
   123→    return scores
   124→
   125→
   126→def _dominant_detector_impact(
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict,
   129→    detectors: dict,
   130→    potentials: dict,
   131→) -> float:
   132→    """Estimate impact using the most consequential detector in a dimension."""
   133→    normalized_scores = {
   134→        key: {
   135→            "score": value["score"],
   136→            "tier": value.get("tier", 3),
   137→            "detectors": value.get("detectors", {}),
   138→        }
   139→        for key, value in dim_scores.items()
   140→    }
   141→    impact = 0.0
   142→    for detector_name, detector_data in detectors.items():
   143→        issue_count = int(detector_data.get("failing", 0) or 0)
   144→        if issue_count <= 0:
   145→            continue
   146→        detector_impact = compute_score_impact(
   147→            normalized_scores,
   148→            potentials,
   149→            detector_name,
   150→            issue_count,
   151→        )
   152→        impact = max(impact, detector_impact)
   153→    return impact
   154→
   155→
   156→def _issue_in_dimension(issue: dict, dim_name: str, dim_scores: dict) -> bool:
   157→    """Check if a issue's detector belongs to a dimension."""
   158→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
   159→    if detector in STRUCTURAL_MERGE:
   160→        detector = "structural"
   161→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   162→        if dim.name == dim_name and detector in dim.detectors:
   163→            return True
   164→    return False
   165→
   166→
   167→def _analyze_debt(dim_scores: dict, issues: dict, history: list[dict]) -> dict:
   168→    """Compute wontfix debt analysis."""
   169→    # Count wontfix
   170→    wontfix_count = sum(1 for f in issues.values() if f["status"] == "wontfix")
   171→
   172→    # Compute gap per dimension
   173→    worst_dim = None
   174→    worst_gap = 0.0
   175→    overall_lenient = 0.0
   176→    overall_strict = 0.0
   177→    if dim_scores:
   178→        w_sum_l = 0.0
   179→        w_sum_s = 0.0
   180→        w_total = 0.0
   181→        for name, ds in dim_scores.items():
   182→            tier = ds.get("tier", 3)
   183→            w = TIER_WEIGHTS.get(tier, 2)
   184→            w_sum_l += ds["score"] * w
   185→            w_sum_s += ds.get("strict", ds["score"]) * w
   186→            w_total += w
   187→            gap = ds["score"] - ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
   188→            if gap > worst_gap:
   189→                worst_gap = gap
   190→                worst_dim = name
   191→        if w_total > 0:
   192→            overall_lenient = round(w_sum_l / w_total, 1)
   193→            overall_strict = round(w_sum_s / w_total, 1)
   194→
   195→    overall_gap = round(overall_lenient - overall_strict, 1)
   196→
   197→    # Trend from history
   198→    trend = "stable"
   199→    if len(history) >= 3:
   200→        gaps = []
   201→        for h in history[-5:]:
   202→            hs = h.get("strict_score")
   203→            hl = h.get("overall_score")
   204→            if hs is not None and hl is not None:
   205→                gaps.append(hl - hs)
   206→        if len(gaps) >= 2:
   207→            if gaps[-1] > gaps[0] + 0.5:
   208→                trend = "growing"
   209→            elif gaps[-1] < gaps[0] - 0.5:
   210→                trend = "shrinking"
   211→
   212→    return {
   213→        "overall_gap": overall_gap,
   214→        "wontfix_count": wontfix_count,
   215→        "worst_dimension": worst_dim,
   216→        "worst_gap": round(worst_gap, 1),
   217→        "trend": trend,
   218→    }
   219→
```

```
1→"""Subjective-score helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→
     9→from .render_support import scorecard_subjective as _scorecard_subjective_impl
    10→
    11→
    12→def _scorecard_subjective(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    13→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    14→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    18→    state: dict,
    19→    dim_scores: dict,
    20→    *,
    21→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    22→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    23→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    24→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    25→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    26→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    27→            continue
    28→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    29→        if strict_val < threshold:
    30→            low.append(
    31→                (
    32→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    33→                    strict_val,
    34→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
    35→                )
    36→            )
    37→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    38→    return low
    39→
    40→
    41→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "_scorecard_subjective"]
    42→
```

```
    74→        carried_forward = bool(

    76→        )
```

```
   115→def _render_plan_focus(plan: dict[str, Any] | None) -> bool:
   116→    if not plan or not plan.get("active_cluster"):
   117→        return False
   118→    cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   119→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   120→    remaining = len(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   121→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   122→    desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   123→    print(
   124→        colorize(
   125→            f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   126→            "cyan",
   127→        )
   128→    )
   129→    print()
   130→    return True
   131→
   132→
   133→def _lowest_focus_context(
   134→    lowest_name: str,
   135→    dim_scores: dict[str, Any],
   136→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict[str, Any]],
   137→) -> tuple[str | None, float, int]:
   138→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   139→        if dim.name != lowest_name:
   140→            continue
   141→        dim_score = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   142→        if not dim_score:
   143→            return None, 101.0, 0
   144→        return (
   145→            "mechanical",
   146→            float(dim_score.get("strict", dim_score["score"])),
   147→            int(dim_score.get("failing", 0)),
   148→        )
   149→
   150→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   151→        if entry.get("name") != lowest_name:
   152→            continue
   153→        return (
   154→            "subjective",
   155→            float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   156→            0,
   157→        )
   158→    return None, 101.0, 0
   159→
   160→
   161→def _mechanical_focus_impact(
   162→    *,
   163→    lowest_name: str,
   164→    lowest_issues: int,
   165→    dim_scores: dict[str, Any],
   166→    state: StateModel,
   167→) -> float | None:
   168→    target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   169→    if target_dim is None:
   170→        return None
   171→    potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   172→    impact = 0.0
   173→    normalized_scores = {
   174→        key: {
   175→            "score": value["score"],
   176→            "tier": value.get("tier", 3),
   177→            "detectors": value.get("detectors", {}),
   178→        }
   179→        for key, value in dim_scores.items()
   180→        if "score" in value
   181→    }
   182→    for detector in target_dim.detectors:
   183→        impact = compute_score_impact(
   184→            normalized_scores,
   185→            potentials,
   186→            detector,
   187→            lowest_issues,
   188→        )
   189→        if impact > 0:
   190→            return impact
   191→    return impact
   192→
   193→
   194→def show_focus_suggestion(
   195→    dim_scores: dict[str, Any], state: StateModel, *, plan: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   196→) -> None:
   197→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   198→    if _render_plan_focus(plan):

   202→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)

   205→

   209→        scorecard_subjective,
   210→    )
   211→    if lowest_score >= 100:
   212→        return
   213→    if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   214→        print(

   217→                "cyan",
   218→            )
   219→        )
   220→        print()
   221→        return
   222→
   223→    impact = _mechanical_focus_impact(

   225→        lowest_issues=lowest_issues,
   226→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   227→        state=state,

   229→    if impact is None:

   231→    impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   232→    print(
   233→        colorize(
   234→            f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   235→            f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   236→            "cyan",
   237→        )
   238→    )
   239→    print()
```

## **File 1: `/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py` (Lines 1-258)**

### Key Functions:
- **`sync_unscored_dimensions()`** — Lines 129-187
- **`sync_stale_dimensions()`** — Lines 194-249
- **`current_unscored_ids()`** — Lines 30-40
- **`current_under_target_ids()`** — Lines 43-57

### How Stale and Under-Target are Treated:

**In `sync_stale_dimensions()` (lines 201-249):**

Line 215-220 defines the key distinction:
```python
stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(...)
under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids  # LINE 220: CONFLATION
```

**The exact conflation logic:**
- **Line 220**: `injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids` — treats both stale and under-target as a combined set for injection purposes
- **Lines 228-240** (cleanup logic):
  - Mid-cycle: keep both only if no objective backlog (line 236)
  - Non-mid-cycle: keep both unless objective backlog exists *and* cycle hasn't just completed (lines 238-240)
- **Lines 244-247** (injection logic):
  - `should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed` (line 244)
  - If true, inject both stale and under-target together (line 247)

**Ordering:** Always appended to back of queue (no reordering), per line 116.

---

## **File 2: `/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py` (Lines 1-294)**

### Key Functions:
- **`sync_subjective_clusters()`** — Lines 116-290
- **`_subjective_state_sets()`** — Lines 41-58
- **`_promote_subjective_ids()`** — Lines 77-113

### How `injectable_ids` is Computed:

**Lines 145-206 show the full computation:**

```python
stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(...)  # Line 145-147

# Track what's currently in queue
unscored_queue_ids = sorted(fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids)  # 149-151
stale_queue_ids = sorted(fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids)  # 152-156

under_target_queue_ids = sorted(fid for fid in under_target_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids)  # 196-198
stale_candidate_ids = sorted(fid for fid in stale_state_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids and fid not in skipped_subjective_ids)  # 199-203

# LINE 204-206: CONFLATION OF STALE AND UNDER_TARGET
deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(set(stale_candidate_ids) | set(under_target_queue_ids))  # Line 204-206
```

**Key insight:** `deferred_subjective_ids` (lines 204-206) combines stale and under_target for deferral purposes, treating them as a single group when deciding to escalate.

### How `defer_meta` Works:

**Lines 251-288 (escalation logic):**
- **Line 251**: `if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:`
  - If objective items exist and cycle hasn't just completed, enter defer logic
- **Lines 253-260**: `update_defer_state()` is called with `deferred_ids=set(deferred_subjective_ids)` — the combined stale+under_target set
- **Lines 262-273**: `should_escalate_defer_state()` checks if deferred state has crossed threshold
  - If escalated: promote deferred subjective IDs to front (line 273), store in `force_visible_ids` (line 271)
  - If NOT escalated: evict all stale and under-target from queue (lines 277-284)
- **Line 286 & 288**: Clear defer metadata when no objective items or cycle just completed

---

## **File 3: `/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/defer_policy.py` (Lines 1-190)**

### Escalation Logic:

**`should_escalate_defer_state()` — Lines 141-180:**

Returns `True` when ANY of these conditions are met:

1. **Line 163-164** (Scan-count escalation):
   ```python
   defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(defer_state.get("defer_count"), default=0)
   if defer_count >= min_scans:  # DEFAULT: DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3 (line 10)
       return True
   ```

2. **Line 171-172** (Scan-distance escalation):
   ```python
   if first_scan >= 0 and current_scan - first_scan + 1 >= min_scans:
       return True
   ```

3. **Line 180** (Time-based escalation):
   ```python
   return (current_at - first_deferred_at).total_seconds() >= min_days * 86400
   # DEFAULT: DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7 (line 11)
   ```

### How `defer_count` is Tracked:

**`update_defer_state()` — Lines 94-138:**

- **Line 117**: `same_ids = bool(current_ids) and current_ids == previous_ids`
- **Lines 118-130** (if same deferred IDs as before):
  - Increment `defer_count` each time the same IDs are deferred in different scans (line 122)
  - Track `first_deferred_scan`, `first_deferred_at`, `last_deferred_scan`, `last_deferred_at`
- **Lines 131-136** (if IDs changed):
  - Reset `defer_count` to 1, reset `first_deferred_scan` and `first_deferred_at`

---

## **Summary: Stale vs Under-Target Conflation**

| Aspect | Location | How Conflated |
|--------|----------|---|
| **ID collection** | `dimensions.py:220`, `auto_cluster_sync.py:204-206` | `(stale_ids \| under_target_ids)` combined into single set |
| **Queue injection** | `dimensions.py:247` | Both injected together when objective backlog clears |
| **Queue eviction** | `auto_cluster_sync.py:277-284` | Both evicted together when objective backlog exists |
| **Escalation** | `auto_cluster_sync.py:262-273` | Escalation applies to combined set; both promoted or both evicted |
| **Deferral tracking** | `auto_cluster_sync.py:204-206, 253-260` | Both tracked in single `deferred_subjective_ids` set |

**Priority/Ordering:**
- No special priority between stale and under-target — they're treated as equal members of one logical group
- Always appended to queue back (no reordering)
- When escalated, promoted ahead of objective backlog (lines 77-113 in `auto_cluster_sync.py`)
- Escalation happens after 3 scans OR 7 days of continuous deferral

```
    61→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:

    66→def effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    67→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""

    88→def _cluster_type_label(cluster_name: str, action_type: str) -> str:

    90→        return CLUSTER_TYPE_LABELS.cluster_names[cluster_name]

    94→def _render_cluster_files(members: list[dict]) -> None:

   100→        return

   102→    print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   103→    for filename, count in file_counts.most_common(3):
   104→        print(f"    {filename} ({count})")
   105→    remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   106→    print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   107→
   108→
   109→def _render_cluster_sample(members: list[dict]) -> None:
   110→    print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   111→    for member in members[:3]:
   112→        summary = member.get("summary") or member.get("id", "")

   118→def cluster_action_commands(cluster_name: str) -> dict[str, str]:
   119→    """Return semantic cluster action commands independent of terminal labels."""
   120→    return {
   121→        "resolve_all": f'desloppify plan resolve "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --confirm',
   122→        "drill_in": f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   123→        "skip": f"desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}",
   124→    }
   125→
   126→
   127→def _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:

   131→    print(

   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name: str) -> None:

   141→    print(

   146→    )

   148→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  {commands['skip']}", "dim"))
   149→
   150→
   151→def _step_display_text(step: str | dict) -> str:
   152→    """Extract display text from an action step (string or dict with title)."""
   153→    if isinstance(step, dict):
   154→        return step.get("title", str(step))
   155→    return str(step)
   156→
   157→
   158→def render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   159→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   160→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   161→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   162→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   163→    is_optional = bool(item.get("cluster_optional"))
   164→    type_label = _cluster_type_label(cluster_name, action_type)
   165→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
   166→    done_count = sum(1 for s in action_steps if isinstance(s, dict) and s.get("done"))
   167→    step_badge = f" [{done_count}/{len(action_steps)} steps done]" if action_steps else ""
   168→    optional_tag = " — optional" if is_optional else ""
   169→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} issues{step_badge}{optional_tag})", "bold"))
   170→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   171→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   172→
   173→    # Action steps
   174→    if action_steps:
   175→        show_count = min(3, len(action_steps))
   176→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps[:show_count], 1):
   177→            marker = "[x]" if isinstance(step, dict) and step.get("done") else "[ ]"
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   179→        remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count
   180→        if remaining > 0:
   181→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more — drill in to view all", "dim"))
   182→
   183→    dep_order = item.get("dependency_order")
   184→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:
   185→        print(colorize("  Priority: complete before other clusters", "cyan"))
   186→
   187→    # Cluster members
   188→    members = item.get("members", [])
   189→    if members:
   190→        _render_cluster_files(members)
   191→        _render_cluster_sample(members)
   192→
   193→    # Primary action
   194→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
   195→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   196→    if autofix_hint:
   197→        print(colorize(f"\n  Try auto first: {autofix_hint}", "cyan"))
   198→        print(colorize("  If auto finds 0, drill into individual issues:", "dim"))
   199→    if primary_command:
   200→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   201→
   202→    if is_optional:
   203→        _render_optional_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   204→        return
   205→
   206→    _render_required_cluster_commands(cluster_name)
   207→
   208→
   209→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   210→    del explain
   211→    total = queue.get("total", 0)
   212→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   213→    # When the only item is the "run scan" workflow action, show "Queue cleared"
   214→    # instead of a misleading "1 items" count.
   215→    if (
   216→        total == 1
   217→        and len(items) == 1
   218→        and items[0].get("id") == "workflow::run-scan"
   219→    ):
   220→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared (1 workflow step)", "bold"))
   221→    else:
   222→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {total} item{'s' if total != 1 else ''}", "bold"))
   223→        if total > 5:
   224→            print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))
   225→
   226→
   227→def show_empty_queue(
   228→    queue: dict,
   229→    strict: float | None,
   230→    *,
   231→    plan_start_strict: float | None = None,
   232→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   233→) -> bool:
   234→    del target_strict
   235→    if queue.get("items"):
   236→        return False
   237→    if plan_start_strict is not None and strict is not None:
   238→        delta = format_plan_delta(strict, plan_start_strict)
   239→        delta_str = f" ({delta})" if delta else ""
   240→        print(colorize("\n  Queue cleared!", "green"))
   241→        print(colorize(
   242→            f"  Frozen plan-start: strict {plan_start_strict:.1f} → Live estimate: strict {strict:.1f}{delta_str}",
   243→            "cyan",
   244→        ))
   245→        print(colorize(
   246→            "  Run `desloppify scan` now to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   247→            "dim",
   248→        ))
   249→        return True
   250→
   251→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   252→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   253→    return True
   254→
   255→
   256→def render_compact_item(item: dict, idx: int, total: int) -> None:
   257→    """One-line summary for cluster drill-in items after the first."""
   258→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   259→    tag = effort_tag(item)
   260→    tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
   261→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   262→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) and (plan_cluster.get("action_steps") or []):
   263→        tag_str += " [plan]"
   264→    fid = item.get("id", "")
   265→    short = fid.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8] if "::" in fid else fid
   266→    print(f"  [{idx + 1}/{total}] [{confidence}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   267→    print(colorize(f"         {item.get('file', '')}  [{short}]", "dim"))
   268→
```

```
    52→    queue: dict,
```
