    54→    state: dict,
    55→    narrative: dict,
    56→    plan_data: dict | None,
    57→    command_name: str = "next",
    58→) -> dict:
    59→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
    60→        queue, items, command=command_name, narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
    61→    )
    62→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    63→    payload["overall_score"] = getattr(scores, "overall", state.get("overall_score"))
    64→    payload["objective_score"] = getattr(
    65→        scores, "objective", state.get("objective_score")
    66→    )
    67→    payload["strict_score"] = getattr(scores, "strict", state.get("strict_score"))
    68→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
    69→        state,
    70→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
    71→    )
    72→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
    73→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
    74→    ]
    75→    return payload

1→"""Action routing and prioritization helpers for narrative actions."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.core import get_dimension_for_detector
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import DETECTOR_TOOLS
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.action_models import ActionItem
    11→
    12→
    13→def _dimension_name(detector: str) -> str:
    14→    """Resolve user-facing dimension name for a detector."""
    15→    dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    16→    return dimension.name if dimension else "Unknown"
    17→
    18→
    19→def _append_reorganize_actions(
    20→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    21→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    22→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    23→) -> None:
    24→    """Append structure/move oriented actions."""
    25→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    26→        if tool_info["action_type"] != "reorganize":
    27→            continue
    28→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
    29→        if count == 0:
    30→            continue
    31→
    32→        guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "restructure with move")
    33→        actions.append(
    34→            {
    35→                "type": "reorganize",
    36→                "detector": detector,
    37→                "count": count,
    38→                "description": f"{count} {detector} issues — {guidance}",
    39→                "command": f"desloppify show {detector} --status open",
    40→                "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
    41→                "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
    42→            }
    43→        )
    44→
    45→
    46→def _build_refactor_entry(
    47→    detector: str,
    48→    tool_info: dict[str, Any],
    49→    count: int,
    50→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    51→) -> ActionItem:
    52→    """Build one refactor/manual action row."""
    53→    guidance = tool_info.get("guidance", "manual fix")
    54→    adjusted_info = {**tool_info, "guidance": guidance}
    55→
    56→    if detector == "subjective_review":
    57→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
    58→        description = (
    59→            f"{count} files need design review — run holistic review to refresh "
    60→            "subjective scores"
    61→        )
    62→    elif detector == "review":
    63→        command = "desloppify show review --status open"
    64→        suffix = "s" if count != 1 else ""
    65→        description = (
    66→            f"{count} review issue{suffix} need investigation — "
    67→            "run `desloppify show review --status open` to see them"
    68→        )
    69→        adjusted_info = {**adjusted_info, "action_type": "refactor"}
    70→    else:
    71→        command = f"desloppify show {detector} --status open"
    72→        description = f"{count} {detector} issues — {guidance}"
    73→
    74→    return {
    75→        "type": adjusted_info["action_type"],
    76→        "detector": detector,
    77→        "count": count,
    78→        "description": description,
    79→        "command": command,
    80→        "impact": round(impact_for(detector, count), 1),
    81→        "dimension": _dimension_name(detector),
    82→    }
    83→
    84→
    85→def _append_refactor_actions(
    86→    actions: list[ActionItem],
    87→    by_detector: dict[str, int],
    88→    impact_for: Callable[[str, int], float],
    89→) -> None:
    90→    """Append refactor/manual actions after auto-fix/reorg buckets."""
    91→    for detector, tool_info in DETECTOR_TOOLS.items():
    92→        if tool_info["action_type"] not in {"refactor", "manual_fix"}:
    93→            continue
    94→        count = by_detector.get(detector, 0)
    95→        if count == 0:
    96→            continue
    97→        actions.append(_build_refactor_entry(detector, tool_info, count, impact_for))
    98→
    99→
   100→def _append_debt_action(actions: list[ActionItem], debt: dict[str, float]) -> None:
   101→    """Append wontfix-debt callout when gap is material."""
   102→    gap = float(debt.get("overall_gap", 0.0) or 0.0)
   103→    if gap <= 2.0:
   104→        return
   105→    actions.append(
   106→        {
   107→            "type": "debt_review",
   108→            "detector": None,
   109→            "description": f"{gap} pts of wontfix debt — review stale decisions",
   110→            "command": "desloppify show --status wontfix",
   111→            "gap": gap,
   112→        }
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→def _assign_priorities(actions: list[ActionItem]) -> list[ActionItem]:
   117→    """Sort and assign sequential priorities."""
   118→    type_order = {
   119→        "issue_queue": 0,
   120→        "auto_fix": 1,
   121→        "reorganize": 2,
   122→        "refactor": 3,
   123→        "manual_fix": 4,
   124→        "debt_review": 5,
   125→    }
   126→    actions.sort(
   127→        key=lambda action: (type_order.get(action["type"], 9), -action.get("impact", 0))
   128→    )
   129→    for index, action in enumerate(actions, start=1):
   130→        action["priority"] = index
   131→    return actions
   132→
   133→
   134→def _cluster_detector(cluster: dict) -> str | None:
   135→    """Extract the primary detector from a cluster."""
   136→    key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   137→    if key:
   138→        parts = key.split("::")
   139→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   140→            return parts[1]
   141→    name = cluster.get("name", "")
   142→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   143→        rest = name[5:]
   144→        return rest.split("-", 1)[0] if "-" in rest else rest
   145→    return None
   146→
   147→
   148→def _annotate_with_clusters(actions: list[ActionItem], clusters: dict | None) -> None:
   149→    """Annotate actions with matching cluster info when clusters exist."""
   150→    if not clusters:
   151→        return
   152→    for action in actions:
   153→        detector = action.get("detector")
   154→        if not detector:
   155→            continue
   156→        matching = [
   157→            name
   158→            for name, cluster in clusters.items()
   159→            if cluster.get("auto") and _cluster_detector(cluster) == detector
   160→        ]
   161→        if matching:
   162→            action["clusters"] = matching
   163→            action["cluster_count"] = len(matching)
   164→            action["command"] = "desloppify next"
   165→            count = action.get("count", 0)
   166→            display = action.get("detector", "unknown")
   167→            action["description"] = (
   168→                f"{count} {display} issues in {len(matching)} cluster(s) — "
   169→                "run `desloppify next`"
   170→            )
   171→
   172→
   173→__all__ = [
   174→    "_annotate_with_clusters",
   175→    "_append_debt_action",
   176→    "_append_refactor_actions",
   177→    "_append_reorganize_actions",
   178→    "_assign_priorities",
   179→    "_build_refactor_entry",
   180→    "_cluster_detector",
   181→    "_dimension_name",
   182→]
   183→

1→"""Follow-up reminder helpers for review lifecycle and reminder decay."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from datetime import UTC
     7→from datetime import datetime as _dt
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import log_best_effort_failure
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative._constants import _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD
    11→from desloppify.intelligence.narrative.reminders_rules_primary import _feedback_base_url
    12→from desloppify.state_io import StateModel
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _review_queue_reminders(
    18→    state: StateModel,
    19→    scoped_issues: dict,
    20→    command: str | None,
    21→    strict_score: float | None,
    22→) -> list[dict]:
    23→    reminders: list[dict] = []
    24→    open_review = [
    25→        issue
    26→        for issue in scoped_issues.values()
    27→        if issue.get("status") == "open" and issue.get("detector") == "review"
    28→    ]
    29→    if open_review:
    30→        uninvestigated = [
    31→            issue
    32→            for issue in open_review
    33→            if not issue.get("detail", {}).get("investigation")
    34→        ]
    35→        if uninvestigated:
    36→            reminders.append(
    37→                {
    38→                    "type": "review_issues_pending",
    39→                    "message": (
    40→                        f"{len(uninvestigated)} review issue(s) need investigation. "
    41→                        "Run `desloppify show review --status open` to see the work queue."
    42→                    ),
    43→                    "command": "desloppify show review --status open",
    44→                }
    45→            )
    46→
    47→    if command == "resolve" and state.get("subjective_assessments"):
    48→        reminders.append(
    49→            {
    50→                "type": "rereview_needed",
    51→                "message": (
    52→                    "Subjective results may be stale after resolve. Re-run "
    53→                    "`desloppify review --prepare` to refresh, or reset with "
    54→                    "`desloppify scan --reset-subjective` before a clean rerun."
    55→                ),
    56→                "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
    57→            }
    58→        )
    59→
    60→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
    61→    if not review_cache.get("files"):
    62→        current_strict = strict_score or 0
    63→        if current_strict >= 80:
    64→            reminders.append(
    65→                {
    66→                    "type": "review_not_run",
    67→                    "message": (
    68→                        "Mechanical checks look good! Run a subjective design review "
    69→                        "to catch issues linters miss: desloppify review --prepare"
    70→                    ),
    71→                    "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
    72→                }
    73→            )
    74→
    75→    return reminders
    76→
    77→
    78→def _has_open_issues(state: StateModel) -> bool:
    79→    """True when any non-suppressed open issues remain in the queue."""
    80→    return any(
    81→        issue.get("status") == "open" and not issue.get("suppressed")
    82→        for issue in (state.get("issues") or {}).values()
    83→    )
    84→
    85→
    86→def _stale_assessment_reminder(state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
    87→    """Nudge when mechanical changes have staled subjective assessments."""
    88→    if _has_open_issues(state):
    89→        return []
    90→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    91→    stale_dims = [
    92→        dim_key
    93→        for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items()
    94→        if isinstance(assessment, dict) and assessment.get("needs_review_refresh")
    95→    ]
    96→    if not stale_dims:
    97→        return []
    98→    dims_arg = ",".join(stale_dims[:10])
    99→    return [
   100→        {
   101→            "type": "stale_assessments",
   102→            "message": (
   103→                f"{len(stale_dims)} subjective dimension"
   104→                f"{'s' if len(stale_dims) != 1 else ''} stale after mechanical changes "
   105→                f"— re-review with: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`"
   106→            ),
   107→            "command": f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}",
   108→        }
   109→    ]
   110→
   111→
   112→def _review_staleness_reminder(state: StateModel, config: dict | None) -> list[dict]:
   113→    review_max_age = (config or {}).get("review_max_age_days", 30)
   114→    review_cache = state.get("review_cache", {})
   115→    if review_max_age <= 0 or not review_cache.get("files"):
   116→        return []
   117→    try:
   118→        oldest_str = min(
   119→            review["reviewed_at"]
   120→            for review in review_cache["files"].values()
   121→            if review.get("reviewed_at")
   122→        )
   123→        oldest = _dt.fromisoformat(oldest_str)
   124→        age_days = (_dt.now(UTC) - oldest).days
   125→    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
   126→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "parse oldest review timestamp", exc)
   127→        return []
   128→    if age_days <= review_max_age:
   129→        return []
   130→    return [
   131→        {
   132→            "type": "review_stale",
   133→            "message": f"Design review is {age_days} days old — run: desloppify review --prepare",
   134→            "command": "desloppify review --prepare",
   135→        }
   136→    ]
   137→
   138→
   139→def _feedback_reminder(
   140→    state: StateModel,
   141→    phase: str,
   142→    command: str | None,
   143→    fp_rates: dict[tuple[str, str], float],
   144→) -> list[dict]:
   145→    scan_count = len(state.get("scan_history", []))
   146→    if scan_count < 2 or command != "scan":
   147→        return []
   148→    high_fp_dets = [
   149→        detector for (detector, _zone), rate in fp_rates.items() if rate > 0.3
   150→    ]
   151→    feedback_url = _feedback_base_url()
   152→    if high_fp_dets:
   153→        nudge_msg = (
   154→            f"Some detectors have high false-positive rates ({', '.join(high_fp_dets)}). "
   155→            f"If patterns are being misclassified, file an issue at {feedback_url} "
   156→            "with the file and expected behavior — it helps calibrate detection for everyone."
   157→        )
   158→    elif phase == "stagnation":
   159→        nudge_msg = (
   160→            "Score has plateaued — if you suspect desloppify is missing patterns "
   161→            f"or not capturing something it should, file an issue at {feedback_url} "
   162→            "describing what you expected. Gaps in detection are a common cause of stagnation."
   163→        )
   164→    else:
   165→        nudge_msg = (
   166→            "If you notice patterns desloppify doesn't detect, false positives, "
   167→            f"or issues that seem off, file an issue at {feedback_url} — include the file "
   168→            "and what you expected. It helps improve the tool."
   169→        )
   170→    return [
   171→        {
   172→            "type": "feedback_nudge",
   173→            "message": nudge_msg,
   174→            "command": None,
   175→        }
   176→    ]
   177→
   178→
   179→_REMINDER_METADATA: dict[str, tuple[int, str]] = {
   180→    "rescan_needed": (1, "high"),
   181→    "ignore_suppression_high": (1, "high"),
   182→    "review_issues_pending": (1, "high"),
   183→    "rereview_needed": (1, "high"),
   184→    "wontfix_growing": (2, "medium"),
   185→    "wontfix_stale": (2, "medium"),
   186→    "stagnant_nudge": (2, "medium"),
   187→    "review_stale": (2, "medium"),
   188→    "stale_assessments": (2, "medium"),
   189→    "auto_fixers_available": (2, "medium"),
   190→    "zone_classification": (2, "medium"),
   191→    "fp_calibration": (2, "medium"),
   192→    "badge_recommendation": (3, "low"),
   193→    "dry_run_first": (3, "low"),
   194→    "feedback_nudge": (3, "low"),
   195→    "review_not_run": (3, "low"),
   196→    "report_scores": (3, "low"),
   197→}
   198→
   199→
   200→def _decorate_reminder_metadata(reminders: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Attach stable priority/severity metadata and sort by priority."""
   202→    decorated: list[dict] = []
   203→    for reminder in reminders:
   204→        reminder_type = str(reminder.get("type", ""))
   205→        key = reminder_type
   206→        if reminder_type.startswith("fp_calibration_"):
   207→            key = "fp_calibration"
   208→        priority, severity = _REMINDER_METADATA.get(key, (3, "low"))
   209→        clone = dict(reminder)
   210→        clone.setdefault("priority", priority)
   211→        clone.setdefault("severity", severity)
   212→        decorated.append(clone)
   213→    decorated.sort(key=lambda item: (int(item.get("priority", 3)), str(item.get("type", ""))))
   214→    return decorated
   215→
   216→
   217→def _report_scores_reminder(command: str | None) -> list[dict]:
   218→    if command != "scan":
   219→        return []
   220→    return [
   221→        {
   222→            "type": "report_scores",
   223→            "message": (
   224→                "ALWAYS share ALL scores with the user: overall, objective, and strict, "
   225→                "plus every dimension score (lenient + strict), including subjective dimensions. "
   226→                "The goal is to maximize strict scores."
   227→            ),
   228→            "command": None,
   229→            "no_decay": True,
   230→        }
   231→    ]
   232→
   233→
   234→def _apply_decay(reminders: list[dict], reminder_history: dict) -> tuple[list[dict], dict]:
   235→    filtered: list[dict] = []
   236→    for reminder in reminders:
   237→        if reminder.get("no_decay"):
   238→            filtered.append(reminder)
   239→            continue
   240→        count = reminder_history.get(reminder["type"], 0)
   241→        if count < _REMINDER_DECAY_THRESHOLD:
   242→            filtered.append(reminder)
   243→
   244→    updated_history = dict(reminder_history)
   245→    for reminder in filtered:
   246→        updated_history[reminder["type"]] = updated_history.get(reminder["type"], 0) + 1
   247→    return filtered, updated_history
   248→
   249→
   250→__all__ = [
   251→    "_apply_decay",
   252→    "_decorate_reminder_metadata",
   253→    "_feedback_reminder",
   254→    "_report_scores_reminder",
   255→    "_review_queue_reminders",
   256→    "_review_staleness_reminder",
   257→    "_stale_assessment_reminder",
   258→]
   259→

| `open_review_ids` | 15-21 | `(state: StateModel) -> set[str]` |
| `current_stale_ids` | 24-41 | `(state: StateModel, *, subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::") -> set[str]` |
| `current_unscored_ids` | 44-79 | `(state: StateModel, *, subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::") -> set[str]` |
| `current_under_target_ids` | 82-109 | `(state: StateModel, *, target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::") -> set[str]` |
| `review_issue_snapshot_hash` | 112-117 | `(state: StateModel) -> str` |
| `compute_new_issue_ids` | 120-124 | `(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]` |
| `is_triage_stale` | 127-139 | `(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> bool` |

**Three Categories Computation**:

1. **`current_unscored_ids()`** (lines 44-79):
   - Primary check: `state["subjective_assessments"][dim_key]["placeholder"]` = True
   - Fallback: `state["dimension_scores"][*]["detectors"]["subjective_assessment"]["placeholder"]` = True
   - Returns `subjective::<slugified_dimension_key>` IDs

2. **`current_stale_ids()`** (lines 24-41):
   - Uses `all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)` iterator
   - Filters for entries where `entry.get("stale")` = True
   - Returns `subjective::<slugified_dimension_key>` IDs

3. **`current_under_target_ids()`** (lines 82-109):
   - Excludes stale and unscored entries first (lines 93-94)
   - Filters for entries where `strict_val < target_strict` (default 80.0)
   - Excludes placeholders and stale entries (line 98)
   - Returns `subjective::<slugified_dimension_key>` IDs not in stale/unscored

---

### 2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py`

**Purpose**: Unified subjective-visibility policy snapshot

**Key Class**:

`SubjectiveVisibility` (lines 25-56) — **frozen dataclass** with:
- `has_objective_backlog: bool` — any open non-subjective issues?
- `objective_count: int` — count of objective issues
- `unscored_ids: frozenset[str]` — `subjective::*` IDs needing initial review
- `stale_ids: frozenset[str]` — `subjective::*` IDs needing re-review
- `under_target_ids: frozenset[str]` — below target, not stale/unscored

**Key Methods**:
- `should_inject_to_plan(fid: str) -> bool` (lines 35-43) — unconditionally inject unscored; conditionally inject stale/under_target if no objective backlog
- `should_evict_from_plan(fid: str) -> bool` (lines 45-51) — never evict unscored; evict stale/under_target if objective backlog exists
- `backlog_blocks_rerun -> bool` (lines 53-56) — preflight check

**Key Function**:

`compute_subjective_visibility()` (lines 79-137):
- Signature: `(state: StateModel, *, target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY, plan: dict | None = None) -> SubjectiveVisibility`
- Calls `current_unscored_ids()`, `current_stale_ids()`, `current_under_target_ids()` from stale.py
- Counts objective issues (non-subjective, open, not suppressed, not evidence-only, in scan scope, not skipped)
- Evidence-only filter: uses detector's `standalone_threshold` from `DETECTORS` registry
- Returns frozen `SubjectiveVisibility` snapshot

**Non-Objective Detectors** (lines 20-22):
```python
NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
})
```

---

### 3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/context.py`

**Purpose**: Shared cycle-phase helpers for queue-sync decisions

**Key Functions**:

| Function | Lines | Signature |
|----------|-------|-----------|
| `has_objective_backlog` | 25-49 | `(state_or_issues: StateModel \| dict, policy: SubjectiveVisibility \| None) -> bool` |
| `is_mid_cycle` | 52-60 | `(plan: PlanModel) -> bool` |

**Cycle Phase Determination** (lines 52-60):
- A cycle is active when `plan["plan_start_scores"]` is set to **real score values**
- The `{"reset": True}` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset) does **NOT** count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan"
- Logic: `bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))`

---

## Existing Infrastructure Summary

**What Already Exists**:

1. **Three-category classification** is fully implemented:
   - `current_stale_ids()` — entries with `stale=True`
   - `current_unscored_ids()` — entries with `placeholder=True`
   - `current_under_target_ids()` — score < target_strict, excluding stale/unscored

2. **SubjectiveVisibility snapshot** (frozen dataclass) encapsulates all three as `frozenset[str]` with guard methods:
   - `should_inject_to_plan()` — respects objective backlog context
   - `should_evict_from_plan()` — evicts lower-priority subjective when backlog arrives
   - `backlog_blocks_rerun` — preflight gate

3. **Cycle phase detection**:
   - `is_mid_cycle(plan)` checks `plan["plan_start_scores"]` presence (excluding reset sentinel)
   - No complementary `cycle_just_completed()` function exists in this file

4. **Objective backlog detection**:
   - `has_objective_backlog()` function accepts pre-computed policy or raw state
   - Filters for `status="open"`, detector not in `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS`, not suppressed
   - Excludes evidence-only issues (below detector's `standalone_threshold`)

    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (

    14→
    15→def scorecard_subjective_entries_for_status(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
    16→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
    17→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    18→        state,
    19→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    20→    )
    21→
    22→
    23→def find_lowest_dimension(
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
    26→) -> str | None:
    27→    """Return the dimension name with the lowest strict score."""
    28→    lowest_name = None
    29→    lowest_score = 101.0
    30→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    31→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    32→        if not ds:
    33→            continue
    34→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    35→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
    36→            lowest_score = strict_val
    37→            lowest_name = dim.name
    38→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
    39→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    40→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
    41→            lowest_score = strict_val
    42→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
    43→    return lowest_name
    44→
    45→
    46→def open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
    47→    """Count open review issues grouped by subjective dimension key."""
    48→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
    49→    if not isinstance(issues, dict):
    50→        return {}
    51→
    52→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    53→    for issue in issues.values():
    54→        if not isinstance(issue, dict):
    55→            continue
    56→        if issue.get("status") != "open" or issue.get("detector") != "review":
    57→            continue
    58→        detail = issue.get("detail", {})
    59→        dimension = ""
    60→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    61→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if not dimension:
    63→            dimension = str(issue.get("dimension", "")).strip()
    64→        if not dimension:
    65→            continue
    66→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
    67→    return counts
    68→
    69→
    70→def render_objective_dimensions(
    71→    dim_scores: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    lowest_name: str | None,
    74→    bar_len: int,
    75→) -> None:
    76→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
    77→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    78→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    79→        if not ds:
    80→            continue
    81→        score_val = ds["score"]
    82→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
    83→        checks = ds["checks"]
    84→
    85→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
    86→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
    87→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
    88→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
    89→        print(
    90→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
    91→        )
    92→
    93→
    94→def render_subjective_dimensions(
    95→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
    96→    *,
    97→    lowest_name: str | None,
    98→    bar_len: int,
    99→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
   100→) -> None:
   101→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   102→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   103→        return
   104→    print(
   105→        colorize(
   106→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   107→            "dim",
   108→        )
   109→    )
   110→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   111→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   112→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   113→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   114→        tier = 4
   115→
   116→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   117→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   118→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   119→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   120→        placeholder_tag = (
   121→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   122→        )
   123→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   124→        cli_keys = [
   125→            str(key).strip()
   126→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   127→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   128→        ]
   129→        if dim_key:
   130→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   131→        elif cli_keys:
   132→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   133→        else:
   134→            issue_count = 0
   135→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   136→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   137→        print(
   138→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   139→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   140→        )

1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→

## User-Facing Messaging Surfaces for Subjective Dimensions, Deferred Work, and Under-Target Scores

### 1. **Next Command Rendering** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/render_support.py`)

**Lines 22-26:**
```python
_CLUSTER_NAME_LABELS = {
    "auto/initial-review": "Initial subjective review",
    "auto/stale-review": "Stale subjective review",
    "auto/under-target-review": "Optional re-review",
}
```

**What it says:** The `auto/under-target-review` cluster is labeled as "Optional re-review" to the user.

**Queue policy conflict:** This directly contradicts the queue policy logic. The label suggests it's optional ("Optional re-review"), but per the subjective visibility policy, under-target dimensions are:
- Only injected when there's no objective backlog (see `subjective.py` line 41-42)
- Evicted when objective backlog appears (line 49-50)
- Treated as automatically deferrable work

The "Optional" label implies the user can freely skip it, but the policy actually **defers it automatically** when mechanical work exists. This messaging could mislead users into thinking they have discretion to skip something that the queue has already deferred.

**Line 224:**
```python
print(colorize("  (Skip items only when explicitly requested.)", "dim"))
```

**What it says:** When queue has >5 items, displays "(Skip items only when explicitly requested.)"

**Queue policy conflict:** Contextually neutral, but could be misread to suggest the tool wants items in the queue completed sequentially. However, under-target-review clusters are specifically designed to be optionally skippable, which this message doesn't acknowledge.

---

### 2. **Next Command Rendering Details** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next/render.py`)

**Lines 42-75 (`_render_subjective_dimension` function):**
```python
def _render_subjective_dimension(item: dict, *, explain: bool) -> None:
    """Render a subjective dimension re-review item."""
    detail = item.get("detail", {})
    subjective_score = float(
        detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    )
    print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
    print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
    print(
        colorize(
            f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
            "cyan",
        )
    )
    print(colorize(
        "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
        "dim",
    ))
```

**What it says:** 
- For subjective dimension items in the queue, it prompts action: `desloppify review --prepare`
- Warning note: "re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered."

**Queue policy conflict:** This is rendering a subjective item that may be an under-target dimension that should be **deferred** when objective work exists. The action ("run re-review") is presented unconditionally, without noting that this item might be temporarily deferred. Users seeing this in the queue may not realize it's been queued despite existing objective work, and that re-reviews will be pushed back once objective issues appear.

---

- The user cannot simply "re-review to improve" if objective work is blocking subjective queue visibility

**Queue policy conflict:** This assumes the user can run subjective review. But if the user then has a scan that creates objective work, the queue policy will defer any under-target subjective dimensions. The message doesn't prepare the user for this automatic deferral behavior.

**Lines 100-109:**
```python
return [
    {
        "type": "stale_assessments",
        "message": (
            f"{len(stale_dims)} subjective dimension"
            f"{'s' if len(stale_dims) != 1 else ''} stale after mechanical changes "
            f"— re-review with: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}`"
        ),
        "command": f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg}",
    }
]
```

**What it says:** After mechanical changes, stale dimensions need "re-review with: `desloppify review --prepare`"

**Queue policy concern (context-dependent):** This reminder only appears `if not _has_open_issues(state)` (line 88), so it's gated. But the message still assumes immediate actionability.

**Lines 222-227:**
```python
{
    "type": "report_scores",
    "message": (
        "ALWAYS share ALL scores with the user: overall, objective, and strict, "
        "plus every dimension score (lenient + strict), including subjective dimensions. "
        "The goal is to maximize strict scores."
    ),
```

**What it says:** Instructs the tool to "maximize strict scores" including subjective.

**Queue policy alignment issue:** This is a meta-instruction to the tool's reasoning, but it doesn't account for the **automatic deferral** of under-target subjective work. The instruction could lead the tool to suggest re-reviews when the queue policy has deferred them.

---

### 9. **Plan Override Skip** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_skip.py`)

**Lines 114-138:**
```python
"  --confirm requires --reason to describe why this is being deferred."
...
required_phrases = ("i have actually reflected", "not deferring")
...
'I am not deferring this for lazy reasons."'
```

**What it says:** When skipping items, the tool requires the user to attest they've "reflected" and are "not deferring" for "lazy reasons."

**Queue policy conflict:** This messaging emphasizes intentional user deferral decisions. But the queue policy **automatically defers** under-target subjective dimensions without user intervention. This creates a mental model mismatch: the tool treats user-initiated skip as requiring reflection, but auto-defers subjective work silently. Users may feel the tool is forcing them to justify what is actually automatic behavior for subjective items.

---

### Summary of Contradictions

| Surface | File & Lines | Messaging | Queue Policy Conflict |
|---------|--------------|-----------|----------------------|
| Cluster label | `render_support.py:25` | "Optional re-review" | Implies discretion, but auto-defers when objective work exists |
| Focus suggestion | `render.py:214-216` | "re-review to improve" | No acknowledgment of automatic deferral |
| Dimension impact | `dimensions.py:58` | "re-review to improve" | Assumes immediate actionability |
| Status focus | `status/render.py:216` | "re-review to improve" | Doesn't mention deferral when objective work present |
| Scan text | `scan/reporting/text.py:56` | "Address review issues → re-review" | Generic, doesn't explain auto-deferral |
| Action routing | `action_engine_routing.py:59` | "files need design review" | Hides the auto-deferral of under-target items |
| Resolve reminder | `reminders_rules_followup.py:52` | "Re-run review" | Doesn't account for auto-deferral after objective work |
| High-score reminder | `reminders_rules_followup.py:68` | "Run a subjective design review" | No caveat about auto-deferral if objective work follows |
| Report scores meta | `reminders_rules_followup.py:225` | "maximize strict scores" | Doesn't account for auto-deferral logic |
| Skip attestation | `plan/override_skip.py:119` | "not deferring for lazy reasons" | Confuses intentional skip with automatic deferral |

The core issue is that **none of these surfaces explain the queue policy behavior of automatically deferring under-target subjective dimensions when objective work exists**. They all present subjective work as immediately actionable ("fix this," "re-review to improve") without acknowledging the policy's deferral mechanism.

> DEVELOPER

Design the implementation for fixing the subjective queue policy so that under_target and stale are no longer conflated.

## Current State

### SubjectiveVisibility (engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py)
- Frozen dataclass with `unscored_ids`, `stale_ids`, `under_target_ids` as separate frozensets
- `should_inject_to_plan(fid)`: unconditionally injects unscored; conditionally injects stale/under_target only if no objective backlog
- `should_evict_from_plan(fid)`: never evicts unscored; evicts stale/under_target if objective backlog exists

### sync_stale_dimensions() (engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py)
- Line 220: `injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids` — unions them
- Line 244: `should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed` — same gate for both
- When objective backlog exists, both are evicted

### auto_cluster_sync.py sync_subjective_clusters()
- Line 204-206: `deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(set(stale_candidate_ids) | set(under_target_queue_ids))` — unions for deferral
- Lines 251-288: defer_meta tracks combined set, escalation applies to both

### defer_policy.py
- Escalation after 3 scans or 7 days applies to the combined stale+under_target set

## Desired Behavior
- **unscored**: always surface at cycle start (unchanged)
- **under_target**: always visible in queue (appended after objective items), never deferred
- **stale**: deferred when objective backlog exists (unchanged)

## Design Constraints
- Minimize changes — the classification infrastructure already exists
- Don't add new abstraction layers (no "shared helper" object)
- Keep the escalation mechanism for stale-only
- Update messaging to not suggest immediate action for deferred stale items

## Key Questions to Answer
1. Exact changes needed in SubjectiveVisibility methods
2. Exact changes needed in sync_stale_dimensions() 
3. Exact changes needed in auto_cluster_sync.py
4. Which messaging surfaces actually need updating vs which are already gated correctly
5. What about the cluster type auto/under-target-review — should it be created mid-cycle even with objective backlog?

1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain, and evict them
     9→  again when objective backlog returns.
    10→
    11→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, QueueSyncResult
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    22→
    23→from .context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle

    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# ID helpers
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    32→
    33→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    34→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    35→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    36→    """
    37→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    38→        state,
    39→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→
    43→def current_under_target_ids(
    44→    state: StateModel,
    45→    *,
    46→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    47→) -> set[str]:
    48→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    49→
    50→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    51→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    52→    """
    53→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    54→        state,
    55→        target_strict=target_strict,
    56→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→# Helpers
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    65→    order: list[str],
    66→    *,
    67→    keep_ids: set[str],
    68→    pruned: list[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    71→    to_remove = [
    72→        fid for fid in order
    73→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    74→        and fid not in keep_ids
    75→    ]
    76→    for fid in to_remove:
    77→        order.remove(fid)
    78→        pruned.append(fid)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    82→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
    83→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    85→        return set()
    86→    return {
    87→        str(fid)
    88→        for fid in skipped
    89→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    90→    }
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    *,
    96→    skipped_ids: set[str],
    97→    pruned: list[str],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return
   102→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   103→    for fid in to_remove:
   104→        order.remove(fid)
   105→        pruned.append(fid)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   109→    order: list[str],
   110→    *,
   111→    inject_ids: set[str],
   112→    injected: list[str],
   113→) -> None:
   114→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   115→
   116→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   117→    """
   118→    existing = set(order)
   119→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   120→        if sid not in existing:
   121→            order.append(sid)
   122→            injected.append(sid)
   123→
   124→
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→
   129→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    state: StateModel,
   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   134→
   135→    1. **Prune** — always remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order``
   136→       that are no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs —
   137→       that is ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).  Pruning runs even
   138→       mid-cycle to clean up items that were incorrectly injected by older
   139→       code that lacked the mid-cycle guard.
   140→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   141→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —
   142→       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.
   143→    """
   144→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   145→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   146→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   147→
   148→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   149→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   150→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   151→    )
   152→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   153→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   154→
   155→    if not mid_cycle:
   156→        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a
   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   161→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   162→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   163→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   164→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   165→
   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   167→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   168→
   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   170→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   171→    # Mid-cycle: prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty) since they
   172→    # shouldn't be in the queue mid-cycle at all.
   173→    if mid_cycle:
   174→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=set(), pruned=result.pruned)
   175→    else:
   176→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   179→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.
   180→    if not mid_cycle:
   181→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   182→            order,
   183→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   184→            injected=result.injected,
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   195→    plan: PlanModel,
   196→    state: StateModel,
   197→    *,
   198→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   199→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   200→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   204→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   205→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   206→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   207→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   208→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   209→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   210→       b. A cycle just completed.
   211→       Never reorders existing items.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   215→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   216→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   217→    )
   218→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   219→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   220→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   221→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   222→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   223→
   224→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   225→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   226→
   227→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   228→
   229→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   230→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   231→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   232→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   233→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   234→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   235→    if mid_cycle:
   236→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   237→    else:
   238→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   239→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   240→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   241→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   242→
   243→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   244→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   245→
   246→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   247→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "current_under_target_ids",
   254→    "current_unscored_ids",
   255→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→

    41→def _subjective_state_sets(

    43→    *,
    44→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    45→    target_strict: float,
    46→) -> tuple[set, set, set]:

    50→        stale_ids = policy.stale_ids

    61→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: dict) -> set[str]:
    62→    """Return skipped subjective IDs so auto-clusters don't re-queue them."""
    63→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    64→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    65→        return set()
    66→    return {
    67→        str(fid)
    68→        for fid in skipped
    69→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    70→    }
    71→

    73→def _clear_subjective_defer_meta(plan: dict) -> None:
    74→    plan.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY, None)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes
   114→

   116→def sync_subjective_clusters(
   117→    plan: dict,
   118→    state: StateModel,
   119→    issues: dict,
   120→    clusters: dict,
   121→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],

   129→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   130→    changes = 0

   132→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])

   134→        overlap = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_subjective_ids]
   135→        for fid in overlap:
   136→            order.remove(fid)
   137→            changes += 1
   138→
   139→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   140→        fid
   141→        for fid in order
   142→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   143→    )
   144→
   145→    stale_state_ids, under_target_ids, unscored_state_ids = _subjective_state_sets(
   146→        state, policy=policy, target_strict=target_strict
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   150→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   151→    )
   152→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(

   154→        for fid in all_subjective_ids
   155→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   156→    )
   157→
   158→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   159→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   160→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   161→        description = (
   162→            f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   163→        )
   164→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   165→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(

   171→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   172→            description=description,
   173→            action=action,
   174→            now=now,
   175→        )
   176→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   177→
   178→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   179→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   180→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   181→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   182→        action = "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   183→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(

   195→
   196→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(
   197→        fid for fid in under_target_ids if fid not in skipped_subjective_ids
   198→    )
   199→    stale_candidate_ids = sorted(

   221→
   222→    has_objective_items = _has_objective_backlog(issues, policy)
   223→
   224→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   225→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)

   229→            f"dimensions under target score"

   232→        sync_result = _sync_auto_cluster(

   234→            clusters,
   235→            existing_by_key,
   236→            [REDACTED],
   237→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   238→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   239→            description=description,
   240→            action=action,
   241→            now=now,
   242→            optional=True,
   243→        )
   244→        changes += int(sync_result.changed)
   245→
   246→        existing_order = set(order)
   247→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   248→            if fid not in existing_order:
   249→                order.append(fid)
   250→
   251→    if has_objective_items and not cycle_just_completed:

   262→            escalated = should_escalate_defer_state(

   265→                options=DeferEscalationOptions(
   266→                    deferred_ids_field=_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_IDS_FIELD,
   267→                    now=now,
   268→                ),
   269→            )
   270→            if escalated:
   271→                defer_state[_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY] = list(deferred_subjective_ids)
   272→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   273→                changes += _promote_subjective_ids(order, deferred_subjective_ids)
   274→            else:
   275→                defer_state.pop(_SUBJECTIVE_FORCE_IDS_KEY, None)
   276→                plan[_SUBJECTIVE_DEFER_META_KEY] = defer_state
   277→                objective_evict = [

   287→    else:

1→"""Shared defer-tracking helpers for triage and subjective rerun fairness."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
     9→
    10→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS = 3
    11→DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS = 7
    12→
    13→
    14→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    15→class DeferUpdateOptions:
    16→    """Options for durable defer-state updates."""
    17→
    18→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    19→    now: str | None = None
    20→
    21→
    22→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    23→class DeferEscalationOptions:
    24→    """Options for defer-state escalation checks."""
    25→
    26→    deferred_ids_field: str = "deferred_ids"
    27→    now: str | None = None
    28→    min_scans: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS
    29→    min_days: int = DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS
    30→
    31→
    32→def _coerce_non_negative_int(value: object, *, default: int = 0) -> int:
    33→    try:
    34→        parsed = int(value)
    35→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    36→        return default
    37→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else default
    38→
    39→
    40→def _scan_count(state: StateModel) -> int:
    41→    return _coerce_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count"), default=0)
    42→
    43→
    44→def _normalize_timestamp(raw: object) -> str | None:
    45→    if not isinstance(raw, str):
    46→        return None
    47→    value = raw.strip()
    48→    return value or None
    49→
    50→
    51→def _parse_timestamp(raw: object) -> datetime | None:
    52→    normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    53→    if not normalized:
    54→        return None
    55→    try:
    56→        return datetime.fromisoformat(normalized.replace("Z", "+00:00")).astimezone(UTC)
    57→    except ValueError:
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→def _current_timestamp(
    62→    state: StateModel,
    63→    *,
    64→    now: str | None = None,
    65→) -> str:
    66→    for raw in (state.get("last_scan"), now):
    67→        normalized = _normalize_timestamp(raw)
    68→        if normalized:
    69→            return normalized
    70→    return utc_now()
    71→
    72→
    73→def _normalized_ids(ids: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    74→    return sorted({str(issue_id).strip() for issue_id in ids if str(issue_id).strip()})
    75→
    76→
    77→def _deferred_ids(
    78→    defer_state: dict[str, object],
    79→    *,
    80→    deferred_ids_field: str,
    81→) -> list[str]:
    82→    raw = defer_state.get(deferred_ids_field)
    83→    if not isinstance(raw, list):
    84→        return []
    85→    return sorted(
    86→        {
    87→            str(issue_id).strip()
    88→            for issue_id in raw
    89→            if str(issue_id).strip()
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→def update_defer_state(
    95→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
    96→    *,
    97→    state: StateModel,
    98→    deferred_ids: set[str],
    99→    options: DeferUpdateOptions | None = None,
   100→) -> dict[str, object]:
   101→    """Return updated durable defer metadata for the current deferred ID set."""
   102→    resolved_options = options or DeferUpdateOptions()
   103→    previous = defer_state if isinstance(defer_state, dict) else {}
   104→    current_ids = _normalized_ids(deferred_ids)
   105→    previous_ids = _deferred_ids(
   106→        previous,
   107→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   108→    )
   109→    scan_count = _scan_count(state)
   110→    timestamp = _current_timestamp(state, now=resolved_options.now)
   111→
   112→    updated = dict(previous)
   113→    updated[resolved_options.deferred_ids_field] = current_ids
   114→    updated["last_deferred_scan"] = scan_count
   115→    updated["last_deferred_at"] = timestamp
   116→
   117→    same_ids = bool(current_ids) and current_ids == previous_ids
   118→    if same_ids:
   119→        defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("defer_count"), default=1)
   120→        prior_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(previous.get("last_deferred_scan"), default=-1)
   121→        if prior_scan != scan_count:
   122→            defer_count += 1
   123→        branch_updates = {
   124→            "defer_count": max(1, defer_count),
   125→            "first_deferred_scan": _coerce_non_negative_int(
   126→            previous.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   127→            default=scan_count,
   128→            ),
   129→            "first_deferred_at": str(previous.get("first_deferred_at") or timestamp),
   130→        }
   131→    else:
   132→        branch_updates = {
   133→            "defer_count": 1 if current_ids else 0,
   134→            "first_deferred_scan": scan_count,
   135→            "first_deferred_at": timestamp,
   136→        }
   137→    updated.update(branch_updates)
   138→    return updated
   139→
   140→
   141→def should_escalate_defer_state(
   142→    defer_state: dict[str, object] | None,
   143→    *,
   144→    state: StateModel,
   145→    options: DeferEscalationOptions | None = None,
   146→) -> bool:
   147→    """Return True when defer metadata crosses configured escalation bounds."""
   148→    if not isinstance(defer_state, dict):
   149→        return False
   150→    resolved_options = options or DeferEscalationOptions()
   151→
   152→    deferred_ids = _deferred_ids(
   153→        defer_state,
   154→        deferred_ids_field=resolved_options.deferred_ids_field,
   155→    )
   156→    if not deferred_ids:
   157→        return False
   158→
   159→    min_scans = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_scans))
   160→    min_days = max(1, int(resolved_options.min_days))
   161→
   162→    defer_count = _coerce_non_negative_int(defer_state.get("defer_count"), default=0)
   163→    if defer_count >= min_scans:
   164→        return True
   165→
   166→    first_scan = _coerce_non_negative_int(
   167→        defer_state.get("first_deferred_scan"),
   168→        default=-1,
   169→    )
   170→    current_scan = _scan_count(state)
   171→    if first_scan >= 0 and current_scan - first_scan + 1 >= min_scans:
   172→        return True
   173→
   174→    first_deferred_at = _parse_timestamp(defer_state.get("first_deferred_at"))
   175→    current_at = _parse_timestamp(state.get("last_scan")) or _parse_timestamp(
   176→        resolved_options.now
   177→    )
   178→    if first_deferred_at is None or current_at is None:
   179→        return False
   180→    return (current_at - first_deferred_at).total_seconds() >= min_days * 86400
   181→
   182→
   183→__all__ = [
   184→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_DAYS",
   185→    "DEFER_ESCALATE_AFTER_SCANS",
   186→    "DeferEscalationOptions",
   187→    "DeferUpdateOptions",
   188→    "should_escalate_defer_state",
   189→    "update_defer_state",
   190→]
   191→

1→"""Shared helpers for queue-sync decisions.
     2→
     3→Every sync module (``sync_triage``, ``sync_dimensions``, ``sync_workflow``,
     4→``auto_cluster_sync``) needs to answer two questions:
     5→
     6→1. **Is there an objective backlog?**  (Should we defer optional items?)
     7→2. **Are we mid-cycle?**  (Has a queue cycle started and not yet completed?)
     8→
     9→This module provides a single definition of each so the logic stays
    10→consistent and changes propagate everywhere at once.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    17→    NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS as _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import (
    20→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→def has_objective_backlog(
    26→    state_or_issues: StateModel | dict,
    27→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None,
    28→) -> bool:
    29→    """Return whether an objective backlog exists (open non-subjective issues).
    30→
    31→    Prefers the pre-computed *policy* snapshot when available.
    32→    Falls back to scanning *state_or_issues* directly — accepts either a
    33→    full ``StateModel`` (looks up ``issues``) or a raw issues dict.
    34→    """
    35→    if policy is not None:
    36→        return policy.has_objective_backlog
    37→
    38→    # Accept either full state payload (`{"issues": ...}`) or raw issues dict.
    39→    issues = state_or_issues
    40→    if isinstance(state_or_issues, dict):
    41→        maybe_issues = state_or_issues.get("issues")
    42→        if isinstance(maybe_issues, dict):
    43→            issues = maybe_issues
    44→    return any(
    45→        f.get("status") == "open"
    46→        and f.get("detector") not in _NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    47→        and not f.get("suppressed")
    48→        for f in issues.values()
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def is_mid_cycle(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    53→    """True when a queue cycle is in progress.
    54→
    55→    A cycle is active when ``plan_start_scores`` is set to real score
    56→    values.  The ``{"reset": True}`` sentinel (set by lifecycle reset)
    57→    does NOT count as mid-cycle — it means "seed real scores on next scan".
    58→    """
    59→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
    60→    return bool(scores) and not (isinstance(scores, dict) and scores.get("reset"))
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["has_objective_backlog", "is_mid_cycle"]
    64→
