---

## Implementation Plan: Deconflate under_target and stale in Subjective Queue Policy

### Problem Summary

Currently, `under_target` and `stale` subjective dimension IDs are treated identically throughout the queue policy pipeline: they are unioned together, deferred together, escalated together, and evicted together when an objective backlog exists. The desired behavior is:

- **under_target**: always visible in the queue (appended after objective items), never deferred
- **stale**: deferred when objective backlog exists (current behavior, unchanged)

### Step 1: SubjectiveVisibility (policy/subjective.py)

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py`

Two methods need changes:

**`should_inject_to_plan()`** (lines 35-43): Change the `under_target_ids` branch to return `True` unconditionally (like `unscored_ids`), instead of gating on `not self.has_objective_backlog`:

```python
# Line 41-42: change from
if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    return not self.has_objective_backlog
# to
if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    return True  # always visible, appended after objective items
```

**`should_evict_from_plan()`** (lines 45-51): Split the combined `stale_ids or under_target_ids` check on line 49. `under_target` should never be evicted:

```python
# Line 49-50: change from
if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    return self.has_objective_backlog
# to
if fid in self.stale_ids:
    return self.has_objective_backlog
if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    return False  # never evict under-target
```

### Step 2: sync_stale_dimensions() (sync/dimensions.py)

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py`

The function `sync_stale_dimensions` (line 194) currently unions `stale_ids | under_target_ids` into `injectable_ids` and applies a single injection gate. Split them:

**Line 220**: Remove the union. Create separate sets:
```python
stale_injectable = stale_ids - skipped_ids
under_target_injectable = under_target_ids - skipped_ids
```

**Lines 235-241 (keep_ids computation)**: Under-target IDs must always be kept in the queue. Change the logic so that `under_target_injectable` is always in `keep_ids`, while `stale_injectable` is only kept when no objective backlog (or cycle just completed):

```python
if mid_cycle:
    keep_ids = under_target_injectable | (stale_injectable if not objective_backlog else set())
else:
    keep_ids = unscored_ids | under_target_injectable
    if not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed:
        keep_ids |= stale_injectable
```

**Lines 244-247 (injection)**: Split into two injection calls. Under-target always injects; stale only injects when `should_inject` (no backlog or cycle just completed):

```python
# under_target: always inject
if under_target_injectable:
    _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=under_target_injectable, injected=result.injected)

# stale: conditional injection
should_inject_stale = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
if should_inject_stale and stale_injectable:
    _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=stale_injectable, injected=result.injected)
```

### Step 3: auto_cluster_sync.py sync_subjective_clusters()

**File**: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py`

Three changes in `sync_subjective_clusters()`:

**(a) Lines 204-206 — deferred_subjective_ids**: Only stale candidates should be deferred. Under-target IDs must not be included:

```python
deferred_subjective_ids = sorted(stale_candidate_ids)  # no longer includes under_target
```

**(b) Lines 224-250 — under-target cluster creation**: Remove the `if not has_objective_items` gate on line 224. The `auto/under-target-review` cluster should be created regardless of objective backlog, and under-target IDs should always be appended to the queue:

```python
# Remove: if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
# Replace with:
if len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
    # ... existing cluster creation code (lines 225-244) stays the same ...
    # ... existing order-append code (lines 246-249) stays the same ...
```

**(c) Lines 251-288 — deferral/eviction block**: Since `deferred_subjective_ids` now only contains stale IDs, the eviction on lines 277-284 must only evict stale IDs (not under_target). Currently line 280 evicts anything in `under_target_ids or stale_state_ids`. Change to only evict stale:

```python
# Line 277-283: change from
objective_evict = [
    fid for fid in order
    if fid in under_target_ids or fid in stale_state_ids
]
# to
objective_evict = [
    fid for fid in order
    if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
]
```

Also on line 273, `_promote_subjective_ids` is called with `deferred_subjective_ids` which now only contains stale IDs -- this is correct for escalation behavior.

### Step 4: Messaging Surfaces

**Question 4 answer**: Most messaging surfaces are already correctly gated or don't need changes:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/reporting/dimensions.py` -- This renders `[stale]` tags based on `subjective_meta.get("stale")`, which is a per-dimension property. Under-target dimensions won't have the stale flag. **No change needed.**

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status/render.py` -- Similar, renders stale labels based on per-entry `stale` flag. **No change needed.**

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/synthetic_workflow.py` `build_deferred_disposition_item()` -- This reads from `_temporary_skipped_ids(plan)` which is general skip tracking. Since under-target items will no longer be deferred/skipped, they won't appear here. **No change needed** (the fix is upstream in not deferring them).

- The docstring of `sync_stale_dimensions()` (lines 201-212) should be updated to reflect that under-target items are always injected while stale items are conditional.

### Step 5: Question 5 — auto/under-target-review cluster mid-cycle

Yes, the `auto/under-target-review` cluster should be created mid-cycle even with objective backlog. The whole point of this change is that under-target dimensions are always visible. Remove the `not has_objective_items` guard as described in Step 3(b). The cluster's `optional=True` flag (line 242) already signals it is lower priority, which is the correct UX.

### Step 6: Tests to Update

Several test files assert the current (conflated) behavior:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_subjective_policy.py` -- `test_inject_under_target_only_when_drained` (line 200) and the eviction tests need updating to reflect that under_target is always injected and never evicted.

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py` -- Tests that verify under_target eviction when objective backlog exists.

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_auto_cluster_sync_direct.py` and `test_auto_cluster_subjective_lifecycle.py` -- Tests covering the deferred union and eviction behavior.

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions_cycle_and_queue_order.py` -- Cycle boundary tests that may assert combined stale+under_target behavior.

### Implementation Order

1. `subjective.py` -- Change `should_inject_to_plan` and `should_evict_from_plan` (smallest, most fundamental change)
2. `dimensions.py` -- Split the union in `sync_stale_dimensions`
3. `auto_cluster_sync.py` -- Split deferred set, remove under-target gate, fix eviction filter
4. Update tests to match new semantics
5. Run full test suite to catch any downstream breakage

### Critical Files for Implementation
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py` - Core policy: must split under_target from stale in should_inject and should_evict
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py` - Queue sync: must split the injectable_ids union and use separate injection gates
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster_sync.py` - Cluster sync: must exclude under_target from deferral, remove objective gate on cluster creation, fix eviction filter
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_subjective_policy.py` - Primary test file for the policy dataclass behavior
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/plan/test_auto_cluster_subjective_lifecycle.py` - Integration tests for the full subjective cluster lifecycle

```
1→"""Subjective-score helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→
     9→from .render_support import scorecard_subjective as _scorecard_subjective_impl
    10→
    11→
    12→def _scorecard_subjective(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    13→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    14→    return _scorecard_subjective_impl(state, dim_scores)
    15→
    16→
    17→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    18→    state: dict,
    19→    dim_scores: dict,
    20→    *,
    21→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    22→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    23→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    24→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    25→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    26→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    27→            continue
    28→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    29→        if strict_val < threshold:
    30→            low.append(
    31→                (
    32→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    33→                    strict_val,
    34→                    int(entry.get("failing", 0)),
    35→                )
    36→            )
    37→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    38→    return low
    39→
    40→
    41→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "_scorecard_subjective"]
    42→
```

```
    17→    SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES,
    18→    ScoreSnapshot,
    19→    WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
    20→    append_log_entry,
    21→    auto_cluster_issues,
    22→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    23→    is_mid_cycle,
    24→    load_plan,
    25→    mark_postflight_scan_completed,
    26→    reconcile_plan_after_scan,
    27→    save_plan,
    28→    sync_communicate_score_needed,
    29→    sync_create_plan_needed,
    30→    sync_stale_dimensions,
    31→    sync_triage_needed,
    32→    sync_unscored_dimensions,
    33→)
    34→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_deferred_disposition_item
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    40→    """Clear all cycle state when --force-rescan is used.
    41→
    42→    Force-rescan means "start over" — the old cycle's triage stages,
    43→    workflow items, subjective dimensions, plan-start scores, and triage
    44→    metadata are all stale and must be removed.
    45→    """
    46→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    47→    synthetic = [item for item in order if any(item.startswith(p) for p in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)]
    48→    if not synthetic and not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    49→        return False
    50→    for item in synthetic:
    51→        order.remove(item)
    52→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    53→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
    54→    plan.pop("scan_count_at_plan_start", None)
    55→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    56→    if isinstance(meta, dict):
    57→        meta.pop("triage_recommended", None)
    58→    count = len(synthetic)
    59→    if count:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(
    62→                f"  Plan: force-rescan — removed {count} synthetic item(s) "
    63→                f"and reset cycle state.",
    64→                "yellow",
    65→            )
    66→        )
    67→    return True
    68→
    69→
    70→def _plan_has_user_content(plan: dict[str, object]) -> bool:
    71→    """Return True when the living plan has any user-managed queue metadata."""
    72→    return bool(
    73→        plan.get("queue_order")
    74→        or plan.get("overrides")
    75→        or plan.get("clusters")
    76→        or plan.get("skipped")
    77→    )
    78→
    79→
    80→def _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, reconcile_fn) -> bool:
    81→    """Apply standard post-scan plan reconciliation when user content exists."""
    82→    if not _plan_has_user_content(plan):
    83→        return False
    84→    recon = reconcile_fn(plan, state)
    85→    if recon.resurfaced:
    86→        print(
    87→            colorize(
    88→                f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
    89→                "cyan",
    90→            )
    91→        )
    92→    return bool(recon.changes)
    93→
    94→
    95→def _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
    96→    """Sync unscored subjective dimensions into the plan queue."""
    97→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
    98→    if sync.resurfaced:
    99→        print(
   100→            colorize(
   101→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.resurfaced)} skipped subjective dimension(s) resurfaced — never reviewed.",
   102→                "yellow",
   103→            )
   104→        )
   105→    if sync.injected:
   106→        print(
   107→            colorize(
   108→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} unscored subjective dimension(s) queued for initial review.",
   109→                "cyan",
   110→            )
   111→        )
   112→    return bool(sync.changes)
   113→
   114→
   115→def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel, sync_fn) -> bool:
   116→    """Sync stale subjective dimensions (prune refreshed + inject stale) in plan queue."""
   117→    sync = sync_fn(plan, state)
   118→    if sync.pruned:
   119→        print(
   120→            colorize(
   121→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   122→                "cyan",
   123→            )
   124→        )
   125→    if sync.injected:
   126→        print(
   127→            colorize(
   128→                f"  Plan: {len(sync.injected)} subjective dimension(s) queued for review.",
   129→                "cyan",
   130→            )
   131→        )
   132→    return bool(sync.changes)

   134→
   135→def _sync_auto_clusters(
   136→    plan: dict[str, object],
   137→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   138→    *,
   139→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   140→    policy=None,
   141→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   142→) -> bool:
   143→    """Regenerate automatic task clusters after scan merge."""
   144→    return bool(auto_cluster_issues(
   145→        plan, state,
   146→        target_strict=target_strict,
   147→        policy=policy,
   148→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   149→    ))

   151→
   152→def _seed_plan_start_scores(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   153→    """Set plan_start_scores when beginning a new queue cycle."""
   154→    existing = plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   155→    if existing and not isinstance(existing, dict):
   156→        return False
   157→    # Seed when empty OR when it's the reset sentinel ({"reset": True})
   158→    if existing and not existing.get("reset"):
   159→        return False
   160→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   161→    if scores.strict is None:
   162→        return False
   163→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {
   164→        "strict": scores.strict,
   165→        "overall": scores.overall,
   166→        "objective": scores.objective,
   167→        "verified": scores.verified,
   168→    }
   169→    # New cycle — clear the communicate-score sentinel so it can fire again.
   170→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   171→    # Record scan count at cycle start so gates can detect whether a new scan ran
   172→    plan["scan_count_at_plan_start"] = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   173→    return True
   174→
   175→
   176→def _has_objective_cycle(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    plan: dict[str, object],
   179→) -> bool | None:
   180→    """Return True when objective queue work exists and a cycle baseline should freeze."""
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   183→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   184→        )
   185→
   186→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   187→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   188→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "compute queue breakdown for plan-start seeding", exc)
   189→        return None
   190→    return breakdown.objective_actionable > 0
   191→
   192→
   193→def _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(
   194→    state: state_mod.StateModel, plan: dict[str, object]
   195→) -> bool:
   196→    """Clear plan-start score snapshot once the queue is fully drained."""
   197→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
   198→        return False
   199→    # Don't clear while communicate-score is pending — the rebaseline just
   200→    # set plan_start_scores and the user hasn't seen the update yet.
   201→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   202→        return False
   203→
   204→    try:
   205→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import (
   206→            ScoreDisplayMode,
   207→            plan_aware_queue_breakdown,
   208→            score_display_mode,
   209→        )
   210→
   211→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   212→        frozen_strict = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→        queue_empty = score_display_mode(breakdown, frozen_strict) is not ScoreDisplayMode.FROZEN
   214→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   215→        log_best_effort_failure(logger, "run post-scan plan reconciliation", exc)
   216→        return False
   217→    if not queue_empty:
   218→        return False
   219→    state["_plan_start_scores_for_reveal"] = dict(plan["plan_start_scores"])
   220→    plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   221→    # Clear the cycle sentinel so communicate-score can be injected
   222→    # in the next cycle.
   223→    plan.pop("previous_plan_start_scores", None)
   224→    return True
   225→
   226→
   227→def _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(
   228→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   229→    plan: dict[str, object],
   230→) -> bool:
   231→    """Record that the scan stage completed for the current empty-queue boundary."""
   232→    if build_deferred_disposition_item(plan) is not None:
   233→        return False
   234→    objective_cycle = _has_objective_cycle(state, plan)
   235→    if objective_cycle is not False:
   236→        return False
   237→    return mark_postflight_scan_completed(
   238→        plan,
   239→        scan_count=int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0),
   240→    )
   241→
   242→
   243→def _subjective_policy_context(
   244→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   245→    plan: dict[str, object],
   246→) -> tuple[float, object, bool]:
   247→    from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
   248→
   249→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config)
   250→    policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
   251→        runtime.state,
   252→        target_strict=target_strict,
   253→        plan=plan,
   254→    )
   255→    cycle_just_completed = not plan.get("plan_start_scores")
   256→    return target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed
   257→
   258→
   259→def _sync_unscored_and_log(
   260→    plan: dict[str, object],
   261→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   262→) -> bool:
   263→    changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   264→    if changed:
   265→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   266→    return changed
   267→
   268→
   269→def _sync_stale_and_log(
   270→    plan: dict[str, object],
   271→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   272→    *,
   273→    policy,
   274→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   275→) -> bool:
   276→    changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(
   277→        plan,
   278→        state,
   279→        lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(
   280→            p,
   281→            s,
   282→            policy=policy,
   283→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   284→        ),
   285→    )
   286→    if changed:
   287→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   288→    return changed
   289→
   290→
   291→def _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   292→    plan: dict[str, object],
   293→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   294→    *,
   295→    target_strict: float,
   296→    policy,
   297→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   298→) -> bool:
   299→    changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
   300→        plan,
   301→        state,
   302→        target_strict=target_strict,
   303→        policy=policy,
   304→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   305→    )
   306→    if changed:
   307→        append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system", detail={"changes": True})
   308→    return changed
   309→
   310→
   311→def _sync_triage_and_log(
   312→    plan: dict[str, object],
   313→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   314→    *,
   315→    policy=None,
   316→) -> bool:
   317→    triage_sync = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   318→    if triage_sync.deferred:
   319→        meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   320→        if meta.get("triage_recommended"):
   321→            print(
   322→                colorize(
   323→                    "  Plan: review issues changed — triage recommended after current work.",
   324→                    "dim",
   325→                )
   326→            )
   327→        return False
   328→    if not triage_sync.changes:
   329→        return False
   330→    if triage_sync.injected:
   331→        print(
   332→            colorize(
   333→                "  Plan: planning mode needed — review issues changed since last triage.",
   334→                "cyan",
   335→            )
   336→        )
   337→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_triage", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   338→    return True
   339→
   340→
   341→def _sync_communicate_score_and_log(
   342→    plan: dict[str, object],
   343→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   344→    *,
   345→    policy,
   346→) -> bool:
   347→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   348→    current_scores = ScoreSnapshot(
   349→        strict=snapshot.strict,
   350→        overall=snapshot.overall,
   351→        objective=snapshot.objective,
   352→        verified=snapshot.verified,
   353→    )
   354→    communicate_sync = sync_communicate_score_needed(
   355→        plan, state, policy=policy, current_scores=current_scores,
   356→    )
   357→    if not communicate_sync.changes:
   358→        return False
   359→    append_log_entry(
   360→        plan,
   361→        "sync_communicate_score",
   362→        actor="system",
   363→        detail={"injected": True},
   364→    )
   365→    return True
   366→
   367→
   368→def _sync_create_plan_and_log(
   369→    plan: dict[str, object],
   370→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   371→    *,
   372→    policy,
   373→) -> bool:
   374→    create_plan_sync = sync_create_plan_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   375→    if not create_plan_sync.changes:
   376→        return False
   377→    if create_plan_sync.injected:
   378→        print(
   379→            colorize(

   383→        )
   384→        append_log_entry(plan, "sync_create_plan", actor="system", detail={"injected": True})
   385→    return True
   386→
   387→
   388→def _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(
   389→    plan: dict[str, object],
   390→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   391→) -> bool:
   392→    seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, state)
   393→    if seeded:
   394→        append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   395→        return True
   396→    # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   397→    # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   398→    cleared = _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(state, plan)
   399→    if cleared:
   400→        append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system", detail={})
   401→    return cleared
   402→
   403→
   404→def _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(
   405→    plan: dict[str, object],
   406→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   407→) -> bool:
   408→    changed = _mark_postflight_scan_completed_if_ready(state, plan)
   409→    if changed:
   410→        append_log_entry(
   411→            plan,
   412→            "complete_postflight_scan",
   413→            actor="system",
   414→            detail={"scan_count": int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)},
   415→        )
   416→    return changed
   417→
   418→
   419→def _sync_post_scan_without_policy(
   420→    *,
   421→    plan: dict[str, object],
   422→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   423→) -> bool:
   424→    """Run post-scan sync steps that do not require subjective policy context."""
   425→    dirty = False
   426→    if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   427→        dirty = True
   428→    if _sync_unscored_and_log(plan, state):
   429→        dirty = True
   430→    return dirty
   431→
   432→
   433→def _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan: dict[str, object], state: state_mod.StateModel) -> bool:
   434→    """Return True when a plan cycle is active and queue items remain.
   435→
   436→    Extends ``is_mid_cycle`` (which checks ``plan_start_scores``) with an
   437→    additional queue-items guard — even if a cycle is nominally active, we
   438→    only skip destructive operations when work actually remains.
   439→
   440→    Mid-cycle scans (via --force-rescan or PHASE_TRANSITION gate) must NOT
   441→    regenerate clusters or inject triage stages — doing so wipes triage
   442→    state and reorders the queue, undoing prioritisation work.
   443→    """
   444→    if not is_mid_cycle(plan):
   445→        return False
   446→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   447→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   448→    return any(item not in skipped for item in order)
   449→
   450→
   451→def _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   452→    *,
   453→    plan: dict[str, object],
   454→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   455→    target_strict: float,
   456→    policy,
   457→    cycle_just_completed: bool,
   458→    force_rescan: bool = False,
   459→) -> bool:
   460→    """Run post-scan sync steps that require policy/cycle context.
   461→
   462→    When running mid-cycle (plan_start_scores set, queue non-empty) or
   463→    via --force-rescan, skip auto-clustering and triage injection.  These
   464→    steps regenerate queue structure and issue IDs, which wipes triage
   465→    state and reorders the queue.  They only run at cycle boundaries
   466→    (pre-flight / post-flight).
   467→    """
   468→    dirty = False
   469→    mid_cycle = _is_mid_cycle_scan(plan, state) or force_rescan
   470→
   471→    if _sync_stale_and_log(
   472→        plan,
   473→        state,
   474→        policy=policy,
   475→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   476→    ):
   477→        dirty = True
   478→    if not mid_cycle:
   479→        if _sync_auto_clusters_and_log(
   480→            plan,
   481→            state,
   482→            target_strict=target_strict,
   483→            policy=policy,
   484→            cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   485→        ):
   486→            dirty = True
   487→    else:
   488→        print(
   489→            colorize(
   490→                "  Plan: mid-cycle scan — skipping cluster regeneration to "
   491→                "preserve queue state.",
   492→                "dim",
   493→            )
   494→        )
   495→    if _sync_communicate_score_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   496→        dirty = True
   497→    if _sync_create_plan_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   498→        dirty = True
   499→    if _sync_triage_and_log(plan, state, policy=policy):
   500→        dirty = True
   501→    if not force_rescan:
   502→        if _sync_plan_start_scores_and_log(plan, state):
   503→            dirty = True
   504→        if _sync_postflight_scan_completion_and_log(plan, state):
   505→            dirty = True
   506→    return dirty
   507→
   508→
   509→def reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: ScanRuntime) -> None:
   510→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   511→    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   512→    try:
   513→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   514→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   515→        logger.warning("Plan reconciliation skipped (load failed): %s", exc)
   516→        return
   517→
   518→    force_rescan = getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False)
   519→
   520→    # Force-rescan: clear all cycle state first.  The user explicitly chose
   521→    # to start over, so triage stages, workflow items, subjective dimensions,
   522→    # and plan-start scores from the old cycle are all stale.
   523→    dirty = _reset_cycle_for_force_rescan(plan) if force_rescan else False
   524→
   525→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_without_policy(plan=plan, state=runtime.state) or dirty
   526→
   527→    # Policy must be computed after the without-policy steps, which mutate
   528→    # plan (reconcile/prune) and state (unscored sync) before policy reads them.
   529→    target_strict, policy, cycle_just_completed = _subjective_policy_context(
   530→        runtime,
   531→        plan,
   532→    )
   533→    dirty = _sync_post_scan_with_policy(
   534→        plan=plan,
   535→        state=runtime.state,
   536→        target_strict=target_strict,
   537→        policy=policy,
   538→        cycle_just_completed=cycle_just_completed,
   539→        force_rescan=getattr(runtime, "force_rescan", False),
   540→    ) or dirty
   541→
   542→    if dirty:
   543→        try:
   544→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   545→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   546→            logger.warning("Plan reconciliation save failed: %s", exc)
   547→
```

```
1→"""Unified subjective-visibility policy.
     2→
     3→A single frozen dataclass computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``has_objective_items`` / ``objective_count`` computations in
     5→``stale_dimensions`` and ``auto_cluster``.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass
    11→
    12→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    13→from desloppify.base.enums import Status
    14→from desloppify.base.registry import DETECTORS
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import issue_in_scan_scope
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.helpers import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→
    19→# Detectors whose issues are NOT objective mechanical work.
    20→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    21→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    22→})
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class SubjectiveVisibility:
    27→    """Immutable snapshot of the subjective-vs-objective balance."""
    28→
    29→    has_objective_backlog: bool  # any open non-subjective issues?
    30→    objective_count: int  # how many
    31→    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing initial review
    32→    stale_ids: frozenset[str]  # subjective::* IDs needing re-review
    33→    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]  # below target, not stale/unscored
    34→
    35→    def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    36→        """Should this subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
    37→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    38→            return True  # unconditional
    39→        if fid in self.stale_ids:
    40→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    41→        if fid in self.under_target_ids:
    42→            return not self.has_objective_backlog
    43→        return False
    44→
    45→    def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    46→        """Should this subjective ID be removed from plan queue_order?"""
    47→        if fid in self.unscored_ids:
    48→            return False  # never evict unscored
    49→        if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
    50→            return self.has_objective_backlog
    51→        return False
    52→
    53→    @property
    54→    def backlog_blocks_rerun(self) -> bool:
    55→        """Preflight: should reruns be blocked?"""
    56→        return self.has_objective_backlog
    57→
    58→
    59→def _is_evidence_only(issue: dict) -> bool:
    60→    """Return True if the issue is below its detector's standalone threshold."""
    61→    detector = issue.get("detector", "")
    62→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
    63→    if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
    64→        threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
    65→        issue_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(issue.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
    66→        if issue_rank > threshold_rank:
    67→            return True
    68→    return False
    69→
    70→
    71→class _ScanPathFromStatePolicy:
    72→    """Sentinel type: resolve scan_path from state."""
    73→
    74→
    75→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY = _ScanPathFromStatePolicy()
    76→ScanPathPolicyOption = str | None | _ScanPathFromStatePolicy
    77→
    78→
    79→def compute_subjective_visibility(
    80→    state: StateModel,
    81→    *,
    82→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    83→    scan_path: ScanPathPolicyOption = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE_POLICY,
    84→    plan: dict | None = None,
    85→) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    86→    """Build the policy snapshot from current state.
    87→
    88→    *scan_path* defaults to ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers don't need to
    89→    thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` to override, or ``None``
    90→    to disable scope filtering.  When *plan* is set, issues whose IDs
    91→    appear in ``plan["skipped"]`` are excluded.
    92→
    93→    Imports policy helpers from ``stale_policy`` so this module remains
    94→    side-effect free and cycle-safe.
    95→    """
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.stale import (
    97→        current_stale_ids,
    98→        current_under_target_ids,
    99→        current_unscored_ids,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→    resolved_scan_path: str | None = (
   103→        state.get("scan_path")
   104→        if isinstance(scan_path, _ScanPathFromStatePolicy)
   105→        else scan_path
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    issues = state.get("issues", {})
   109→    skipped_ids = set((plan or {}).get("skipped", {}).keys())
   110→
   111→    # Count open, non-suppressed, objective issues.
   112→    # Evidence-only issues (below standalone confidence threshold) are
   113→    # excluded — they still affect scores but are not actionable queue items.
   114→    # Issues outside scan_path and plan-skipped issues are also excluded
   115→    # so the policy matches what the user actually sees in the queue.
   116→    objective_count = sum(
   117→        1
   118→        for issue_id, issue in issues.items()
   119→        if issue.get("status") == Status.OPEN
   120→        and issue.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   121→        and not issue.get("suppressed")
   122→        and not _is_evidence_only(issue)
   123→        and issue_in_scan_scope(str(issue.get("file", "")), resolved_scan_path)
   124→        and issue_id not in skipped_ids
   125→    )
   126→
   127→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   128→    stale = current_stale_ids(state)
   129→    under_target = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    return SubjectiveVisibility(
   132→        has_objective_backlog=objective_count > 0,
   133→        objective_count=objective_count,
   134→        unscored_ids=frozenset(unscored),
   135→        stale_ids=frozenset(stale),
   136→        under_target_ids=frozenset(under_target),
   137→    )
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = [
   141→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   142→    "SubjectiveVisibility",
   143→    "compute_subjective_visibility",
   144→]
   145→
```

```
   117→def _raise_on_partial_skip(diff: dict, *, allow_partial: bool) -> None:
   118→    """Refuse import when payload skips issues and partial imports are disabled."""
   119→    if diff.get("skipped", 0) <= 0 or allow_partial:
   120→        return
   121→    details_lines: list[str] = []
   122→    for detail in diff.get("skipped_details", []):
   123→        reasons = "; ".join(detail.get("missing", []))
   124→        details_lines.append(
   125→            f"  #{detail.get('index', '?')} ({detail.get('identifier', '<none>')}): {reasons}"
   126→        )
   127→    msg = "import produced skipped issue(s); refusing partial import."
   128→    if details_lines:
   129→        msg += "\n" + "\n".join(details_lines)
   130→    msg += "\nFix the payload and retry, or pass --allow-partial to override."
   131→    raise CommandError(msg, exit_code=1)

   135→    *,

   188→def _guard_pending_import_scores_match(

   196→    """Refuse durable imports that do not match the queued score-import batch."""

   201→        return
   202→    plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan(plan_path)
   203→    if plan_queue_mod.WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   204→        return
   205→    pending_meta = plan_queue_mod.pending_import_scores_meta(plan, state)
   206→    matches, reason = plan_queue_mod.import_scores_meta_matches(
   207→        pending_meta,
   208→        import_file=import_file,
   209→        import_payload=issues_data,
   210→    )
   211→    if matches:
   212→        return
   213→    expected_file = ""
   214→    if isinstance(pending_meta, dict):
   215→        expected_file = str(pending_meta.get("import_file", "")).strip()
   216→    raise CommandError(
   217→        "Refusing durable score import: the pending "
   218→        "`workflow::import-scores` task is bound to a different review batch.\n"
   219→        f"  - {reason}\n"
   220→        + (f"Expected queued import file: {expected_file}\n" if expected_file else "")
   221→        + "Use the exact file shown by `desloppify next`, or clear the stale workflow item first.",
   222→        exit_code=1,

   226→def _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state: dict) -> bool:
   227→    """Return True when state has scan-backed scorecard context.
   228→
   229→    Review imports can run against minimal/synthetic states (for example test
   230→    fixtures or pre-scan workflows). Refreshing the badge from those states can
   231→    overwrite scorecard.png with a misleading partial card.
   232→    """
   233→    if not state.get("last_scan"):
   234→        return False
   235→
   236→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores")
   237→    if not isinstance(dim_scores, dict) or not dim_scores:
   238→        return False
   239→
   240→    for data in dim_scores.values():
   241→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   242→            continue
   243→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   244→        if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
   245→            continue
   246→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
   247→            continue
   248→        if int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0) <= 0:
   249→            continue
   250→        return True
   251→    return False
   252→

   261→    if not assessment_policy.assessments_present or not assessment_policy.trusted:
   262→        return False
   263→    if not _has_refreshable_scorecard_context(state):
   264→        return False
   265→    emit_scorecard_badge(
   266→        SimpleNamespace(no_badge=False, badge_path=None),
   267→        config or {},
   268→        state,
   269→    )
   270→    return True
```

```
1→"""Post-import plan sync for review importing."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→from pathlib import Path

     7→import desloppify.engine.plan_queue as plan_queue_mod
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_issue_id
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review.importing.flags import imported_assessment_keys
    11→from desloppify.base.config import target_strict_score_from_config
    12→from desloppify.base.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    13→from desloppify.base.output.terminal import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine.plan_triage import (

    20→def _print_review_import_sync(
    21→    state: dict,
    22→    result: plan_queue_mod.ReviewImportSyncResult,
    23→    *,
    24→    workflow_injected: bool,
    25→) -> None:
    26→    """Print summary of plan changes after review import sync."""
    27→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    28→    stale_pruned = result.stale_pruned_from_queue
    29→    print()
    30→    if new_ids:
    31→        print(colorize(
    32→            f"  Plan updated: {len(new_ids)} new review issue(s) added to queue.",
    33→            "bold",
    34→        ))
    35→        issues = state.get("issues", {})
    36→        for finding_id in sorted(new_ids)[:10]:
    37→            finding = issues.get(finding_id, {})
    38→            print(f"    * [{short_issue_id(finding_id)}] {finding.get('summary', '')}")
    39→        if len(new_ids) > 10:
    40→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 10} more", "dim"))
    41→    if stale_pruned:
    42→        print(colorize(
    43→            f"  Plan updated: {len(stale_pruned)} stale review issue(s) removed from queue.",
    44→            "bold",
    45→        ))
    46→    print()
    47→    print(colorize(
    48→        "  Review queue sync completed. Workflow follow-up may be front-loaded.",
    49→        "dim",
    50→    ))
    51→    print()
    52→    print(colorize("  View execution queue:  desloppify plan queue", "dim"))
    53→    print(colorize("  View newest first:     desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
    54→    print(colorize("  View broader backlog:  desloppify backlog", "dim"))
    55→    print()
    56→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP:", "yellow"))
    57→    print(colorize("    Run:    desloppify next", "yellow"))
    58→    if result.triage_injected and not workflow_injected:
    59→        print(colorize(f"    Codex:  {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CODEX}", "dim"))
    60→        print(colorize(f"    Claude: {TRIAGE_CMD_RUN_STAGES_CLAUDE}", "dim"))
    61→        print(colorize("    Manual dashboard: desloppify plan triage", "dim"))
    62→    print(colorize(
    63→        "  (Follow the queue in order; score communication and planning come before triage.)",
    64→        "dim",
    65→    ))
    66→
    67→
    68→def sync_plan_after_import(
    69→    state: dict,
    70→    diff: dict,
    71→    assessment_mode: str,
    72→    *,
    73→    state_file: str | Path | None = None,
    74→    config: dict | None = None,
    75→    import_file: str | None = None,
    76→    import_payload: dict | None = None,
    77→) -> None:
    78→    """Apply issue/workflow syncs after import in one load/save cycle."""
    79→    try:
    80→        plan_path = None
    81→        target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config)
    82→        if state_file is not None:
    83→            plan_path = plan_queue_mod.plan_path_for_state(Path(state_file))
    84→        if not plan_queue_mod.has_living_plan(plan_path):
    85→            return
    86→
    87→        plan = plan_queue_mod.load_plan(plan_path)
    88→        dirty = False
    89→        workflow_injected_ids: list[str] = []
    90→        policy = plan_queue_mod.compute_subjective_visibility(
    91→            state,
    92→            target_strict=target_strict,
    93→            plan=plan,
    94→        )
    95→
    96→        snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    97→        current_scores = plan_queue_mod.ScoreSnapshot(
    98→            strict=snapshot.strict,
    99→            overall=snapshot.overall,
   100→            objective=snapshot.objective,
   101→            verified=snapshot.verified,
   102→        )
   103→        trusted_score_import = assessment_mode in {"trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
   104→
   105→        communicate_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_communicate_score_needed(
   106→            plan,
   107→            state,
   108→            policy=policy,
   109→            scores_just_imported=trusted_score_import,
   110→            current_scores=current_scores,
   111→        )
   112→        if communicate_result.changes:
   113→            dirty = True
   114→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::communicate-score")
   115→
   116→        has_review_issue_delta = (
   117→            int(diff.get("new", 0) or 0) > 0
   118→            or int(diff.get("reopened", 0) or 0) > 0
   119→            or int(diff.get("auto_resolved", 0) or 0) > 0
   120→        )
   121→        assessment_keys = (
   122→            imported_assessment_keys(import_payload)
   123→            if isinstance(import_payload, dict)
   124→            else set()
   125→        )
   126→        import_result = None
   127→        covered_ids = [
   128→            f"subjective::{dim_key}"
   129→            for dim_key in sorted(assessment_keys)
   130→        ]
   131→        stale_sync_result = None
   132→        auto_cluster_changes = 0
   133→
   134→        injected_parts: list[str] = []
   135→        if communicate_result.changes:
   136→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::communicate-score`")
   137→
   138→        import_scores_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_import_scores_needed(
   139→            plan,
   140→            state,
   141→            assessment_mode=assessment_mode,
   142→            import_file=import_file,
   143→            import_payload=import_payload,
   144→        )
   145→        if import_scores_result.changes:
   146→            dirty = True
   147→            if import_scores_result.injected:
   148→                workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::import-scores")
   149→                injected_parts.append("`workflow::import-scores`")
   150→
   151→        create_plan_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_create_plan_needed(
   152→            plan,
   153→            state,
   154→            policy=policy,
   155→        )
   156→        if create_plan_result.changes:
   157→            dirty = True
   158→            workflow_injected_ids.append("workflow::create-plan")
   159→            injected_parts.append("`workflow::create-plan`")
   160→
   161→        if has_review_issue_delta:
   162→            import_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_plan_after_review_import(
   163→                plan,
   164→                state,
   165→                policy=policy,
   166→            )
   167→            if import_result is not None:
   168→                dirty = True
   169→
   170→        if has_review_issue_delta or assessment_keys:
   171→            # Assessment-bearing imports can change subjective queue semantics
   172→            # even when they add no review findings. Keep queue_order aligned
   173→            # first, then rebuild the derived auto-clusters from that queue.
   174→            stale_sync_result = plan_queue_mod.sync_stale_dimensions(
   175→                plan,
   176→                state,
   177→                policy=policy,
   178→            )
   179→            if stale_sync_result.changes:
   180→                dirty = True
   181→
   182→            auto_cluster_changes = int(plan_queue_mod.auto_cluster_issues(
   183→                plan,
   184→                state,
   185→                target_strict=target_strict,
   186→                policy=policy,
   187→            ))
   188→            if auto_cluster_changes:
   189→                dirty = True
   190→
   191→        if dirty:
   192→            if communicate_result.changes:
   193→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   194→                    plan,
   195→                    "sync_communicate_score",
   196→                    actor="system",
   197→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   198→                )
   199→            if import_scores_result.changes:
   200→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   201→                    plan,
   202→                    "sync_import_scores",
   203→                    actor="system",
   204→                    detail={
   205→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   206→                        "injected": bool(import_scores_result.injected),
   207→                        "pruned": list(import_scores_result.pruned),
   208→                    },
   209→                )
   210→            if create_plan_result.changes:
   211→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   212→                    plan,
   213→                    "sync_create_plan",
   214→                    actor="system",
   215→                    detail={"trigger": "review_import", "injected": True},
   216→                )
   217→            if import_result is not None or workflow_injected_ids or covered_ids:
   218→                plan_queue_mod.append_log_entry(
   219→                    plan,
   220→                    "review_import_sync",
   221→                    actor="system",
   222→                    detail={
   223→                        "trigger": "review_import",
   224→                        "new_ids": sorted(import_result.new_ids) if import_result is not None else [],
   225→                        "added_to_queue": (
   226→                            import_result.added_to_queue if import_result is not None else []
   227→                        ),
   228→                        "workflow_injected_ids": workflow_injected_ids,
   229→                        "triage_injected": (
   230→                            import_result.triage_injected if import_result is not None else False
   231→                        ),
   232→                        "triage_injected_ids": (
   233→                            import_result.triage_injected_ids if import_result is not None else []
   234→                        ),
   235→                        "triage_deferred": (
   236→                            import_result.triage_deferred if import_result is not None else False
   237→                        ),
   238→                        "diff_new": diff.get("new", 0),
   239→                        "diff_reopened": diff.get("reopened", 0),
   240→                        "diff_auto_resolved": diff.get("auto_resolved", 0),
   241→                        "stale_pruned_from_queue": (
   242→                            import_result.stale_pruned_from_queue if import_result is not None else []
   243→                        ),
   244→                        "covered_subjective": covered_ids,
   245→                        "stale_sync_injected": (
   246→                            sorted(stale_sync_result.injected)
   247→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   248→                        ),
   249→                        "stale_sync_pruned": (
   250→                            sorted(stale_sync_result.pruned)
   251→                            if stale_sync_result is not None else []
   252→                        ),
   253→                        "auto_cluster_changes": auto_cluster_changes,
   254→                    },
   255→                )
   256→            plan_queue_mod.save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   257→
   258→        if import_result is not None:
   259→            _print_review_import_sync(
   260→                state,
   261→                import_result,
   262→                workflow_injected=bool(workflow_injected_ids),
   263→            )
   264→        if injected_parts:
   265→            print(colorize(
   266→                f"  Plan: {' and '.join(injected_parts)} queued. Run `desloppify next`.",
   267→                "cyan",
   268→            ))
   269→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   270→        print(
   271→            colorize(
   272→                f"  Note: skipped plan sync after review import ({exc}).",
   273→                "dim",
   274→            )
   275→        )
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = ["sync_plan_after_import"]
   279→
```

```
1→"""Plan sync helpers for review-import flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.promoted_ids import prune_promoted_ids
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.sync.triage import (
    10→    compute_new_issue_ids,
    11→    compute_open_issue_ids,
    12→    sync_triage_needed,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    15→
    16→
    17→@dataclass
    18→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
    19→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
    20→
    21→    new_ids: set[str]
    22→    added_to_queue: list[str]
    23→    triage_injected: bool
    24→    stale_pruned_from_queue: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    25→    triage_injected_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    26→    triage_deferred: bool = False
    27→
    28→
    29→def _has_triage_baseline(plan: PlanModel) -> bool:
    30→    """Return True when triage has recorded at least one baseline issue ID."""
    31→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
    32→    triaged_ids = meta.get("triaged_ids", [])
    33→    return bool(triaged_ids)
    34→
    35→
    36→def _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
    37→    plan: PlanModel,
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    open_review_ids: set[str] | None = None,
    41→) -> set[str]:
    42→    """Return review IDs that should be synced into queue_order after import.
    43→
    44→    No triage baseline yet: include all currently-open review IDs so the
    45→    first import cannot drop follow-up work.
    46→    Existing triage baseline: include only IDs that are new since triage.
    47→    """
    48→    if _has_triage_baseline(plan):
    49→        return compute_new_issue_ids(plan, state)
    50→    return set(open_review_ids) if open_review_ids is not None else compute_open_issue_ids(state)
    51→
    52→
    53→def _is_review_queue_id(issue_id: str) -> bool:
    54→    """Return True for queue IDs representing review/concerns issues."""
    55→    return issue_id.startswith("review::") or issue_id.startswith("concerns::")
    56→
    57→
    58→def _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
    59→    plan: PlanModel,
    60→    *,
    61→    live_open_review_ids: set[str],
    62→) -> list[str]:
    63→    """Remove stale review IDs from queue-related plan containers.
    64→
    65→    Review imports can auto-resolve prior review IDs. These IDs must be removed
    66→    from queue_order (and linked queue metadata) or the queue can drift from
    67→    state reality.
    68→    """
    69→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    70→    stale_ids = sorted(
    71→        {
    72→            issue_id
    73→            for issue_id in order
    74→            if _is_review_queue_id(issue_id) and issue_id not in live_open_review_ids
    75→        }
    76→    )
    77→    if not stale_ids:
    78→        return []
    79→
    80→    stale_set = set(stale_ids)
    81→    order[:] = [issue_id for issue_id in order if issue_id not in stale_set]
    82→    prune_promoted_ids(plan, stale_set)
    83→
    84→    deferred = plan.get("deferred")
    85→    if isinstance(deferred, list):
    86→        plan["deferred"] = [issue_id for issue_id in deferred if issue_id not in stale_set]
    87→
    88→    skipped = plan.get("skipped")
    89→    if isinstance(skipped, dict):
    90→        for issue_id in stale_set:
    91→            skipped.pop(issue_id, None)
    92→
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        issue_ids = cluster.get("issue_ids")
    95→        if not isinstance(issue_ids, list):
    96→            continue
    97→        cluster["issue_ids"] = [
    98→            issue_id for issue_id in issue_ids if issue_id not in stale_set
    99→        ]
   100→
   101→    return stale_ids
   102→
   103→
   104→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→    *,
   108→    policy=None,
   109→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   110→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   111→
   112→    Appends new issue IDs to queue_order, prunes stale review IDs from queue
   113→    containers, and injects triage stages if needed (respects mid-cycle guard
   114→    — defers when objective work remains). Returns ``None`` when no queue
   115→    changes are required.
   116→    """
   117→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   118→    open_review_ids = compute_open_issue_ids(state)
   119→    stale_pruned_from_queue = _prune_stale_review_ids_from_plan(
   120→        plan,
   121→        live_open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   122→    )
   123→    new_ids = _review_issue_ids_for_import_sync(
   124→        plan,
   125→        state,
   126→        open_review_ids=open_review_ids,
   127→    )
   128→    if not new_ids and not stale_pruned_from_queue:
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    # Add new issue IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   132→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   133→    existing = set(order)
   134→    added: list[str] = []
   135→    for issue_id in sorted(new_ids):
   136→        if issue_id not in existing:
   137→            order.append(issue_id)
   138→            added.append(issue_id)
   139→
   140→    # Inject triage stages if needed (policy enables mid-cycle guard)
   141→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state, policy=policy)
   142→    triage_injected_ids = list(getattr(triage_result, "injected", []) or [])
   143→    triage_injected = bool(triage_injected_ids)
   144→    triage_deferred = bool(triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "deferred", False))
   145→
   146→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   147→        new_ids=new_ids,
   148→        added_to_queue=added,
   149→        stale_pruned_from_queue=stale_pruned_from_queue,
   150→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   151→        triage_injected_ids=triage_injected_ids,
   152→        triage_deferred=triage_deferred,
   153→    )
   154→
   155→
   156→__all__ = ["ReviewImportSyncResult", "sync_plan_after_review_import"]
   157→
```

```
1→"""Canonical stale/unscored subjective policy helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import hashlib
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
     8→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import all_subjective_entries
    11→
    12→_REVIEW_DETECTORS = ("review", "concerns")
    13→
    14→
    15→def open_review_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    16→    """Return IDs of open review/concerns issues from state."""
    17→    return {
    18→        fid
    19→        for fid, f in state.get("issues", {}).items()
    20→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in _REVIEW_DETECTORS
    21→    }
    22→
    23→
    24→def current_stale_ids(
    25→    state: StateModel,
    26→    *,
    27→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    28→) -> set[str]:
    29→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    30→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    31→    if not dim_scores:
    32→        return set()
    33→
    34→    stale: set[str] = set()
    35→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    36→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    37→            continue
    38→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    39→        if dim_key:
    40→            stale.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    41→    return stale
    42→
    43→
    44→def current_unscored_ids(
    45→    state: StateModel,
    46→    *,
    47→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    48→) -> set[str]:
    49→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored."""
    50→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    51→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
    52→        unscored: set[str] = set()
    53→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    54→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    55→                continue
    56→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    57→                continue
    58→            if dim_key:
    59→                unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    60→        return unscored
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    unscored = set()
    67→    for data in dim_scores.values():
    68→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
    69→            continue
    70→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    71→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
    72→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    73→            continue
    74→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
    75→            continue
    76→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
    77→        if dim_key:
    78→            unscored.add(f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    79→    return unscored
    80→
    81→
    82→def current_under_target_ids(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    86→    subjective_prefix: str = "subjective::",
    87→) -> set[str]:
    88→    """Return under-target subjective IDs that are neither stale nor unscored."""
    89→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    90→    if not dim_scores:
    91→        return set()
    92→
    93→    stale_ids = current_stale_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    94→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state, subjective_prefix=subjective_prefix)
    95→
    96→    under_target: set[str] = set()
    97→    for entry in all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    98→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
    99→            continue
   100→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   101→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   102→            continue
   103→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   104→        if not dim_key:
   105→            continue
   106→        item_id = f"{subjective_prefix}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   107→        if item_id not in stale_ids and item_id not in unscored_ids:
   108→            under_target.add(item_id)
   109→    return under_target
   110→
   111→
   112→def review_issue_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   113→    """Hash open review/concerns issue IDs to detect triage-relevant changes."""
   114→    review_ids = sorted(open_review_ids(state))
   115→    if not review_ids:
   116→        return ""
   117→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   118→
   119→
   120→def compute_new_issue_ids(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   121→    """Return open review/concerns IDs that appeared since the last triage."""
   122→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   123→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   124→    return open_review_ids(state) - triaged if triaged else set()
   125→
   126→
   127→def is_triage_stale(
   128→    plan: dict,
   129→    state: StateModel,
   130→) -> bool:
   131→    """Return True when genuinely new review issues appeared since last triage.
   132→
   133→    In-progress triage (confirmed stages + stage IDs in queue) is NOT
   134→    considered stale — the lifecycle filter in the work queue already
   135→    forces triage stages to the front.
   136→    """
   137→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   138→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   139→    return bool(open_review_ids(state) - triaged_ids)
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "compute_new_issue_ids",
   144→    "current_stale_ids",
   145→    "current_under_target_ids",
   146→    "current_unscored_ids",
   147→    "is_triage_stale",
   148→    "open_review_ids",
   149→    "review_issue_snapshot_hash",
   150→]
   151→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — append never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *back* of the queue unconditionally.
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain, and evict them
     9→  again when objective backlog returns.
    10→
    11→Invariant: new items are always appended — sync never reorders existing queue.
    12→"""
    13→
    14→from __future__ import annotations
    15→
    16→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE
    17→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy import stale as stale_policy_mod
    18→from desloppify.engine._plan.constants import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, QueueSyncResult
    19→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    20→from desloppify.engine._plan.policy.subjective import SubjectiveVisibility
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

    23→from .context import has_objective_backlog, is_mid_cycle

    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# ID helpers
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    32→
    33→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    34→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    35→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
    36→    """
    37→    return stale_policy_mod.current_unscored_ids(
    38→        state,
    39→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    40→    )
    41→
    42→
    43→def current_under_target_ids(
    44→    state: StateModel,
    45→    *,
    46→    target_strict: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
    47→) -> set[str]:
    48→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
    49→
    50→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
    51→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
    52→    """
    53→    return stale_policy_mod.current_under_target_ids(
    54→        state,
    55→        target_strict=target_strict,
    56→        subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    57→    )
    58→
    59→
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→# Helpers
    62→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→
    64→def _prune_subjective_ids(
    65→    order: list[str],
    66→    *,
    67→    keep_ids: set[str],
    68→    pruned: list[str],
    69→) -> None:
    70→    """Remove subjective IDs from *order* that are not in *keep_ids*, appending removed to *pruned*."""
    71→    to_remove = [
    72→        fid for fid in order
    73→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    74→        and fid not in keep_ids
    75→    ]
    76→    for fid in to_remove:
    77→        order.remove(fid)
    78→        pruned.append(fid)
    79→
    80→
    81→def _skipped_subjective_ids(plan: PlanModel) -> set[str]:
    82→    """Return subjective IDs that are currently skipped in the plan."""
    83→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    84→    if not isinstance(skipped, dict):
    85→        return set()
    86→    return {
    87→        str(fid)
    88→        for fid in skipped
    89→        if isinstance(fid, str) and fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
    90→    }
    91→
    92→
    93→def _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(
    94→    order: list[str],
    95→    *,
    96→    skipped_ids: set[str],
    97→    pruned: list[str],
    98→) -> None:
    99→    """Repair invalid overlap by removing skipped subjective IDs from queue order."""
   100→    if not skipped_ids:
   101→        return
   102→    to_remove = [fid for fid in order if fid in skipped_ids]
   103→    for fid in to_remove:
   104→        order.remove(fid)
   105→        pruned.append(fid)
   106→
   107→
   108→def _inject_subjective_ids(
   109→    order: list[str],
   110→    *,
   111→    inject_ids: set[str],
   112→    injected: list[str],
   113→) -> None:
   114→    """Inject subjective IDs into *order* if not already present.
   115→
   116→    Always appends to the back — new items never reorder existing queue.
   117→    """
   118→    existing = set(order)
   119→    for sid in sorted(inject_ids):
   120→        if sid not in existing:
   121→            order.append(sid)
   122→            injected.append(sid)
   123→
   124→
   125→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   126→# Unscored dimension sync (back of queue, unconditional)
   127→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   128→
   129→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   130→    plan: PlanModel,
   131→    state: StateModel,
   132→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   133→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.

   140→    2. **Inject** — append currently-unscored IDs to the *back* of
   141→       ``queue_order``.  Never reorders existing items.  Skipped mid-cycle —
   142→       unscored dimensions surface at cycle boundaries only.
   143→    """

   145→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   146→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   147→
   148→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   149→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   150→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
```

```
   152→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   153→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   154→
   155→    if not mid_cycle:
   156→        # Unscored dimensions have never been reviewed — a permanent skip on a
   157→        # placeholder is premature (the scoring pipeline needs actual scores).
   158→        # Clear any skip entries so they resurface for initial review.
   159→        skipped_dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   160→        if isinstance(skipped_dict, dict):
   161→            for sid in sorted(unscored_ids & skipped_ids):
   162→                skipped_dict.pop(sid, None)
   163→                result.resurfaced.append(sid)
   164→            skipped_ids -= unscored_ids
   165→
   166→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   167→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   168→
   169→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   170→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   171→    # Mid-cycle: prune ALL subjective IDs (keep_ids empty) since they
   172→    # shouldn't be in the queue mid-cycle at all.
   173→    if mid_cycle:
   174→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=set(), pruned=result.pruned)
   175→    else:
   176→        _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=unscored_ids | stale_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: append unscored IDs to back of queue ---------------------
   179→    # Mid-cycle: don't inject — they'll surface at cycle end.
   180→    if not mid_cycle:
   181→        _inject_subjective_ids(
   182→            order,
   183→            inject_ids=unscored_ids - skipped_ids,
   184→            injected=result.injected,
   185→        )
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   191→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   192→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   193→
   194→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   195→    plan: PlanModel,
   196→    state: StateModel,
   197→    *,
   198→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
   199→    cycle_just_completed: bool = False,
   200→) -> QueueSyncResult:
   201→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale and under-target subjective dimensions.
   202→
   203→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   204→       longer stale/under-target and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned
   205→       by ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   206→       When objective backlog exists (and this is not a just-completed cycle),
   207→       stale/under-target IDs are also evicted so they do not block objective work.
   208→    2. Append stale and under-target dimension IDs to the *back* when either:
   209→       a. No objective items remain (mid-cycle), OR
   210→       b. A cycle just completed.
   211→       Never reorders existing items.
   212→    """
   213→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   214→    result = QueueSyncResult()
   215→    stale_ids = stale_policy_mod.current_stale_ids(
   216→        state, subjective_prefix=SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
   217→    )
   218→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state)
   219→    skipped_ids = _skipped_subjective_ids(plan)
   220→    injectable_ids = (stale_ids | under_target_ids) - skipped_ids
   221→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   222→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   223→
   224→    # Keep queue/skipped invariants healthy even if an old plan contains overlap.
   225→    _prune_skipped_subjective_ids(order, skipped_ids=skipped_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   226→
   227→    objective_backlog = has_objective_backlog(state, policy)
   228→
   229→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   230→    # Mid-cycle: don't keep unscored IDs — sync_unscored_dimensions owns
   231→    # those and prunes them mid-cycle.  Only keep stale/under-target when
   232→    # objective backlog is clear or just-completed.
   233→    # Non-mid-cycle: keep unscored IDs always (they belong in the queue).
   234→    mid_cycle = is_mid_cycle(plan)
   235→    if mid_cycle:
   236→        keep_ids = injectable_ids if not objective_backlog else set()
   237→    else:
   238→        keep_ids = unscored_ids | injectable_ids
   239→        if objective_backlog and not cycle_just_completed:
   240→            keep_ids = unscored_ids
   241→    _prune_subjective_ids(order, keep_ids=keep_ids, pruned=result.pruned)
   242→
   243→    # --- Inject stale + under-target dimensions --------------------------
   244→    should_inject = not objective_backlog or cycle_just_completed
   245→
   246→    if should_inject and injectable_ids:
   247→        _inject_subjective_ids(order, inject_ids=injectable_ids, injected=result.injected)
   248→
   249→    return result
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "current_under_target_ids",
   254→    "current_unscored_ids",
   255→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→
```

```
   178→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {marker} {_step_display_text(step)}", "dim"))
   179→        remaining = len(action_steps) - show_count

   182→
   183→    dep_order = item.get("dependency_order")
   184→    if dep_order is not None and dep_order <= 2:

   198→        print(colorize("  If auto finds 0, drill into individual issues:", "dim"))
```
