```
1→"""Subjective scan reporting: common helpers, integrity checks, and output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.base.config import DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE, coerce_target_score
     8→from desloppify.engine._scoring.subjective.core import DISPLAY_NAMES
     9→from desloppify.intelligence import integrity as subjective_integrity_mod
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Common helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_common)
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class SubjectiveFollowup:
    18→    threshold: float
    19→    threshold_label: str
    20→    low_assessed: list[dict]
    21→    rendered: str
    22→    command: str
    23→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None
    24→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    25→
    26→
    27→def flatten_cli_keys(items: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    28→    """Flatten CLI keys across up to max_items subjective entries, preserving order."""
    29→    seen: set[str] = set()
    30→    ordered: list[str] = []
    31→    for item in items[:max_items]:
    32→        for key in item.get("cli_keys", []):
    33→            if key in seen:
    34→                continue
    35→            ordered.append(key)
    36→            seen.add(key)
    37→    return ",".join(ordered)
    38→
    39→
    40→def render_subjective_scores(entries: list[dict], *, max_items: int = 3) -> str:
    41→    return ", ".join(
    42→        f"{entry.get('name', 'Subjective')} {float(entry.get('strict', entry.get('score', 100.0))):.1f}%"
    43→        for entry in entries[:max_items]
    44→    )
    45→
    46→
    47→def render_subjective_names(entries: list[dict], *, max_names: int = 3) -> str:
    48→    count = len(entries)
    49→    names = ", ".join(
    50→        str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")) for entry in entries[:max_names]
    51→    )
    52→    if count > max_names:
    53→        names = f"{names}, +{count - max_names} more"
    54→    return names
    55→
    56→
    57→def coerce_notice_count(value: object) -> int:
    58→    if isinstance(value, bool):
    59→        return 0
    60→    if isinstance(value, int):
    61→        return value
    62→    if isinstance(value, float):
    63→        return int(value)
    64→    if isinstance(value, str):
    65→        try:
    66→            return int(value.strip())
    67→        except ValueError:
    68→            return 0
    69→    return 0
    70→
    71→
    72→def coerce_str_keys(value: object) -> list[str]:
    73→    if not isinstance(value, list | tuple | set):
    74→        return []
    75→    return [key for key in value if isinstance(key, str) and key]
    76→
    77→
    78→def subjective_rerun_command(
    79→    items: list[dict],
    80→    *,
    81→    max_items: int = 5,
    82→    refresh: bool = True,
    83→    has_prior_review: bool | None = None,
    84→) -> str:
    85→    _ = refresh
    86→    # If dimensions already have open review issues, route to the review queue
    87→    # instead of prompting a blocked rerun.
    88→    if any(
    89→        int(entry.get("failing", 0) or 0) > 0
    90→        for entry in items[:max_items]
    91→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
    92→    ):
    93→        return "`desloppify show review --status open`"
    94→
    95→    dim_keys = flatten_cli_keys(items, max_items=max_items)
    96→
    97→    # If no evidence of prior runner usage, suggest --prepare first
    98→    if has_prior_review is False:
    99→        command_parts = ["desloppify", "review", "--prepare"]
   100→        if dim_keys:
   101→            command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   102→        return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   103→
   104→    command_parts = [
   105→        "desloppify",
   106→        "review",
   107→        "--prepare",
   108→        "--force-review-rerun",
   109→    ]
   110→    if dim_keys:
   111→        command_parts.extend(["--dimensions", dim_keys])
   112→    return f"`{' '.join(command_parts)}`"
   113→
   114→
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→# Integrity and dimension-mapping helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_integrity)
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→
   119→
   120→def _subjective_display_name_from_key(dimension_key: str) -> str:
   121→    return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(
   122→        dimension_key, dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
   123→    )
   124→
   125→
   126→def subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   127→    dimension_keys: list[str], entries: list[dict]
   128→) -> list[dict]:
   129→    by_key: dict[str, dict] = {}
   130→    for entry in entries:
   131→        for key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   132→            by_key.setdefault(str(key), entry)
   133→
   134→    mapped: list[dict] = []
   135→    for key in dimension_keys:
   136→        if key in by_key:
   137→            mapped.append(by_key[key])
   138→            continue
   139→        mapped.append(
   140→            {
   141→                "name": _subjective_display_name_from_key(key),
   142→                "score": 0.0,
   143→                "strict": 0.0,
   144→                "failing": 0,
   145→                "placeholder": False,
   146→                "cli_keys": [key],
   147→            }
   148→        )
   149→    return mapped
   150→
   151→
   152→def _integrity_notice_for_explicit_status(
   153→    *,
   154→    status: str,
   155→    matched_keys: list[str],
   156→    reset_keys: list[str],
   157→    target_display: float,
   158→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   159→    max_items: int,
   160→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   161→    if status == "penalized" and reset_keys:
   162→        reset_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   163→            reset_keys,
   164→            subjective_entries,
   165→        )
   166→        return {
   167→            "status": "penalized",
   168→            "count": len(reset_keys),
   169→            "target": target_display,
   170→            "entries": reset_entries,
   171→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(reset_entries),
   172→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(reset_entries, max_items=max_items),
   173→        }
   174→    if status == "warn" and matched_keys:
   175→        matched_entries = subjective_entries_for_dimension_keys(
   176→            matched_keys,
   177→            subjective_entries,
   178→        )
   179→        return {
   180→            "status": "warn",
   181→            "count": len(matched_keys),
   182→            "target": target_display,
   183→            "entries": matched_entries,
   184→            "rendered": render_subjective_names(matched_entries),
   185→            "command": subjective_rerun_command(matched_entries, max_items=max_items),
   186→        }
   187→    return None
   188→
   189→
   190→def _at_target_entries(
   191→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   192→    *,
   193→    threshold_value: float,
   194→) -> list[dict]:
   195→    return sorted(
   196→        [
   197→            entry
   198→            for entry in subjective_entries
   199→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   200→            and subjective_integrity_mod.matches_target_score(
   201→                float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   202→                threshold_value,
   203→            )
   204→        ],
   205→        key=lambda entry: str(entry.get("name", "")).lower(),
   206→    )
   207→
   208→
   209→def subjective_integrity_followup(
   210→    state: dict,
   211→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   212→    *,
   213→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   214→    max_items: int = 5,
   215→) -> dict[str, object] | None:
   216→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold)
   217→    raw_integrity_state = state.get("subjective_integrity")
   218→    integrity_state: dict[str, object] = (
   219→        raw_integrity_state if isinstance(raw_integrity_state, dict) else {}
   220→    )
   221→    status = str(integrity_state.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   222→    raw_target = integrity_state.get("target_score")
   223→    target_display = coerce_target_score(raw_target, fallback=threshold_value)
   224→    matched_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("matched_dimensions", []))
   225→    reset_keys = coerce_str_keys(integrity_state.get("reset_dimensions", []))
   226→
   227→    explicit_notice = _integrity_notice_for_explicit_status(
   228→        status=status,
   229→        matched_keys=matched_keys,
   230→        reset_keys=reset_keys,
   231→        target_display=target_display,
   232→        subjective_entries=subjective_entries,
   233→        max_items=max_items,
   234→    )
   235→    if explicit_notice is not None:
   236→        return explicit_notice
   237→
   238→    at_target = _at_target_entries(
   239→        subjective_entries,
   240→        threshold_value=threshold_value,
   241→    )
   242→    if not at_target:
   243→        return None
   244→
   245→    return {
   246→        "status": "at_target",
   247→        "count": len(at_target),
   248→        "target": threshold_value,
   249→        "entries": at_target,
   250→        "rendered": render_subjective_names(at_target),
   251→        "command": subjective_rerun_command(at_target, max_items=max_items),
   252→    }
   253→
   254→
   255→def subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   256→    integrity_notice: dict[str, object] | None,
   257→    *,
   258→    fallback_target: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   259→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   260→    if not integrity_notice:
   261→        return []
   262→
   263→    status = str(integrity_notice.get("status", "")).strip().lower()
   264→    count = coerce_notice_count(integrity_notice.get("count", 0))
   265→    target_display = coerce_target_score(
   266→        integrity_notice.get("target"),
   267→        fallback=fallback_target,
   268→    )
   269→    rendered = str(integrity_notice.get("rendered", "subjective dimensions"))
   270→    command = str(integrity_notice.get("command", ""))
   271→
   272→    if status == "penalized":
   273→        return [
   274→            (
   275→                "red",
   276→                "WARNING: "
   277→                f"{count} subjective dimensions matched target {target_display:.1f} "
   278→                f"and were reset to 0.0 this scan: {rendered}.",
   279→            ),
   280→            (
   281→                "yellow",
   282→                "Anti-gaming safeguard applied. Re-review objectively and import fresh assessments.",
   283→            ),
   284→            ("dim", f"Rerun now: {command}"),
   285→        ]
   286→
   287→    if status == "warn":
   288→        dimension_label = "dimension is" if count == 1 else "dimensions are"
   289→        return [
   290→            (
   291→                "yellow",
   292→                "WARNING: "
   293→                f"{count} subjective {dimension_label} parked on target {target_display:.1f}. "
   294→                "Re-run that review with evidence-first scoring before treating this score as final.",
   295→            ),
   296→            ("dim", f"Next step: {command}"),
   297→        ]
   298→
   299→    if status == "at_target":
   300→        return [
   301→            (
   302→                "yellow",
   303→                "WARNING: "
   304→                f"{count} of your subjective scores matches the target score, indicating a high risk of gaming. "
   305→                f"Can you rerun them by running {command} taking extra care to be objective.",
   306→            ),
   307→        ]
   308→
   309→    return []
   310→
   311→
   312→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   313→# Output-oriented helpers (formerly scan_reporting_subjective_output)
   314→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   315→
   316→
   317→def build_subjective_followup(
   318→    state: dict,
   319→    subjective_entries: list[dict],
   320→    *,
   321→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   322→    max_quality_items: int = 3,
   323→    max_integrity_items: int = 5,
   324→) -> SubjectiveFollowup:
   325→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold)
   326→    threshold_label = f"{threshold_value:.1f}".rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
   327→    low_assessed = sorted(
   328→        [
   329→            entry
   330→            for entry in subjective_entries
   331→            if not entry.get("placeholder")
   332→            and float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))) < threshold_value
   333→        ],
   334→        key=lambda entry: float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0))),
   335→    )
   336→    rendered = render_subjective_scores(low_assessed, max_items=max_quality_items)
   337→    command = subjective_rerun_command(
   338→        low_assessed,
   339→        max_items=max_quality_items,
   340→    )
   341→    integrity_notice = subjective_integrity_followup(
   342→        state,
   343→        subjective_entries,
   344→        threshold=threshold_value,
   345→        max_items=max_integrity_items,
   346→    )
   347→    integrity_lines = subjective_integrity_notice_lines(
   348→        integrity_notice,
   349→        fallback_target=threshold_value,
   350→    )
   351→    return SubjectiveFollowup(
   352→        threshold=threshold_value,
   353→        threshold_label=threshold_label,
   354→        low_assessed=low_assessed,
   355→        rendered=rendered,
   356→        command=command,
   357→        integrity_notice=integrity_notice,
   358→        integrity_lines=integrity_lines,
   359→    )
   360→
   361→
   362→def show_subjective_paths(
   363→    state: dict,
   364→    dim_scores: dict,
   365→    *,
   366→    colorize_fn,
   367→    scorecard_subjective_entries_fn,
   368→    threshold: float = DEFAULT_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE,
   369→) -> None:
   370→    threshold_value = coerce_target_score(threshold)
   371→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries_fn(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   372→    if not subjective_entries:
   373→        return
   374→
   375→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   376→        state,
   377→        subjective_entries,
   378→        threshold=threshold_value,
   379→        max_quality_items=3,
   380→        max_integrity_items=5,
   381→    )
   382→    unassessed = sorted(
   383→        [entry for entry in subjective_entries if entry["placeholder"]],
   384→        key=lambda item: item["name"].lower(),
   385→    )
   386→    low_assessed = followup.low_assessed
   387→
   388→    all_issues = state.get("issues", {})
   389→    if not isinstance(all_issues, dict):
   390→        all_issues = {}
   391→    coverage_global, _reason_counts, _holistic_reasons = (
   392→        subjective_integrity_mod.subjective_review_open_breakdown(all_issues)
   393→    )
   394→
   395→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   396→    if (
   397→        not unassessed
   398→        and not low_assessed
   399→        and stale_count == 0
   400→        and coverage_global <= 0
   401→        and not followup.integrity_notice
   402→    ):
   403→        return
   404→
   405→    # Integrity lines are always preserved (anti-gaming safeguard).
   406→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   407→        print(colorize_fn(f"    {message}", style))
   408→
   409→    # Collapsed summary replacing verbose Subjective path section.
   410→    parts: list[str] = []
   411→    if low_assessed:
   412→        parts.append(f"{len(low_assessed)} below target ({followup.threshold_label}%)")
   413→    if unassessed:
   414→        parts.append(f"{len(unassessed)} unassessed")
   415→    if stale_count:
   416→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   417→    if coverage_global > 0:
   418→        parts.append(f"{coverage_global} files need review")
   419→
   420→    if parts:
   421→        print(colorize_fn(f"  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   422→        print(colorize_fn("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   423→
   424→    print()
   425→
   426→
   427→__all__ = [
   428→    "SubjectiveFollowup",
   429→    "build_subjective_followup",
   430→    "coerce_notice_count",
   431→    "coerce_str_keys",
   432→    "flatten_cli_keys",
   433→    "render_subjective_names",

   437→    "subjective_integrity_followup",
   438→    "subjective_integrity_notice_lines",
   439→    "subjective_rerun_command",
   440→]
   441→
```

```
    71→        not_scanned = bool(
    72→            not is_subjective and not detectors and checks == 0
    73→        )
    74→        carried_forward = bool(
    75→            not is_subjective and data.get("carried_forward")
    76→        )
    77→        dim_key = str(subjective_meta.get("dimension_key", "")) if subjective_meta else ""
    78→        stale = bool(subjective_meta.get("stale")) if subjective_meta else False
    79→        cli_keys = (
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.base.output.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "kind": member.get("kind", "issue"),
    19→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    20→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    21→        "file": member.get("file"),
    22→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    23→        "status": member.get("status"),
    24→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    25→    }
    26→
    27→
    28→def _serialize_cluster_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    29→    """Serialize cluster queue meta-items with sampled members."""
    30→    members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    31→    serialized_members = [
    32→        _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    33→        for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    34→    ]
    35→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    36→    serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    37→        "id": item.get("id"),
    38→        "kind": "cluster",
    39→        "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    40→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    41→        "member_count": member_count,
    42→        "members": serialized_members,
    43→        "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    44→        "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    45→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    46→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    47→    }
    48→    if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→        serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→        serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→
    52→    autofix_hint = item.get("autofix_hint")
    53→    if autofix_hint:
    54→        serialized_cluster["autofix_hint"] = autofix_hint
    55→    action_steps = item.get("action_steps") or []
    56→    if action_steps:
    57→        serialized_cluster["action_steps"] = action_steps
    58→    return serialized_cluster
    59→
    60→
    61→def _serialize_issue_item_base(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    62→    """Serialize core issue fields shared across output modes."""
    63→    return {
    64→        "id": item.get("id"),
    65→        "kind": item.get("kind", "issue"),
    66→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    67→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    68→        "file": item.get("file"),
    69→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    70→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    71→        "status": item.get("status"),
    72→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    73→    }
    74→
    75→
    76→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    77→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    78→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    79→        return _serialize_cluster_item(item)
    80→
    81→    serialized = _serialize_issue_item_base(item)
    82→
    83→    # Workflow state
    84→    blocked_by = item.get("blocked_by")
    85→    if blocked_by:
    86→        serialized["blocked_by"] = blocked_by
    87→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    88→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    89→    explain = item.get("explain")
    90→    if explain is not None:
    91→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    92→
    93→    # Plan metadata
    94→    for key in ("queue_position", "plan_description", "plan_note", "plan_cluster"):
    95→        value = item.get(key)
    96→        if value:
    97→            serialized[key] = value
    98→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    99→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
   100→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
   101→        skip_reason = item.get("plan_skip_reason")
   102→        if skip_reason:
   103→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   104→
   105→    return serialized
   106→
   107→
   108→def build_query_payload(
   109→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
   110→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   111→    *,
   112→    command: str,
   113→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   114→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, Any]:
   116→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
   117→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
   118→    mode = "execution" if command == "next" else command
   119→    queue_section: dict[str, Any] = {
   120→        "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
   121→        "mode": mode,
   122→    }
   123→
   124→    agent_notes = [
   125→        "Do NOT use `desloppify plan skip` unless the user explicitly asks you to skip an item.",
   126→        "If you cannot fix an item, report it and move to the next one.",
   127→        "For cluster items: if the autofix_hint finds 0 results, drill into individual issues with the primary_command.",
   128→    ]
   129→    if command == "backlog":
   130→        agent_notes = [
   131→            "This is backlog discovery, not the active execution queue.",
   132→            "Use `desloppify next` for the current execution item.",
   133→            "Do NOT treat backlog rank as permission to bypass the living plan.",
   134→        ]
   135→
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
   137→        "command": command,
   138→        "items": serialized,
   139→        "queue": queue_section,
   140→        "narrative": narrative,
   141→        "agent_notes": agent_notes,
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    if plan and (
   145→        plan.get("queue_order")
   146→        or plan.get("skipped")
   147→        or plan.get("clusters")
   148→    ):
   149→        clusters_summary = []
   150→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
   151→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("issue_ids", []))
   152→            clusters_summary.append({
   153→                "name": name,
   154→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   155→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   156→            })
   157→        payload["plan"] = {
   158→            "active": True,
   159→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   160→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   161→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   162→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   163→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   164→        }
   165→
   166→    return payload
   167→
   168→
   169→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   170→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   171→    return render_markdown_for_command(items, command="next")
   172→
   173→
   174→def render_markdown_for_command(
   175→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   176→    *,
   177→    command: str,
   178→) -> str:
   179→    """Render queue items as markdown for the given queue surface."""
   180→    heading = "# Desloppify Execution Queue"
   181→    if command == "backlog":
   182→        heading = "# Desloppify Backlog"
   183→    lines = [
   184→        heading,
   185→        "",
   186→        "| Kind | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   187→        "|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   188→    ]
   189→    for item in items:
   190→        kind = item.get("kind", "issue")
   191→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   192→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   193→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   194→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   195→    lines.append("")
   196→    return "\n".join(lines)
   197→
   198→
   199→def write_output_file(
   200→    output_file: str,
```

```
    22→def detail_dict(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    23→    """Return issue detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    return detector in {"subjective_assessment", "holistic_review"}
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_review_issue(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:

    40→
    41→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    42→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    43→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    44→        return True
    45→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    46→        members = item.get("members", [])
    47→        return bool(members) and all(
    48→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    49→        )
    50→    return False
    51→
    52→
    53→def review_issue_weight(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    54→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    55→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    56→    weight_by_confidence = {
    57→        "high": 1.0,
    58→        "medium": 0.7,
    59→        "low": 0.3,
    60→    }
    61→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    62→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    63→        weight *= 10.0
    64→    return float(weight)
    65→
    66→
    67→def scope_matches(item: WorkQueueItem | dict[str, Any], scope: str | None) -> bool:
    68→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    69→    if not scope:
    70→        return True
    71→
    72→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    73→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    74→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    75→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    76→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    77→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    78→
    79→    if "*" in scope:
    80→        return any(
    81→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    82→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    83→        )
    84→
    85→    if "::" in scope:
    86→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    87→
    88→    lowered = scope.lower()

    90→        return (
    91→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    92→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    93→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    94→        )
    95→
    96→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a issue ID.
    97→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    98→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    99→
   100→    return (

   102→        or filepath == scope
   103→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   104→    )
```

```
    77→def _promote_subjective_ids(order: list[str], ids: list[str]) -> int:
    78→    """Move subjective IDs ahead of objective backlog while preserving order."""
    79→    target_ids: list[str] = []
    80→    seen: set[str] = set()
    81→    for fid in ids:
    82→        sid = str(fid).strip()
    83→        if not sid or sid in seen:
    84→            continue
    85→        target_ids.append(sid)
    86→        seen.add(sid)
    87→    if not target_ids:
    88→        return 0
    89→
    90→    insert_at = 0
    91→    while insert_at < len(order):
    92→        current = str(order[insert_at])
    93→        if not (
    94→            current.startswith("workflow::") or current.startswith("triage::")
    95→        ):
    96→            break
    97→        insert_at += 1
    98→
    99→    changes = 0
   100→    for sid in target_ids:
   101→        existing_idx = order.index(sid) if sid in order else None
   102→        target_idx = min(insert_at, len(order))
   103→        if existing_idx is not None:
   104→            if existing_idx == target_idx:
   105→                insert_at += 1
   106→                continue
   107→            order.pop(existing_idx)
   108→            if existing_idx < target_idx:
   109→                target_idx -= 1
   110→        order.insert(target_idx, sid)
   111→        insert_at = target_idx + 1
   112→        changes += 1
   113→    return changes
```

> AGENT

Perfect. Now I have enough information to map the complete lifecycle. Let me create a comprehensive summary:

## Full Subjective Lifecycle: Scan → Review → Score → Triage → Work

### 1. **Scan: Initial Detection of Subjective Dimensions**

**Where:** `desloppify/app/commands/scan/plan_reconcile.py` and `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py`

At scan time, subjective dimensions are discovered and classified into three categories:

- **Unscored (placeholder):** Dimensions detected but never reviewed. Stored in `subjective_assessments[<dim_key>]` with `placeholder: True`, or in `dimension_scores[<dim_name>].detectors.subjective_assessment` with `placeholder: True`.
- **Stale:** Previously scored dimensions where evidence/scoring details may be outdated. Marked in dimension score metadata.
- **Under-target:** Scored dimensions below the target threshold (default 80%) that aren't stale or unscored.

Post-scan reconciliation in `plan_reconcile.py` calls `sync_unscored_dimensions()` and `sync_stale_dimensions()` to keep the plan queue in sync (lines 95-132).

---

### 2. **Next: What Command Does `next` Show for a Subjective Item?**

**Primary Location:** `desloppify/app/commands/next/render.py` lines 42-74 (`_render_subjective_dimension`)

When `next` surfaces a subjective item:

```python
print(f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}")
```

The `primary_command` is generated by `auto_cluster_sync.py` lines 164, 182, 205-208:

- **For unscored dimensions** (cluster `auto/initial-review`):  
  `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <cli_keys>`  
  Example: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions correctness,consistency`

- **For stale dimensions** (cluster `auto/stale-review`):  
  `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <cli_keys>`  
  (Same command but different cluster label)

- **For under-target dimensions** (cluster `auto/under-target-review`):  
  `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <cli_keys>`  
  (Also same command, optional-tier cluster)

The actual dimension CLI keys are extracted from the `dimension_key` metadata and slugified. When a subjective item appears in `next` output, it also includes a "user message" (lines 60-67):

> "Hey — this is a subjective review item. Run `desloppify review --run-batches --dry-run` to generate prompt files... Then import with `--import-run <run-dir> --scan-after-import`."

---

### 3. **Review Import: How Scores Update and Plan Responds**

**Main Flow:** `desloppify/app/commands/review/importing/cmd.py::do_import()` and `plan_sync.py::sync_plan_after_import()`

The review import lifecycle:

1. **Load and validate** the import payload (lines 284-308 in `cmd.py`)
2. **Apply assessment policy** (lines 96-114):
   - `manual_override` mode → mark assessments as provisional (will reset on next scan)
   - `trusted_internal` / `attested_external` → clear provisional flags (durable)
3. **Import holistic issues** via `import_holistic_issues()` which updates state with review findings and assessments
4. **Persist state** (lines 330-339) and synchronize the plan

**In `plan_sync.py::sync_plan_after_import()`** (lines 68-276):

After state is persisted, the plan is synced:

- **Compute subjective visibility policy** (line 90): `compute_subjective_visibility()` snapshots current unscored/stale/under_target IDs based on fresh state
- **Sync `communicate-score`** (lines 105-114): If trusted assessment import, inject workflow to signal score change
- **Sync review issues** (lines 161-168): If review findings changed, call `sync_plan_after_review_import()` to append new review IDs and prune stale ones
- **Sync stale dimensions** (lines 174-180): Refresh stale/under-target set after import, inject/evict from queue based on objective backlog
- **Auto-cluster regeneration** (lines 182-189): Rebuild subjective clusters (initial/stale/under-target) with updated dimension state

**Key insight:** **Scoring happens implicitly during import** — the `dimension_scores` in state are updated by the holistic importer when subjective assessments are received. When `sync_plan_after_import()` recomputes visibility policy, it reads the fresh scores from state.

---

### 4. **Score Update and Plan Reconciliation**

**Location:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/sync/dimensions.py` lines 194-249 (`sync_stale_dimensions`)

The `SubjectiveVisibility` policy (from `engine/_plan/policy/subjective.py`) encapsulates the logic:

```python
def should_inject_to_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    if fid in self.unscored_ids:
        return True  # unconditional
    if fid in self.stale_ids:
        return not self.has_objective_backlog  # inject when objective work is done
    if fid in self.under_target_ids:
        return not self.has_objective_backlog  # same as stale
    return False

def should_evict_from_plan(self, fid: str) -> bool:
    if fid in self.unscored_ids:
        return False  # never evict unscored
    if fid in self.stale_ids or fid in self.under_target_ids:
        return self.has_objective_backlog  # evict when objective work returns
    return False
```

**How scoring changes the queue:**

1. A subjective dimension under-target gets **reviewed and scored higher** → no longer in `under_target_ids`
2. Next sync (via import or scan), `sync_stale_dimensions()` prunes it from queue (line 241)
3. If dimension goes from under-target **to at-target or above**, it's removed

Conversely:

1. A dimension goes **from at-target to below target** (e.g., new evidence found during re-review)
2. Next sync, it re-appears in `current_under_target_ids()` → injected back into queue (line 247)

**The crucial constraint:** Stale and under-target dimensions are **only injected when `has_objective_backlog` is False** (line 244). When objective mechanical work exists, stale/under-target dimensions are evicted to keep them from blocking work (lines 239-240).

---

### 5. **Triage and the Plan Queue Lifecycle**

**Location:** `desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile_review_import.py` lines 259-405 and `_plan/sync/dimensions.py`

After scoring, triage is managed separately from subjective dimension lifecycle:

- **Triage sync** happens in `sync_triage_needed()` and is deferred if objective backlog exists (plan_reconcile.py lines 311-338)
- **Subjective dimensions** are **orthogonal** to triage — they don't feed into triage decisions. Triage is about review **issues** (things found by the review detector), not subjective **scores**.

Mid-cycle protection (plan_reconcile.py lines 433-448):

- When `plan_start_scores` is set, a queue cycle is active
- If mid-cycle, `sync_stale_dimensions()` is called but with the constraint that unscored/stale/under-target are **not regenerated as clusters** (auto-clustering is skipped, lines 478-494)
- This preserves queue state during active work

---

### 6. **Stuck Dimensions: The "Always Visible But Never Actionable" Trap**

**Can a subjective dimension get stuck?** 

**For unscored dimensions:** NO — they surface unconditionally at cycle boundaries and remain in queue until reviewed. Even if skipped, `sync_unscored_dimensions()` line 160-164 clears the skip entry when not mid-cycle, forcing them to resurface.

**For stale/under-target dimensions:** POTENTIALLY YES, in this scenario:

1. A dimension is stale/under-target and the queue is never drained (objective backlog always exists)
2. Stale/under-target IDs are **never injected** because `has_objective_backlog` is always True (line 244)
3. The dimension remains in queue from a **prior cycle** but is not updated
4. User runs `next` but sees the dimension is **not actionable** until objective work completes

**Mitigation:** The visibility policy is **recomputed on every scan and import** (plan_reconcile.py lines 250-254). So as soon as objective work clears, the next scan/import will re-inject stale/under-target dimensions automatically.

---

### Summary Table: Subjective Lifecycle Transitions

| State | Trigger | Action | Result |
|-------|---------|--------|--------|
| **Unscored (placeholder)** | `scan` | Sync at cycle boundary | Inject to queue unconditionally |
| **Unscored (placeholder)** | Skipped + non-mid-cycle | Resurfacing logic | Clear skip, resurface |
| **Stale** | Scored but evidence outdated | Recompute in next sync | In-queue if no objective backlog |
| **Under-target** | Score < 80% (strict) | Recompute in next sync | In-queue if no objective backlog |
| **At-target or above** | Score ≥ 80% | Prune in next sync | Remove from queue |
| **Any dimension** | Objective backlog exists | Evict policy | Temporarily hidden until backlog clears |
| **Objective backlog clears** | `scan` / `import` | Recompute visibility | Re-inject stale/under-target |
